    key_with(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Down, mods(true, false));
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(0, 2), "end of the A1:A3 block");
    key_with(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Down, mods(true, false));
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(0, 9_999), "then the bottom of the grid");
    key_with(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Up, mods(true, false));
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(0, 2));
}

#[test]
fn home_end_and_page_keys() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    for _ in 0..4 {
        press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Down);
    }
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Right);
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Home);
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(0, 4));
    key_with(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Home, mods(true, false));
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(0, 0));
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::End);
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(1, 0), "last used column of the row");
    key_with(&mut app, &mut host, Key::End, mods(true, false));
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(1, 2), "last used cell");

    key_with(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Home, mods(true, false));
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::PageDown);
    let page = app.view.rows_per_page(&app.workbook.sheets[0], 600.0);
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor.row, page);
    assert_eq!(app.view.scroll_row, page);
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::PageUp);
    assert_eq!((app.session.cursor.row, app.view.scroll_row), (0, 0));
}

#[test]
fn tab_and_shift_tab_move_sideways() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Tab);
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Tab);
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(2, 0));
    key_with(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Tab, mods(false, true));
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(1, 0));
}

#[test]
fn the_scroll_wheel_pans_the_view() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    app.handle_event(&mut host, Event::Scroll { cols: 1, rows: 3 });
    assert_eq!((app.view.scroll_col, app.view.scroll_row), (1, 3));
    app.handle_event(&mut host, Event::Scroll { cols: -5, rows: -9 });
    assert_eq!((app.view.scroll_col, app.view.scroll_row), (0, 0));
    assert!(app.take_dirty());
}

// ---- collapse, expand, fit, set size ---------------------------------------

#[test]
fn collapse_squeezes_and_expand_restores_the_old_size() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    app.workbook.sheets[0].set_col_width(0, 150.0);
    app.workbook.sheets[0].set_row_height(0, 60.0);

    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, '0');
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].col_width(0), 10.0);
    key_with(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Char('0'), mods(true, true));
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].col_width(0), 150.0);

    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, '9');
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].row_height(0), 8.0);
    key_with(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Char('9'), mods(true, true));
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].row_height(0), 60.0);
}

#[test]
fn collapse_and_expand_cover_the_whole_selection_and_undo() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    app.session.selection_anchor = Some(c(2, 0));
    app.session.cursor = c(0, 0);
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, '0');
    for col in 0..=2 {
        assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].col_width(col), 10.0);
    }
    assert!(app.status().contains("Collapsed 3"), "{}", app.status());
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, '0');
    assert!(app.status().contains("Already collapsed"), "{}", app.status());

    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'z');
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].col_width(1), 100.0, "undo restores every column");
}

#[test]
fn expanding_without_a_remembered_size_uses_the_default() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    app.workbook.sheets[0].set_col_width(0, 10.0); // made tiny some other way
    key_with(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Char('0'), mods(true, true));
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].col_width(0), 100.0);
    key_with(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Char('0'), mods(true, true));
    assert!(app.status().contains("Not collapsed"), "{}", app.status());
}

#[test]
fn fit_sizes_the_column_to_its_widest_value() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Right); // column B holds "Projection Summary"
    let scale = app.view.font_scale;
    let expected = ops::fit_col_width(&app.workbook.sheets[0], 1, &|s: &str| app.font.measure_string(s, scale).0);
    assert_ne!(expected, 100.0, "the test needs a column that actually changes");
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/CF");
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].col_width(1), expected);
}

#[test]
fn the_width_prompt_sets_the_width() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/CW");
    assert_eq!(app.prompt, Some(Prompt::ColumnWidth));
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_prompt.as_deref(), Some("Column width: "));
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_buffer.as_deref(), Some("100"), "shows the current width");

    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "150"); // replaces the selected suggestion
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Enter);
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].col_width(0), 150.0);
    assert_eq!((app.prompt, app.session.edit_buffer.clone()), (None, None));
}

#[test]
fn a_bad_size_is_reported_and_changes_nothing() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/CW");
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "abc");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Enter);
    assert!(app.status().contains("not a number"), "{}", app.status());
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].col_width(0), 100.0);

    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/RH");
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "40");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Enter);
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].row_height(0), 40.0);
}

#[test]
fn escape_cancels_a_prompt() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/CW");
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "999");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Escape);
    assert_eq!((app.prompt, app.session.edit_prompt.clone(), app.session.edit_buffer.clone()), (None, None, None));
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].col_width(0), 100.0);
}

// ---- find and go to ----------------------------------------------------------

#[test]
fn find_asks_then_jumps_and_f3_repeats() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'f');
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_prompt.as_deref(), Some("Find: "));
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "active");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Enter);
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(1, 1));
    assert!(app.status().contains("Found"), "{}", app.status());

    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::F(3));
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(1, 1), "the only match wraps to itself");

    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'f');
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_buffer.as_deref(), Some("active"), "the last search is offered");
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "zzz");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Enter);
    assert!(app.status().contains("not found"), "{}", app.status());
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(1, 1), "a miss leaves the cursor alone");
}

#[test]
fn f3_with_no_earlier_search_asks_what_to_find() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::F(3));
    assert_eq!(app.prompt, Some(Prompt::Find));
}

#[test]
fn go_to_takes_an_address_or_a_name() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'g');
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "c30");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Enter);
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(2, 29));
    let r = app.view.cell_rect(&app.workbook.sheets[0], c(2, 29));
    assert!(r.max_y <= 600.0, "scrolled into view");

    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::F(5));
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "REVENUE");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Enter);
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(0, 0));

    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::F(5));
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "nowhere");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Enter);
    assert!(app.status().contains("No cell or name"), "{}", app.status());
}

#[test]
fn clicking_the_grid_cancels_a_prompt() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'f');
    click(&mut app, &mut host, 1, 1);
    assert_eq!((app.prompt, app.session.edit_buffer.clone()), (None, None));
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(1, 1));
}

// ---- selection -----------------------------------------------------------------

#[test]
fn dragging_selects_a_range_and_copies_it_as_tab_separated_text() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    drag(&mut app, &mut host, (0, 0), (1, 2));
    assert_eq!(app.session.selection(), Some(Range::new(c(0, 0), c(1, 2))));
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(1, 2));

    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'c');
    assert_eq!(
        host.clipboard.as_deref(),
        Some("8000\tProjection Summary\n0.2\tActive\n=revenue * tax_rate\t")
    );
    assert_eq!(app.status(), "Copied A1:B3");
}

#[test]
fn a_plain_click_selects_one_cell_only() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    click(&mut app, &mut host, 1, 1);
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(1, 1));
    assert_eq!((app.session.selection(), app.session.selection_anchor), (None, None));
}

#[test]
fn a_drag_ends_when_the_button_was_released_outside_the_window() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    let (ax, ay) = centre(&mut app, 0, 0);
    app.handle_event(&mut host, Event::PointerDown { x: ax, y: ay, button: PointerButton::Left });
    assert!(app.dragging);
    app.handle_event(&mut host, Event::PointerMove { x: ax + 200.0, y: ay + 50.0, left_down: false });
    assert!(!app.dragging);
    assert_eq!(app.session.selection_anchor, None);
}

#[test]
fn right_click_selects_what_you_clicked_unless_it_is_inside_the_selection() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    drag(&mut app, &mut host, (0, 0), (1, 1));

    let (x, y) = centre(&mut app, 1, 1);
    app.handle_event(&mut host, Event::PointerDown { x, y, button: PointerButton::Right });
    assert_eq!(app.session.selection(), Some(Range::new(c(0, 0), c(1, 1))), "inside: selection kept");
    assert!(app.context_menu.is_visible);

    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Escape);
    let (x, y) = centre(&mut app, 3, 4);
    app.handle_event(&mut host, Event::PointerDown { x, y, button: PointerButton::Right });
    assert_eq!((app.session.cursor, app.session.selection()), (c(3, 4), None), "outside: moves there");
}

#[test]
fn f8_extends_the_selection_with_the_arrows_and_escape_ends_it() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::F(8));
    assert!(app.session.extend_mode);
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Down);
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Down);
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Right);
    assert_eq!(app.session.selection(), Some(Range::new(c(0, 0), c(1, 2))));
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Escape);
    assert_eq!((app.session.selection(), app.session.extend_mode), (None, false));
}

#[test]
fn plain_arrows_drop_the_selection() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    drag(&mut app, &mut host, (0, 0), (1, 1));
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Right);
    assert_eq!(app.session.selection(), None);
}

#[test]
fn ctrl_a_selects_the_used_range() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'a');
    assert_eq!(app.session.selection(), Some(Range::new(c(0, 0), c(1, 2))));
    assert_eq!(app.status(), "Selected A1:B3");
}

// ---- ranges: clipboard, clear, fill, sort ------------------------------------

#[test]
fn copying_and_pasting_a_range_moves_relative_references() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = blank_app();
    put(&mut app, 0, 0, "4");
    put(&mut app, 1, 0, "=A1*3");
    app.session.selection_anchor = Some(c(1, 0));
    app.session.cursor = c(0, 0);
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'c');
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Escape);

    for _ in 0..4 {
        press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Down);
    }
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'v');
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&app, 1, 4), "=A5*3");
    assert_eq!(result_at(&app, 1, 4), Some(12.0));
    assert_eq!(app.session.selection(), Some(Range::new(c(0, 4), c(1, 4))), "the pasted block is selected");
}

#[test]
fn text_from_outside_pastes_exactly_as_written() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = blank_app();
    host.clipboard = Some("=A1*2\t5\n".into());
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Down);
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'v');
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&app, 0, 1), "=A1*2");
    assert_eq!(result_at(&app, 1, 1), Some(5.0));
}

#[test]
fn delete_clears_the_whole_selection() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    drag(&mut app, &mut host, (0, 0), (1, 1));
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Delete);
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].cells.len(), 1, "only A3 is left");
    assert_eq!(app.status(), "Cleared A1:B2");
}

#[test]
fn cutting_a_range_fills_the_clipboard_and_clears_it() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    drag(&mut app, &mut host, (0, 0), (0, 1));
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'x');
    assert_eq!(host.clipboard.as_deref(), Some("8000\n0.2"));
    assert!(value_at(&app, 0, 0).is_none() && value_at(&app, 0, 1).is_none());
}

#[test]
fn ctrl_d_and_ctrl_r_fill() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = blank_app();
    put(&mut app, 0, 0, "1");
    put(&mut app, 1, 0, "=A1+1");
    app.session.selection_anchor = Some(c(1, 2));
    app.session.cursor = c(0, 0);
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'd');
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&app, 1, 2), "=A3+1");
    assert_eq!(result_at(&app, 1, 2), Some(2.0));

    app.session.clear_selection();
    app.session.selection_anchor = Some(c(3, 0));
    app.session.cursor = c(0, 0);
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'r');
    assert_eq!(result_at(&app, 3, 0), Some(1.0));
}

#[test]
fn fill_from_the_menu_reports_when_there_is_nothing_to_copy_from() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/DFD"); // Data > Fill > Down on A1 (nothing above)
    assert!(app.status().contains("Nothing above"), "{}", app.status());
    assert!(app.undo.is_empty(), "a failed edit is not an undo step");
}

#[test]
fn sort_orders_the_selected_rows_by_the_cursor_column() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = blank_app();
    for (row, n) in [(0, "3"), (1, "1"), (2, "2")] {
        put(&mut app, 0, row, n);
        put(&mut app, 1, row, &format!("=A{}*10", row + 1));
    }
    app.session.selection_anchor = Some(c(1, 2));
    app.session.cursor = c(0, 0); // column A is the key

    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/DSA");
    let a: Vec<_> = (0..3).map(|r| result_at(&app, 0, r).unwrap()).collect();
    assert_eq!(a, vec![1.0, 2.0, 3.0]);
    assert_eq!(result_at(&app, 1, 0), Some(10.0), "same-row formulas follow their row");
    assert!(app.status().contains("ascending"), "{}", app.status());

    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/DSD");
    assert_eq!(result_at(&app, 0, 0), Some(3.0));
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'z');
    assert_eq!(result_at(&app, 0, 0), Some(1.0), "sort is one undo step");
}

#[test]
fn sort_without_a_selection_says_what_to_do() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/DSA");
    assert!(app.status().contains("Select the rows"), "{}", app.status());
}

// ---- insert and delete -------------------------------------------------------

#[test]
fn inserting_and_deleting_rows_repairs_formulas_and_undoes() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = blank_app();
    put(&mut app, 0, 0, "1");
    put(&mut app, 0, 1, "2");
    put(&mut app, 0, 2, "=A1+A2");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Down); // cursor on A2

    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/RI");
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&app, 0, 3), "=A1+A3");
    assert_eq!(result_at(&app, 0, 3), Some(3.0));
    assert!(app.status().starts_with("Inserted 1 row"), "{}", app.status());

    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'z');
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&app, 0, 2), "=A1+A2");

    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/RD"); // delete A2
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&app, 0, 1), "=A1+#REF!");
    assert!(app.status().starts_with("Deleted 1 row"), "{}", app.status());
}

#[test]
fn inserting_a_column_shifts_the_references_across() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = blank_app();
    put(&mut app, 0, 0, "1");
    put(&mut app, 1, 0, "2");
    put(&mut app, 2, 0, "=A1+B1");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Right); // cursor on B1
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/CI");
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&app, 3, 0), "=A1+C1");
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/CD");
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&app, 2, 0), "=A1+B1");
}

#[test]
fn a_selection_of_several_rows_inserts_that_many() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = blank_app();
    put(&mut app, 0, 0, "1");
    put(&mut app, 0, 1, "2");
    app.session.selection_anchor = Some(c(0, 1));
    app.session.cursor = c(0, 0);
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/RI");
    assert!(app.status().starts_with("Inserted 2 row"), "{}", app.status());
    assert_eq!(result_at(&app, 0, 2), Some(1.0));
}

#[test]
fn an_insert_that_cannot_fit_is_refused_and_leaves_no_undo_step() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = blank_app();
    put(&mut app, 0, 9_999, "1");
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/RI");
    assert!(app.status().contains("pushed past"), "{}", app.status());
    assert_eq!(result_at(&app, 0, 9_999), Some(1.0));
    assert!(app.undo.is_empty());
}

// ---- undo / redo ---------------------------------------------------------------

#[test]
fn undo_and_redo_walk_through_edits() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "42");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Enter);
    assert!(is_number(value_at(&app, 0, 0), 42.0));

    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'z');
    assert!(is_number(value_at(&app, 0, 0), 8000.0));
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].get_cell(c(0, 0)).unwrap().name.as_deref(), Some("revenue"));
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'y');
    assert!(is_number(value_at(&app, 0, 0), 42.0));

    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'z');
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "7");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Enter);
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'y');
    assert!(app.status().contains("Nothing to redo"), "a new edit discards the redo trail: {}", app.status());
}

#[test]
fn undo_reverts_a_clear_a_cut_and_a_paste() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Delete);
    assert!(value_at(&app, 0, 0).is_none());
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'z');
    assert!(is_number(value_at(&app, 0, 0), 8000.0));

    host.clipboard = Some("1\t2\n3\t4".into());
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'v');
    assert!(is_number(value_at(&app, 1, 1), 4.0));
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'z');
    assert_eq!(app.workbook.sheets[0].get_cell(c(1, 1)).unwrap().value, CellValue::Text("Active".into()));
}

#[test]
fn undo_returns_the_cursor_to_where_the_edit_happened() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Right);
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Delete);
    for _ in 0..5 {
        press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Down);
    }
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'z');
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(1, 0));
}

#[test]
fn undo_history_is_capped() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    for _ in 0..(UNDO_LIMIT + 20) {
        press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Delete);
    }
    assert_eq!(app.undo.len(), UNDO_LIMIT);
}

#[test]
fn reloading_forgets_the_undo_history() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 's');
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Delete);
    assert!(!app.undo.is_empty());
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/FR");
    assert!(app.undo.is_empty() && app.redo.is_empty());
}

// ---- editing like a text editor --------------------------------------------------

#[test]
fn the_edit_line_behaves_like_a_text_editor() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "HELLO WORLD");
    for _ in 0..5 {
        shift(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Left);
    }
    assert_eq!(app.session.selected_text().as_deref(), Some("WORLD"));

    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'c');
    assert_eq!(host.clipboard.as_deref(), Some("WORLD"));
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'x');
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_buffer.as_deref(), Some("HELLO "));
    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'v');
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_buffer.as_deref(), Some("HELLO WORLD"));

    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Home);
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Delete);
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_buffer.as_deref(), Some("ELLO WORLD"));
    key_with(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Right, mods(true, false));
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_cursor, 5, "Ctrl+Right jumps a word");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::End);
    key_with(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Left, mods(true, false));
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_cursor, 5, "Ctrl+Left jumps back to the start of the word");

    ctrl(&mut app, &mut host, 'a');
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "X");
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_buffer.as_deref(), Some("X"), "typing replaces the selection");
}

#[test]
fn backspace_and_typing_work_at_the_caret_not_just_the_end() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "abc");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Left);
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Backspace);
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_buffer.as_deref(), Some("ac"));
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "Z");
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_buffer.as_deref(), Some("aZc"));
}

#[test]
fn a_slash_inside_text_is_text_not_the_menu() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "a/b");
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_buffer.as_deref(), Some("a/b"));
    assert!(!app.session.menu.is_active);
}

#[test]
fn arrows_and_tab_commit_the_edit_and_move() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "5");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Down);
    assert!(is_number(value_at(&app, 0, 0), 5.0));
    assert_eq!((app.session.cursor, app.session.edit_buffer.clone()), (c(0, 1), None));

    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "6");
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Tab);
    assert!(is_number(value_at(&app, 0, 1), 6.0));
    assert_eq!(app.session.cursor, c(1, 1));
}

#[test]
fn f2_edits_the_current_cell_in_place() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::F(2));
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_buffer.as_deref(), Some("8000"));
    assert_eq!(app.session.edit_cursor, 4, "caret at the end");
}

// ---- the menu tree ------------------------------------------------------------------

#[test]
fn every_menu_command_runs_without_panicking() {
    fn leaves(items: &[MenuItem], out: &mut Vec<MenuAction>) {
        for i in items {
            if i.submenus.is_empty() {
                out.push(i.action.clone());
            } else {
                leaves(&i.submenus, out);
            }
        }
    }
    let mut all = Vec::new();
    leaves(&default_lotus_menu(), &mut all);
    assert!(all.len() >= 33, "the menu has grown to {} commands", all.len());

    for action in all {
        let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
        app.session.selection_anchor = Some(c(1, 2)); // give range commands a range
        app.execute(&mut host, action.clone());
        press(&mut app, &mut host, Key::Escape); // dismiss any prompt the command opened
        assert!(app.prompt.is_none(), "{action:?} left a prompt open");
    }
}

#[test]
fn quit_still_works_from_the_longer_menu() {
    let (mut app, mut host) = new_app();
    type_str(&mut app, &mut host, "/Q");
    assert!(host.quit_requested);
}

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-core/src/basic/bytecode.rs
================================================================================
//! Instruction set and value representation for the Lotus Modern BASIC bytecode VM.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{format, string::String};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Value {
    Number(f64),
    StringLit(String),
    Boolean(bool),
    Nil,
}

impl Value {
    pub fn as_f64(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        match self {
            Value::Number(n) => Some(*n),
            Value::Boolean(b) => Some(if *b { 1.0 } else { 0.0 }),
            Value::StringLit(s) => s.parse::<f64>().ok(),
            Value::Nil => None,
        }
    }

    pub fn is_truthy(&self) -> bool {
        match self {
            Value::Boolean(b) => *b,
            Value::Number(n) => *n != 0.0,
            Value::StringLit(s) => !s.is_empty(),
            Value::Nil => false,
        }
    }

    pub fn to_string_repr(&self) -> String {
        match self {
            Value::Number(n) => format!("{}", n),
            Value::StringLit(s) => s.clone(),
            Value::Boolean(b) => (if *b { "TRUE" } else { "FALSE" }).into(),
            Value::Nil => "NIL".into(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Opcode {
    Push(Value),
    Load(String),
    Store(String),
    GetCell,
    SetCell,
    Add,
    Sub,
    Mul,
    Div,
    Eq,
    NotEq,
    Lt,
    LtEq,
    Gt,
    GtEq,
    Jump(usize),
    JumpIfFalse(usize),
    Print,
    TurtleForward,
    TurtleBack,
    TurtleRight,
    TurtleLeft,
    TurtlePenUp,
    TurtlePenDown,
    TurtleColor,
    TurtleWidth,
    TurtleHome,
    TurtleClear,
    TurtleSetPos,
    TurtleSetHeading,
    TurtleLabel,
    CommitTurtle,
    Halt,
}

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-core/src/basic/compiler.rs
================================================================================
//! Single-pass compiler from Modern BASIC source to compact bytecode.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{string::String, string::ToString, vec::Vec};

use super::bytecode::{Opcode, Value};

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum CompileError {
    UnexpectedToken(String),
    UnexpectedEof,
    SyntaxError(String),
}

pub struct Compiler<'a> {
    tokens: Vec<Token>,
    pos: usize,
    code: Vec<Opcode>,
    _phantom: core::marker::PhantomData<&'a ()>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
enum Token {
    Ident(String),
    Number(f64),
    StringLit(String),
    Plus,
    Minus,
    Star,
    Slash,
    Eq,
    NotEq,
    Lt,
    LtEq,
    Gt,
    GtEq,
    LParen,
    RParen,
    Comma,
    Newline,
}

impl<'a> Compiler<'a> {
    pub fn compile(source: &str) -> Result<Vec<Opcode>, CompileError> {
        let tokens = tokenize_basic(source);
        let mut compiler = Self {
            tokens,
            pos: 0,
            code: Vec::new(),
            _phantom: core::marker::PhantomData,
        };
        compiler.parse_program()?;
        compiler.code.push(Opcode::Halt);
        Ok(compiler.code)
    }

    fn peek(&self) -> Option<&Token> {
        self.tokens.get(self.pos)
    }

    fn advance(&mut self) -> Option<Token> {
        if self.pos < self.tokens.len() {
            let tok = self.tokens[self.pos].clone();
            self.pos += 1;
            Some(tok)
        } else {
            None
        }
    }

    fn skip_newlines(&mut self) {
        while let Some(Token::Newline) = self.peek() {
            self.advance();
        }
    }

    fn parse_program(&mut self) -> Result<(), CompileError> {
        self.skip_newlines();
        while self.pos < self.tokens.len() {
            self.parse_statement()?;
            self.skip_newlines();
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn parse_statement(&mut self) -> Result<(), CompileError> {
        self.skip_newlines();
        let tok = match self.peek() {
            Some(t) => t.clone(),
            None => return Ok(()),
        };

        if let Token::Ident(word) = tok {
            let upper = word.to_ascii_uppercase();
            match upper.as_str() {
                "SUB" => {
                    self.advance(); // consume SUB
                    // consume name and optional ()
                    if let Some(Token::Ident(_)) = self.peek() {
                        self.advance();
                    }
                    if self.peek() == Some(&Token::LParen) {
                        self.advance();
                        if self.peek() == Some(&Token::RParen) {
                            self.advance();
                        }
                    }
                    // Parse until END SUB
                    while let Some(t) = self.peek() {
                        if let Token::Ident(w) = t {
                            if w.to_ascii_uppercase() == "END" {
                                self.advance();
                                if let Some(Token::Ident(s)) = self.peek() {
                                    if s.to_ascii_uppercase() == "SUB" {
                                        self.advance();
                                        break;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        self.parse_statement()?;
                        self.skip_newlines();
                    }
                    Ok(())
                }
                "LET" => {
                    self.advance(); // consume LET
                    let var_name = match self.advance() {
                        Some(Token::Ident(name)) => name,
                        _ => return Err(CompileError::SyntaxError("Expected variable name after LET".into())),
                    };
                    match self.advance() {
                        Some(Token::Eq) => {}
                        _ => return Err(CompileError::SyntaxError("Expected '=' after variable name in LET".into())),
                    }
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    self.code.push(Opcode::Store(var_name));
                    Ok(())
                }
                "SET_CELL" => {
                    self.advance(); // consume SET_CELL
                    match self.advance() {
                        Some(Token::LParen) => {}
                        _ => return Err(CompileError::SyntaxError("Expected '(' after SET_CELL".into())),
                    }
                    // Arg 1: Target (coord or name)
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    match self.advance() {
                        Some(Token::Comma) => {}
                        _ => return Err(CompileError::SyntaxError("Expected ',' between SET_CELL arguments".into())),
                    }
                    // Arg 2: Value
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    match self.advance() {
                        Some(Token::RParen) => {}
                        _ => return Err(CompileError::SyntaxError("Expected ')' after SET_CELL arguments".into())),
                    }
                    self.code.push(Opcode::SetCell);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "PRINT" => {
                    self.advance(); // consume PRINT
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    self.code.push(Opcode::Print);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "IF" => {
                    self.advance(); // consume IF
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    if let Some(Token::Ident(w)) = self.peek() {
                        if w.to_ascii_uppercase() == "THEN" {
                            self.advance();
                        }
                    }

                    let jump_false_idx = self.code.len();
                    self.code.push(Opcode::JumpIfFalse(0)); // Placeholder

                    // Body
                    self.skip_newlines();
                    while let Some(t) = self.peek() {
                        if let Token::Ident(w) = t {
                            let u = w.to_ascii_uppercase();
                            if u == "END" || u == "ELSE" {
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                        self.parse_statement()?;
                        self.skip_newlines();
                    }

                    // Check for ELSE
                    let mut has_else = false;
                    let mut jump_end_idx = 0;
                    if let Some(Token::Ident(w)) = self.peek() {
                        if w.to_ascii_uppercase() == "ELSE" {
                            self.advance(); // consume ELSE
                            has_else = true;
                            jump_end_idx = self.code.len();
                            self.code.push(Opcode::Jump(0)); // Placeholder to jump over ELSE body
                            // Patch jump_false to point here
                            self.code[jump_false_idx] = Opcode::JumpIfFalse(self.code.len());

                            self.skip_newlines();
                            while let Some(t) = self.peek() {
                                if let Token::Ident(w) = t {
                                    if w.to_ascii_uppercase() == "END" {
                                        break;
                                    }
                                }
                                self.parse_statement()?;
                                self.skip_newlines();
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Expect END IF
                    if let Some(Token::Ident(w)) = self.advance() {
                        if w.to_ascii_uppercase() == "END" {
                            if let Some(Token::Ident(sub)) = self.advance() {
                                if sub.to_ascii_uppercase() != "IF" {
                                    return Err(CompileError::SyntaxError("Expected 'END IF'".into()));
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    if has_else {
                        self.code[jump_end_idx] = Opcode::Jump(self.code.len());
                    } else {
                        self.code[jump_false_idx] = Opcode::JumpIfFalse(self.code.len());
                    }

                    Ok(())
                }
                "WHILE" => {
                    self.advance(); // consume WHILE
                    let loop_start = self.code.len();
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    let jump_false_idx = self.code.len();
                    self.code.push(Opcode::JumpIfFalse(0)); // Placeholder

                    self.skip_newlines();
                    while let Some(t) = self.peek() {
                        if let Token::Ident(w) = t {
                            if w.to_ascii_uppercase() == "WEND" {
                                self.advance(); // consume WEND
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                        self.parse_statement()?;
                        self.skip_newlines();
                    }

                    // Jump back to start
                    self.code.push(Opcode::Jump(loop_start));
                    self.code[jump_false_idx] = Opcode::JumpIfFalse(self.code.len());
                    Ok(())
                }
                "FOR" => {
                    self.advance(); // consume FOR
                    let var_name = match self.advance() {
                        Some(Token::Ident(name)) => name,
                        _ => return Err(CompileError::SyntaxError("Expected variable name after FOR".into())),
                    };
                    match self.advance() {
                        Some(Token::Eq) => {}
                        _ => return Err(CompileError::SyntaxError("Expected '=' in FOR loop".into())),
                    }
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    self.code.push(Opcode::Store(var_name.clone()));

                    match self.advance() {
                        Some(Token::Ident(to_word)) if to_word.to_ascii_uppercase() == "TO" => {}
                        _ => return Err(CompileError::SyntaxError("Expected 'TO' in FOR loop".into())),
                    }

                    let loop_start = self.code.len();
                    self.code.push(Opcode::Load(var_name.clone()));
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    self.code.push(Opcode::LtEq);
                    let jump_false_idx = self.code.len();
                    self.code.push(Opcode::JumpIfFalse(0));

                    self.skip_newlines();
                    while let Some(t) = self.peek() {
                        if let Token::Ident(w) = t {
                            if w.to_ascii_uppercase() == "NEXT" {
                                self.advance(); // consume NEXT
                                if let Some(Token::Ident(v)) = self.peek() {
                                    if v.to_ascii_uppercase() == var_name.to_ascii_uppercase() {
                                        self.advance(); // consume optional variable after NEXT
                                    }
                                }
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                        self.parse_statement()?;
                        self.skip_newlines();
                    }

                    // Increment variable: var = var + 1
                    self.code.push(Opcode::Load(var_name.clone()));
                    self.code.push(Opcode::Push(Value::Number(1.0)));
                    self.code.push(Opcode::Add);
                    self.code.push(Opcode::Store(var_name));

                    self.code.push(Opcode::Jump(loop_start));
                    self.code[jump_false_idx] = Opcode::JumpIfFalse(self.code.len());
                    Ok(())
                }
                "FORWARD" | "FD" => {
                    self.advance();
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    self.code.push(Opcode::TurtleForward);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "BACK" | "BK" => {
                    self.advance();
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    self.code.push(Opcode::TurtleBack);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "RIGHT" | "RT" => {
                    self.advance();
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    self.code.push(Opcode::TurtleRight);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "LEFT" | "LT" => {
                    self.advance();
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    self.code.push(Opcode::TurtleLeft);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "PENUP" | "PU" => {
                    self.advance();
                    self.code.push(Opcode::TurtlePenUp);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "PENDOWN" | "PD" => {
                    self.advance();
                    self.code.push(Opcode::TurtlePenDown);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "COLOR" | "PENCOLOR" => {
                    self.advance();
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    self.code.push(Opcode::TurtleColor);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "WIDTH" | "PENWIDTH" => {
                    self.advance();
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    self.code.push(Opcode::TurtleWidth);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "HOME" => {
                    self.advance();
                    self.code.push(Opcode::TurtleHome);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "CLEAR" => {
                    self.advance();
                    self.code.push(Opcode::TurtleClear);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "SETPOS" => {
                    self.advance();
                    let has_paren = self.peek() == Some(&Token::LParen);
                    if has_paren { self.advance(); }
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    if self.peek() == Some(&Token::Comma) { self.advance(); }
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    if has_paren && self.peek() == Some(&Token::RParen) { self.advance(); }
                    self.code.push(Opcode::TurtleSetPos);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "SETHEADING" => {
                    self.advance();
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    self.code.push(Opcode::TurtleSetHeading);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "LABEL" => {
                    self.advance();
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    self.code.push(Opcode::TurtleLabel);
                    Ok(())
                }
                "COMMIT_TURTLE" | "SAVE_TURTLE" => {
                    self.advance();
                    let has_paren = self.peek() == Some(&Token::LParen);
                    if has_paren { self.advance(); }
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    if has_paren && self.peek() == Some(&Token::RParen) { self.advance(); }
                    self.code.push(Opcode::CommitTurtle);
                    Ok(())
                }
                _ => {
                    // Implicit LET: var = expr
                    self.advance(); // consume var name
                    if self.peek() == Some(&Token::Eq) {
                        self.advance(); // consume '='
                        self.parse_expr()?;
                        self.code.push(Opcode::Store(word));
                        Ok(())
                    } else {
                        Err(CompileError::SyntaxError(word))
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            self.advance();
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    fn parse_expr(&mut self) -> Result<(), CompileError> {
        self.parse_comparison()
    }

    fn parse_comparison(&mut self) -> Result<(), CompileError> {
        self.parse_addition()?;
        while let Some(tok) = self.peek() {
            let op = match tok {
                Token::Eq => Opcode::Eq,
                Token::NotEq => Opcode::NotEq,
                Token::Lt => Opcode::Lt,
                Token::LtEq => Opcode::LtEq,
                Token::Gt => Opcode::Gt,
                Token::GtEq => Opcode::GtEq,
                _ => break,
            };
            self.advance();
            self.parse_addition()?;
            self.code.push(op);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn parse_addition(&mut self) -> Result<(), CompileError> {
        self.parse_multiplication()?;
        while let Some(tok) = self.peek() {
            let op = match tok {
                Token::Plus => Opcode::Add,
                Token::Minus => Opcode::Sub,
                _ => break,
            };
            self.advance();
            self.parse_multiplication()?;
            self.code.push(op);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn parse_multiplication(&mut self) -> Result<(), CompileError> {
        self.parse_primary()?;
        while let Some(tok) = self.peek() {
            let op = match tok {
                Token::Star => Opcode::Mul,
                Token::Slash => Opcode::Div,
                _ => break,
            };
            self.advance();
            self.parse_primary()?;
            self.code.push(op);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn parse_primary(&mut self) -> Result<(), CompileError> {
        let tok = self.advance().ok_or(CompileError::UnexpectedEof)?;
        match tok {
            Token::Number(n) => {
                self.code.push(Opcode::Push(Value::Number(n)));
                Ok(())
            }
            Token::StringLit(s) => {
                self.code.push(Opcode::Push(Value::StringLit(s)));
                Ok(())
            }
            Token::LParen => {
                self.parse_expr()?;
                match self.advance() {
                    Some(Token::RParen) => Ok(()),
                    _ => Err(CompileError::SyntaxError("Expected ')'".into())),
                }
            }
            Token::Ident(name) => {
                let upper = name.to_ascii_uppercase();
                if upper == "GET_CELL" {
                    match self.advance() {
                        Some(Token::LParen) => {}
                        _ => return Err(CompileError::SyntaxError("Expected '(' after GET_CELL".into())),
                    }
                    self.parse_expr()?;
                    match self.advance() {
                        Some(Token::RParen) => {}
                        _ => return Err(CompileError::SyntaxError("Expected ')' after GET_CELL argument".into())),
                    }
                    self.code.push(Opcode::GetCell);
                    Ok(())
                } else if upper == "TRUE" {
                    self.code.push(Opcode::Push(Value::Boolean(true)));
                    Ok(())
                } else if upper == "FALSE" {
                    self.code.push(Opcode::Push(Value::Boolean(false)));
                    Ok(())
                } else {
                    self.code.push(Opcode::Load(name));
                    Ok(())
                }
            }
            _ => Err(CompileError::UnexpectedToken("Unexpected primary expression".into())),
        }
    }
}

fn tokenize_basic(source: &str) -> Vec<Token> {
    let mut tokens = Vec::new();
    let chars: Vec<char> = source.chars().collect();
    let mut i = 0;

    while i < chars.len() {
        let c = chars[i];
        if c.is_ascii_whitespace() {
            if c == '\n' {
                tokens.push(Token::Newline);
            }
            i += 1;
            continue;
        }

        match c {
            ':' => tokens.push(Token::Newline),
            '\'' => {
                // Comment until end of line
                while i < chars.len() && chars[i] != '\n' {
                    i += 1;
                }
                tokens.push(Token::Newline);
                continue;
            }
            '+' => tokens.push(Token::Plus),
            '-' => tokens.push(Token::Minus),
            '*' => tokens.push(Token::Star),
            '/' => tokens.push(Token::Slash),
            '(' => tokens.push(Token::LParen),
            ')' => tokens.push(Token::RParen),
            ',' => tokens.push(Token::Comma),
            '=' => tokens.push(Token::Eq),
            '<' => {
                if i + 1 < chars.len() && chars[i + 1] == '>' {
                    tokens.push(Token::NotEq);
                    i += 1;
                } else if i + 1 < chars.len() && chars[i + 1] == '=' {
                    tokens.push(Token::LtEq);
                    i += 1;
                } else {
                    tokens.push(Token::Lt);
                }
            }
            '>' => {
                if i + 1 < chars.len() && chars[i + 1] == '=' {
                    tokens.push(Token::GtEq);
                    i += 1;
                } else {
                    tokens.push(Token::Gt);
                }
            }
            '"' => {
                i += 1;
                let mut s = String::new();
                while i < chars.len() && chars[i] != '"' {
                    s.push(chars[i]);
                    i += 1;
                }
                tokens.push(Token::StringLit(s));
            }
            ch if ch.is_ascii_digit() || ch == '.' => {
                let mut num_str = String::from(ch);
                while i + 1 < chars.len() && (chars[i + 1].is_ascii_digit() || chars[i + 1] == '.') {
                    i += 1;
                    num_str.push(chars[i]);
                }
                let val = num_str.parse::<f64>().unwrap_or(0.0);
                tokens.push(Token::Number(val));
            }
            ch if ch.is_alphabetic() || ch == '_' => {
                let mut ident = String::from(ch);
                while i + 1 < chars.len() && (chars[i + 1].is_alphanumeric() || chars[i + 1] == '_') {
                    i += 1;
                    ident.push(chars[i]);
                }
                tokens.push(Token::Ident(ident));
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        i += 1;
    }

    tokens
}

pub mod bytecode;
pub mod compiler;
pub mod runner;
pub mod turtle;
pub mod vm;

pub use bytecode::{Opcode, Value};
pub use compiler::{CompileError, Compiler};
pub use runner::{BasicError, BasicRunner};
pub use turtle::Turtle;
pub use vm::{CellOperation, Vm, VmError};

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::calc::CalcEngine;
    use crate::model::{Cell, CellValue, Coord, Sheet};

    #[test]
    fn test_basic_execution_and_formula_recalc() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        // A1 = 100.0
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 100.0).with_name("revenue"));
        // A2 = =A1 * 1.5
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(0, 1), "=A1 * 1.5"));

        // Recalculate initial state
        CalcEngine::recalculate_sheet(&mut sheet, None).unwrap();
        match &sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 1)).unwrap().value {
            CellValue::Formula { cached_num, .. } => assert_eq!(*cached_num, Some(150.0)),
            _ => panic!("Expected formula"),
        }

        // Run BASIC script that modifies A1
        let script = r#"
        SUB Simulate()
            LET cur = GET_CELL("revenue")
            SET_CELL("A1", cur + 200)
        END SUB
        "#;

        let writes = BasicRunner::execute(&mut sheet, Coord::new(0, 0), script, &[], &[], None).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(writes, 1);

        // Verify A1 was updated to 300.0
        match &sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 0)).unwrap().value {
            CellValue::Number(n) => assert_eq!(*n, 300.0),
            _ => panic!("Expected number"),
        }

        // Verify dependent formula A2 automatically recalculated to 450.0 (300 * 1.5)!
        match &sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 1)).unwrap().value {
            CellValue::Formula { cached_num, .. } => assert_eq!(*cached_num, Some(450.0)),
            _ => panic!("Expected recalculated formula"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_cell_write_requires_explicit_grant() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 10.0));

        // A script owned by A1 cannot write to Z99 without an explicit grant.
        let hostile_script = r#"
        LET cur = GET_CELL("A1")
        SET_CELL("Z99", cur * 10)
        "#;

        let result = BasicRunner::execute(&mut sheet, Coord::new(0, 0), hostile_script, &[], &[], None);

        match result {
            Err(BasicError::Runtime(VmError::CellAccessDenied { coord, operation: vm::CellOperation::Write })) => {
                assert_eq!(coord, Coord::parse_a1("Z99").unwrap());
            }
            _ => panic!("Expected write access denial, got {:?}", result),
        }

        // Verify sheet was untouched
        assert_eq!(sheet.cells.len(), 1);
        assert!(sheet.get_cell(Coord::parse_a1("Z99").unwrap()).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_cross_cell_access_requires_separate_grants() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 9.0).with_name("input"));
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(1, 0), 1.0).with_name("output"));
        let owner = Coord::new(2, 0);
        let script = r#"
            LET input_value = GET_CELL("input")
            SET_CELL("output", input_value + 1)
        "#;
        let input = Coord::new(0, 0);
        let output = Coord::new(1, 0);

        let denied_read = BasicRunner::execute(&mut sheet, owner, script, &[], &[], None);
        assert!(matches!(denied_read, Err(BasicError::Runtime(VmError::CellAccessDenied {
            coord, operation: vm::CellOperation::Read
        })) if coord == input));
        assert_eq!(sheet.get_cell(output).unwrap().value, CellValue::Number(1.0));

        let denied_write = BasicRunner::execute(&mut sheet, owner, script, &[input], &[], None);
        assert!(matches!(denied_write, Err(BasicError::Runtime(VmError::CellAccessDenied {
            coord, operation: vm::CellOperation::Write
        })) if coord == output));
        assert_eq!(sheet.get_cell(output).unwrap().value, CellValue::Number(1.0));

        let writes = BasicRunner::execute(&mut sheet, owner, script, &[input], &[output], None).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(writes, 1);
        assert_eq!(sheet.get_cell(output).unwrap().value, CellValue::Number(10.0));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_step_budget_infinite_loop() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 10.0));

        // Infinite loop
        let runaway_script = r#"
        LET i = 0
        WHILE 1
            LET i = i + 1
        WEND
        "#;

        let result = BasicRunner::execute(&mut sheet, Coord::new(0, 0), runaway_script, &[], &[], Some(500));

        match result {
            Err(BasicError::Runtime(VmError::StepBudgetExceeded)) => {}
            _ => panic!("Expected StepBudgetExceeded, got {:?}", result),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn formula_cell_write_is_rejected_transactionally() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        let formula = Coord::new(0, 0);
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(formula, "=1+2"));
        let script = "SET_CELL(\"A1\", 99)";
        let result = BasicRunner::execute(&mut sheet, Coord::new(1, 0), script, &[], &[formula], None);
        assert!(matches!(result, Err(BasicError::Runtime(VmError::WriteToFormulaCell(c))) if c == formula));
        assert!(matches!(sheet.get_cell(formula).unwrap().value, CellValue::Formula { .. }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_turtle_graphics_script_execution() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        let owner = Coord::new(0, 0);

        // A script drawing a square and labeling it with Hershey font
        let script = r#"
            WIDTH 2
            COLOR "red"
            FOR I = 1 TO 4
                FD 100
                RT 90
            NEXT I
            LABEL "LOGO"
        "#;

        let (writes, vector_buf) = BasicRunner::execute_with_turtle(&mut sheet, owner, script, &[], &[], None)
            .expect("Turtle script should execute successfully");

        assert_eq!(writes, 0, "No cells were directly written");
        assert!(vector_buf.len() > 10, "Vector buffer should contain square lines and label strokes");

        // Verify bounding box of the turtle drawing
        let bounds = vector_buf.bounds().expect("Bounds should exist");
        assert!(bounds.width() > 50.0);
        assert!(bounds.height() > 50.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_turtle_commit_to_far_out_asset_blob() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        let owner = Coord::new(0, 0);
        let target_asset = Coord::parse_a1("A10000").unwrap();

        // Create empty asset cell or grant write to A10000
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::blob(target_asset, "logo_blob", vec![]));

        let script = r#"
            COLOR "cyan"
            WIDTH 3
            FD 50
            RT 90
            FD 50
            COMMIT_TURTLE("A10000")
        "#;

        let writes = BasicRunner::execute(&mut sheet, owner, script, &[], &[target_asset], None)
            .expect("Should execute and commit turtle blob");

        assert_eq!(writes, 1, "Should have committed 1 cell write to A10000");

        // Verify the cell in A10000 now contains a valid serialized VectorBuffer blob
        let cell = sheet.get_cell(target_asset).expect("Cell A10000 should exist");
        match &cell.value {
            CellValue::Blob { bytes } => {
                assert!(!bytes.is_empty());
                let decoded = crate::model::VectorBuffer::from_bytes(bytes)
                    .expect("Blob should decode as valid vector buffer");
                assert!(decoded.len() >= 4);
            }
            _ => panic!("Expected CellValue::Blob"),
        }
    }
}

FILE: lotus-core/src/basic/runner.rs

//! Transactional, cell-scoped execution coordinator for embedded Modern BASIC.
//!
//! Runtime access is limited to workbook-resident cells. Host-side import and
//! export operations remain outside the runtime; scripts cannot access the
//! filesystem, network, processes, or other operating-system services.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{format, string::String};

use super::{
    compiler::{CompileError, Compiler},
    vm::{Vm, VmError},
};
use crate::{
    calc::CalcEngine,
    model::{Coord, Sheet},
};

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum BasicError {
    Compile(CompileError),
    Runtime(VmError),
    Recalc(String),
}

pub struct BasicRunner;

impl BasicRunner {
    /// Compiles and executes a Modern BASIC script against a worksheet.
    ///
    /// Transactional semantics:
    /// - The owning cell is readable and writable by default.
    /// - Other cells require explicit read and/or write grants.
    /// - All writes are staged in a sandbox.
    /// - If the script completes successfully within its grants and step limits,
    ///   the staged writes commit atomically, and all affected formulas recalculate.
    /// - If an error or budget violation occurs, staged writes are discarded and the sheet is unmodified.
    pub fn execute(
        sheet: &mut Sheet,
        owner: Coord,
        source: &str,
        readable_grants: &[Coord],
        writable_grants: &[Coord],
        max_steps: Option<usize>,
    ) -> Result<usize, BasicError> {
        let code = Compiler::compile(source).map_err(BasicError::Compile)?;

        let mut vm = Vm::new(sheet, &code).with_cell_access(owner, readable_grants, writable_grants);
        if let Some(steps) = max_steps {
            vm = vm.with_step_limit(steps);
        }

        vm.run().map_err(BasicError::Runtime)?;

        // Atomic Commit of staged writes
        let num_writes = vm.staged_writes.len();
        for (coord, val) in vm.staged_writes {
            if let Some(cell) = sheet.cells.get_mut(&coord) {
                cell.value = val;
            } else {
                sheet.set_cell(crate::model::Cell::new(coord, val));
            }
        }

        // Trigger DAG recalculation
        CalcEngine::recalculate_sheet(sheet, None).map_err(BasicError::Recalc)?;

        Ok(num_writes)
    }

    /// Compiles and executes a Modern BASIC script, returning the number of committed writes
    /// and the accumulated vector buffer from any turtle graphics operations.
    pub fn execute_with_turtle(
        sheet: &mut Sheet,
        owner: Coord,
        source: &str,
        readable_grants: &[Coord],
        writable_grants: &[Coord],
        max_steps: Option<usize>,
    ) -> Result<(usize, crate::model::VectorBuffer), BasicError> {
        let code = Compiler::compile(source).map_err(BasicError::Compile)?;

        let mut vm = Vm::new(sheet, &code).with_cell_access(owner, readable_grants, writable_grants);
        if let Some(steps) = max_steps {
            vm = vm.with_step_limit(steps);
        }

        vm.run().map_err(BasicError::Runtime)?;

        let vector_output = vm.turtle.buffer.clone();

        // Atomic Commit of staged writes
        let num_writes = vm.staged_writes.len();
        for (coord, val) in vm.staged_writes {
            if let Some(cell) = sheet.cells.get_mut(&coord) {
                cell.value = val;
            } else {
                sheet.set_cell(crate::model::Cell::new(coord, val));
            }
        }

        // Trigger DAG recalculation
        CalcEngine::recalculate_sheet(sheet, None).map_err(BasicError::Recalc)?;

        Ok((num_writes, vector_output))
    }
}
