//! Logo-style Turtle Graphics engine for embedded Modern BASIC.
//!
//! Provides coordinate tracking, relative turtle movement, angle turns,
//! pen attributes, Hershey text rendering along turtle trajectories,
//! and vector command buffer generation.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::string::String;

use crate::model::vector::{ColorRgba, VectorBuffer};
use crate::view::hershey::HersheyFont;

/// A Logo turtle state machine that accumulates vector path commands.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Turtle {
    pub x: f32,
    pub y: f32,
    /// Heading in degrees.
    /// 0° = North / UP (-Y in screen space)
    /// 90° = East / RIGHT (+X in screen space)
    /// 180° = South / DOWN (+Y in screen space)
    /// 270° = West / LEFT (-X in screen space)
    pub heading_deg: f32,
    pub pen_down: bool,
    pub color: ColorRgba,
    pub width: f32,
    pub buffer: VectorBuffer,
}

impl Default for Turtle {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl Turtle {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let mut buffer = VectorBuffer::new();
        buffer.set_color(ColorRgba::WHITE);
        buffer.set_width(1.0);
        buffer.move_to(0.0, 0.0);

        Self {
            x: 0.0,
            y: 0.0,
            heading_deg: 0.0,
            pen_down: true,
            color: ColorRgba::WHITE,
            width: 1.0,
            buffer,
        }
    }

    /// Reset turtle to origin (0, 0) facing North (0°).
    pub fn home(&mut self) {
        self.x = 0.0;
        self.y = 0.0;
        self.heading_deg = 0.0;
        if self.pen_down {
            self.buffer.line_to(0.0, 0.0);
        } else {
            self.buffer.move_to(0.0, 0.0);
        }
    }

    /// Clear all accumulated vector commands and reset turtle state.
    pub fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.buffer.clear();
        self.buffer.set_color(self.color);
        self.buffer.set_width(self.width);
        self.home();
    }

    /// Move turtle forward by `dist` units along its current heading.
    pub fn forward(&mut self, dist: f32) {
        let rad = self.heading_deg.to_radians();
        let sin = rad.sin();
        let cos = rad.cos();

        // 0° is North (screen -Y), 90° is East (screen +X)
        let next_x = self.x + dist * sin;
        let next_y = self.y - dist * cos;

        if self.pen_down {
            self.buffer.line_to(next_x, next_y);
        } else {
            self.buffer.move_to(next_x, next_y);
        }

        self.x = next_x;
        self.y = next_y;
    }

    /// Move turtle backward by `dist` units along its current heading.
    pub fn back(&mut self, dist: f32) {
        self.forward(-dist);
    }

    /// Turn right (clockwise) by `angle_deg` degrees.
    pub fn right(&mut self, angle_deg: f32) {
        self.heading_deg = normalize_degrees(self.heading_deg + angle_deg);
    }

    /// Turn left (counter-clockwise) by `angle_deg` degrees.
    pub fn left(&mut self, angle_deg: f32) {
        self.heading_deg = normalize_degrees(self.heading_deg - angle_deg);
    }

    /// Set pen to down state (subsequent movements draw lines).
    pub fn pen_up(&mut self) {
        self.pen_down = false;
    }

    /// Set pen to down state (subsequent movements draw lines).
    pub fn pen_down(&mut self) {
        self.pen_down = true;
    }

    /// Set stroke color.
    pub fn set_color(&mut self, color: ColorRgba) {
        self.color = color;
        self.buffer.set_color(color);
    }

    /// Set stroke width in points/pixels.
    pub fn set_width(&mut self, width: f32) {
        let width = width.max(0.25);
        self.width = width;
        self.buffer.set_width(width);
    }

    /// Jump to absolute coordinate (x, y).
    pub fn set_pos(&mut self, x: f32, y: f32) {
        if self.pen_down {
            self.buffer.line_to(x, y);
        } else {
            self.buffer.move_to(x, y);
        }
        self.x = x;
        self.y = y;
    }

    /// Set absolute heading in degrees (0° = North, 90° = East, etc.).
    pub fn set_heading(&mut self, deg: f32) {
        self.heading_deg = normalize_degrees(deg);
    }

    /// Render Hershey stroke text oriented along the turtle's current trajectory.
    ///
    /// If heading is 90° (East), text is oriented upright and reads left-to-right.
    /// Advances the turtle along its heading by the measured string advance width.
    pub fn label(&mut self, text: &str) {
        let font = HersheyFont::load_simplex();
        let scale = (self.width * 0.75).clamp(0.5, 5.0);

        // Heading 90° is standard reading left-to-right
        let rotation_rad = (self.heading_deg - 90.0).to_radians();
        let cos_r = rotation_rad.cos();
        let sin_r = rotation_rad.sin();

        let mut pen_advance = 0.0f32;

        for ch in text.chars() {
            if ch == '\n' {
                continue;
            }
            if let Some(glyph) = font.get_glyph(ch) {
                let left_offset = glyph.left as f32;

                for stroke in &glyph.strokes {
                    let mut is_first = true;
                    for &(px, py) in &stroke.points {
                        let gx = (px as f32 - left_offset) * scale + pen_advance;
                        let gy = (py as f32) * scale;

                        // Rotate by turtle orientation
                        let rot_x = gx * cos_r - gy * sin_r;
                        let rot_y = gx * sin_r + gy * cos_r;

                        let screen_x = self.x + rot_x;
                        let screen_y = self.y + rot_y;

                        if is_first {
                            self.buffer.move_to(screen_x, screen_y);
                            is_first = false;
                        } else {
                            self.buffer.line_to(screen_x, screen_y);
                        }
                    }
                }

                let advance = glyph.advance_width() * scale;
                pen_advance += advance;
            } else {
                pen_advance += 16.0 * scale;
            }
        }

        // Advance turtle to the end of the text label
        self.forward(pen_advance);
    }
}

/// Normalize an angle in degrees into [0.0, 360.0).
fn normalize_degrees(deg: f32) -> f32 {
    let rem = deg % 360.0;
    if rem < 0.0 {
        rem + 360.0
    } else {
        rem
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_turtle_square() {
        let mut turtle = Turtle::new();

        // Draw a 100x100 square
        for _ in 0..4 {
            turtle.forward(100.0);
            turtle.right(90.0);
        }

        // Should return close to origin
        assert!(turtle.x.abs() < 0.001);
        assert!(turtle.y.abs() < 0.001);
        assert!((turtle.heading_deg - 0.0).abs() < 0.001);

        // Initial move_to + 4 line_to = 5 operations
        assert_eq!(turtle.buffer.len(), 7); // SetColor, SetWidth, MoveTo(0,0) + 4 LineTo
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_turtle_label() {
        let mut turtle = Turtle::new();
        turtle.set_heading(90.0); // Facing East
        turtle.label("LOTUS");

        assert!(turtle.x > 30.0, "Turtle should have advanced horizontally");
        assert!(turtle.buffer.len() > 10, "Buffer should contain Hershey text strokes");
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-core/src/basic/vm.rs
================================================================================
//! Virtual Machine with cell confinement and transactional write staging.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet}, format, string::String, vec::Vec};
#[cfg(feature = "std")]
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

use super::bytecode::{Opcode, Value};
use super::turtle::Turtle;
use crate::model::{CellValue, ColorRgba, Coord, Sheet};

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum VmError {
    StepBudgetExceeded,
    CellAccessDenied { coord: Coord, operation: CellOperation },
    WriteToFormulaCell(Coord),
    StackUnderflow,
    TypeMismatch(&'static str),
    DivisionByZero,
    InvalidCellTarget(String),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum CellOperation {
    Read,
    Write,
}

pub struct Vm<'a> {
    sheet: &'a Sheet,
    code: &'a [Opcode],
    ip: usize,
    stack: Vec<Value>,
    vars: BTreeMap<String, Value>,
    steps: usize,
    max_steps: usize,
    pub staged_writes: BTreeMap<Coord, CellValue>,
    readable_cells: BTreeSet<Coord>,
    writable_cells: BTreeSet<Coord>,
    pub turtle: Turtle,
}

impl<'a> Vm<'a> {
    pub fn new(sheet: &'a Sheet, code: &'a [Opcode]) -> Self {
        Self {
            sheet,
            code,
            ip: 0,
            stack: Vec::new(),
            vars: BTreeMap::new(),
            steps: 0,
            max_steps: 100_000,
            staged_writes: BTreeMap::new(),
            readable_cells: BTreeSet::new(),
            writable_cells: BTreeSet::new(),
            turtle: Turtle::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn with_step_limit(mut self, max_steps: usize) -> Self {
        self.max_steps = max_steps;
        self
    }

    /// Grant this procedure access to its own cell and explicitly listed cells.
    pub fn with_cell_access(mut self, owner: Coord, readable: &[Coord], writable: &[Coord]) -> Self {
        self.readable_cells.insert(owner);
        self.writable_cells.insert(owner);
        self.readable_cells.extend(readable.iter().copied());
        self.writable_cells.extend(writable.iter().copied());
        self
    }

    pub fn run(&mut self) -> Result<usize, VmError> {
        while self.ip < self.code.len() {
            self.steps += 1;
            if self.steps > self.max_steps {
                return Err(VmError::StepBudgetExceeded);
            }

            match &self.code[self.ip] {
                Opcode::Push(val) => {
                    self.stack.push(val.clone());
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::Load(name) => {
                    let val = self.vars.get(name).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Nil);
                    self.stack.push(val);
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::Store(name) => {
                    let val = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    self.vars.insert(name.clone(), val);
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::GetCell => {
                    let target = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let coord = self.resolve_coord(&target)?;
                    if !self.readable_cells.contains(&coord) {
                        return Err(VmError::CellAccessDenied { coord, operation: CellOperation::Read });
                    }
                    let val = self.read_cell_value(coord);
                    self.stack.push(val);
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::SetCell => {
                    let val = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let target = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let coord = self.resolve_coord(&target)?;

                    if !self.writable_cells.contains(&coord) {
                        return Err(VmError::CellAccessDenied { coord, operation: CellOperation::Write });
                    }
                    if matches!(self.sheet.get_cell(coord).map(|cell| &cell.value), Some(CellValue::Formula { .. })) {
                        return Err(VmError::WriteToFormulaCell(coord));
                    }

                    // Convert Value to CellValue and stage it
                    let cell_val = match val {
                        Value::Number(n) => CellValue::Number(n),
                        Value::StringLit(s) => CellValue::Text(s),
                        Value::Boolean(b) => CellValue::Boolean(b),
                        Value::Nil => CellValue::Empty,
                    };
                    self.staged_writes.insert(coord, cell_val);
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::Add => {
                    let r = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let l = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                        (Some(a), Some(b)) => self.stack.push(Value::Number(a + b)),
                        _ => self.stack.push(Value::StringLit(format!("{}{}", l.to_string_repr(), r.to_string_repr()))),
                    }
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::Sub => {
                    let r = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let l = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                        (Some(a), Some(b)) => self.stack.push(Value::Number(a - b)),
                        _ => return Err(VmError::TypeMismatch("Expected numbers for '-'")),
                    }
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::Mul => {
                    let r = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let l = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                        (Some(a), Some(b)) => self.stack.push(Value::Number(a * b)),
                        _ => return Err(VmError::TypeMismatch("Expected numbers for '*'")),
                    }
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::Div => {
                    let r = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let l = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                        (Some(_), Some(b)) if b == 0.0 => return Err(VmError::DivisionByZero),
                        (Some(a), Some(b)) => self.stack.push(Value::Number(a / b)),
                        _ => return Err(VmError::TypeMismatch("Expected numbers for '/'")),
                    }
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::Eq => {
                    let r = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let l = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    self.stack.push(Value::Boolean(l == r));
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::NotEq => {
                    let r = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let l = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    self.stack.push(Value::Boolean(l != r));
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::Lt => {
                    let r = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let l = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                        (Some(a), Some(b)) => self.stack.push(Value::Boolean(a < b)),
                        _ => self.stack.push(Value::Boolean(l.to_string_repr() < r.to_string_repr())),
                    }
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::LtEq => {
                    let r = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let l = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                        (Some(a), Some(b)) => self.stack.push(Value::Boolean(a <= b)),
                        _ => self.stack.push(Value::Boolean(l.to_string_repr() <= r.to_string_repr())),
                    }
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::Gt => {
                    let r = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let l = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                        (Some(a), Some(b)) => self.stack.push(Value::Boolean(a > b)),
                        _ => self.stack.push(Value::Boolean(l.to_string_repr() > r.to_string_repr())),
                    }
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::GtEq => {
                    let r = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let l = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                        (Some(a), Some(b)) => self.stack.push(Value::Boolean(a >= b)),
                        _ => self.stack.push(Value::Boolean(l.to_string_repr() >= r.to_string_repr())),
                    }
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::Jump(target) => {
                    self.ip = *target;
                }
                Opcode::JumpIfFalse(target) => {
                    let cond = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    if !cond.is_truthy() {
                        self.ip = *target;
                    } else {
                        self.ip += 1;
                    }
                }
                Opcode::Print => {
                    let _ = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::TurtleForward => {
                    let dist = self.pop_f32("FORWARD")?;
                    self.turtle.forward(dist);
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::TurtleBack => {
                    let dist = self.pop_f32("BACK")?;
                    self.turtle.back(dist);
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::TurtleRight => {
                    let angle = self.pop_f32("RIGHT")?;
                    self.turtle.right(angle);
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::TurtleLeft => {
                    let angle = self.pop_f32("LEFT")?;
                    self.turtle.left(angle);
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::TurtlePenUp => {
                    self.turtle.pen_up();
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::TurtlePenDown => {
                    self.turtle.pen_down();
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::TurtleColor => {
                    let val = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let color = match val {
                        Value::Number(n) => ColorRgba::from_u32_rgb(n as u32),
                        Value::StringLit(s) => match s.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
                            "black" => ColorRgba::BLACK,
                            "white" => ColorRgba::WHITE,
                            "red" => ColorRgba::RED,
                            "green" => ColorRgba::GREEN,
                            "blue" => ColorRgba::BLUE,
                            "yellow" => ColorRgba::rgb(255, 255, 0),
                            "cyan" => ColorRgba::rgb(0, 255, 255),
                            "magenta" => ColorRgba::rgb(255, 0, 255),
                            _ => ColorRgba::WHITE,
                        },
                        _ => return Err(VmError::TypeMismatch("Expected color number or string")),
                    };
                    self.turtle.set_color(color);
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::TurtleWidth => {
                    let w = self.pop_f32("WIDTH")?;
                    self.turtle.set_width(w);
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::TurtleHome => {
                    self.turtle.home();
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::TurtleClear => {
                    self.turtle.clear();
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::TurtleSetPos => {
                    let y = self.pop_f32("SETPOS Y")?;
                    let x = self.pop_f32("SETPOS X")?;
                    self.turtle.set_pos(x, y);
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::TurtleSetHeading => {
                    let deg = self.pop_f32("SETHEADING")?;
                    self.turtle.set_heading(deg);
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::TurtleLabel => {
                    let text = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?.to_string_repr();
                    self.turtle.label(&text);
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::CommitTurtle => {
                    let target_val = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
                    let target = self.resolve_coord(&target_val)?;
                    if !self.writable_cells.contains(&target) {
                        return Err(VmError::CellAccessDenied { coord: target, operation: CellOperation::Write });
                    }
                    if let Some(cell) = self.sheet.get_cell(target) {
                        if matches!(cell.value, CellValue::Formula { .. }) {
                            return Err(VmError::WriteToFormulaCell(target));
                        }
                    }
                    let blob_bytes = self.turtle.buffer.to_bytes();
                    self.staged_writes.insert(target, CellValue::Blob { bytes: blob_bytes });
                    self.ip += 1;
                }
                Opcode::Halt => break,
            }
        }

        Ok(self.staged_writes.len())
    }

    fn pop_f32(&mut self, context: &'static str) -> Result<f32, VmError> {
        let val = self.stack.pop().ok_or(VmError::StackUnderflow)?;
        val.as_f64().map(|n| n as f32).ok_or(VmError::TypeMismatch(context))
    }

    fn resolve_coord(&self, val: &Value) -> Result<Coord, VmError> {
        match val {
            Value::StringLit(s) => {
                if let Some(c) = Coord::parse_a1(s) {
                    return Ok(c);
                }
                // Try resolving named cell
                for cell in self.sheet.cells.values() {
                    if cell.name.as_deref() == Some(s) {
                        return Ok(cell.coord);
                    }
                }
                Err(VmError::InvalidCellTarget(s.clone()))
            }
            _ => Err(VmError::InvalidCellTarget(val.to_string_repr())),
        }
    }

    fn read_cell_value(&self, coord: Coord) -> Value {
        // First check locally staged writes
        if let Some(staged) = self.staged_writes.get(&coord) {
            return match staged {
                CellValue::Number(n) => Value::Number(*n),
                CellValue::Text(t) => Value::StringLit(t.clone()),
                CellValue::Boolean(b) => Value::Boolean(*b),
                _ => Value::Nil,
            };
        }

        // Read from sheet
        match self.sheet.get_cell(coord) {
            Some(cell) => match &cell.value {
                CellValue::Number(n) => Value::Number(*n),
                CellValue::Text(t) => Value::StringLit(t.clone()),
                CellValue::Boolean(b) => Value::Boolean(*b),
                CellValue::Formula { cached_num, cached_text, cached_bool, .. } => {
                    if let Some(n) = cached_num {
                        Value::Number(*n)
                    } else if let Some(value) = cached_bool {
                        Value::Boolean(*value)
                    } else if let Some(t) = cached_text {
                        Value::StringLit(t.clone())
                    } else {
                        Value::Nil
                    }
                }
                _ => Value::Nil,
            },
            None => Value::Nil,
        }
    }
}

================================================================================

//! Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) definitions for spreadsheet formulas.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{boxed::Box, string::String, vec::Vec};

use crate::model::Coord;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum BinaryOp {
    Add,      // +
    Sub,      // -
    Mul,      // *
    Div,      // /
    Mod,      // %
    Pow,      // ^
    Concat,   // &
    Eq,       // =
    NotEq,    // <>
    Lt,       // <
    LtEq,     // <=
    Gt,       // >
    GtEq,     // >=
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum UnaryOp {
    Neg, // -
    Not, // NOT
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Expr {
    Number(f64),
    Text(String),
    Boolean(bool),
    CellRef(Coord),
    NamedRef(String),
    RangeRef {
        top_left: Coord,
        bottom_right: Coord,
    },
    Unary {
        op: UnaryOp,
        expr: Box<Expr>,
    },
    Binary {
        op: BinaryOp,
        left: Box<Expr>,
        right: Box<Expr>,
    },
    FunctionCall {
        name: String,
        args: Vec<Expr>,
    },
}

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-core/src/calc/engine.rs
================================================================================
//! Dependency Graph (DAG) and Incremental Topological Recalculator.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{
    collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet},
    string::String,
    vec::Vec,
};
#[cfg(feature = "std")]
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

use super::{
    ast::Expr,
    eval::{EvalValue, Evaluator},
    parser::Parser,
};
use crate::model::{CellValue, Coord, Sheet, Workbook};

pub struct CalcEngine;

impl CalcEngine {
    /// Recalculates all formulas across a sheet using topological dependency ordering.
    /// Detects circular references and marks them with `#CYCLE!`.
    pub fn recalculate_sheet(sheet: &mut Sheet, workbook: Option<&Workbook>) -> Result<usize, String> {
        // 1. Parse all formula cells and extract dependencies
        let mut parsed_formulas: BTreeMap<Coord, (Expr, BTreeSet<Coord>)> = BTreeMap::new();

        for cell in sheet.cells.values() {
            if let CellValue::Formula { expr, .. } = &cell.value {
                if let Ok(ast) = Parser::parse_str(expr) {
                    let mut deps = BTreeSet::new();
                    collect_dependencies(&ast, &mut deps, sheet);
                    parsed_formulas.insert(cell.coord, (ast, deps));
                }
            }
        }

        let formula_cells: BTreeSet<Coord> = parsed_formulas.keys().copied().collect();
        let mut dependents: BTreeMap<Coord, BTreeSet<Coord>> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut in_degree: BTreeMap<Coord, usize> = BTreeMap::new();

        for (coord, (_, deps)) in &parsed_formulas {
            let mut formula_deps_count = 0;
            for dep in deps {
                if formula_cells.contains(dep) {
                    dependents.entry(*dep).or_default().insert(*coord);
                    formula_deps_count += 1;
                }
            }
            in_degree.insert(*coord, formula_deps_count);
        }

        // 2. Cycle Detection using Kahn's algorithm / Topological sort
        let mut queue: Vec<Coord> = Vec::new();
        let mut remaining_in_degree = in_degree.clone();

        for (coord, deg) in &in_degree {
            if *deg == 0 {
                queue.push(*coord);
            }
        }

        let mut eval_order: Vec<Coord> = Vec::new();
        while let Some(coord) = queue.pop() {
            eval_order.push(coord);

            if let Some(downstream) = dependents.get(&coord) {
                for next in downstream {
                    if let Some(deg) = remaining_in_degree.get_mut(next) {
                        *deg = deg.saturating_sub(1);
                        if *deg == 0 {
                            queue.push(*next);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // If eval_order does not contain all formulas, cycles exist!
        let mut cycle_cells = BTreeSet::new();
        for (coord, deg) in &remaining_in_degree {
            if *deg > 0 {
                cycle_cells.insert(*coord);
            }
        }

        // Mark cycle cells with #CYCLE!
        for coord in &cycle_cells {
            if let Some(cell) = sheet.cells.get_mut(coord) {
                if let CellValue::Formula { ref mut cached_num, ref mut cached_text, ref mut cached_bool, .. } = cell.value {
                    *cached_num = None;
                    *cached_text = Some("#CYCLE!".into());
                    *cached_bool = None;
                }
            }
        }

        // 3. Evaluate in topological order
        let mut count = 0;
        for coord in eval_order {
            if let Some((ast, _)) = parsed_formulas.get(&coord) {
                let eval = Evaluator::new(sheet, workbook);
                let result = eval.eval(ast);

                if let Some(cell) = sheet.cells.get_mut(&coord) {
                    if let CellValue::Formula { ref mut cached_num, ref mut cached_text, ref mut cached_bool, .. } = cell.value {
                        match result {
                            EvalValue::Number(n) => {
                                *cached_num = Some(n);
                                *cached_text = None;
                                *cached_bool = None;
                            }
                            EvalValue::Text(t) => {
                                *cached_num = None;
                                *cached_text = Some(t);
                                *cached_bool = None;
                            }
                            EvalValue::Boolean(b) => {
                                *cached_num = None;
                                *cached_text = None;
                                *cached_bool = Some(b);
                            }
                            EvalValue::Empty => {
                                *cached_num = None;
                                *cached_text = None;
                                *cached_bool = None;
                            }
                            EvalValue::Error(e) => {
                                *cached_num = None;
                                *cached_text = Some(e);
                                *cached_bool = None;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                count += 1;
            }
        }

        Ok(count)
    }

    /// Recalculates all sheets in the entire workbook.
    pub fn recalculate_workbook(wb: &mut Workbook) -> Result<usize, String> {
        let mut total = 0;
        let num_sheets = wb.sheets.len();
        for i in 0..num_sheets {
            let mut sheet = wb.sheets[i].clone();
            let count = Self::recalculate_sheet(&mut sheet, None)?;
            wb.sheets[i] = sheet;
            total += count;
        }
        Ok(total)
    }
}

fn collect_dependencies(expr: &Expr, deps: &mut BTreeSet<Coord>, sheet: &Sheet) {
    match expr {
        Expr::CellRef(c) => {
            deps.insert(*c);
        }
        Expr::RangeRef { top_left, bottom_right } => {
            for col in top_left.col..=bottom_right.col {
                for row in top_left.row..=bottom_right.row {
                    deps.insert(Coord::new(col, row));
                }
            }
        }
        Expr::NamedRef(name) => {
            if let Some(cell) = sheet.cells.values().find(|cell| cell.name.as_deref() == Some(name)) {
                deps.insert(cell.coord);
            }
        }
        Expr::Unary { expr, .. } => collect_dependencies(expr, deps, sheet),
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
            collect_dependencies(left, deps, sheet);
            collect_dependencies(right, deps, sheet);
        }
        Expr::FunctionCall { args, .. } => {
            for arg in args {
                collect_dependencies(arg, deps, sheet);
            }
        }
        _ => {}
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-core/src/calc/eval.rs
================================================================================
//! Evaluator and built-in function engine for spreadsheet formulas.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{format, string::String, vec::Vec};

use super::ast::{BinaryOp, Expr, UnaryOp};
use crate::model::{CellValue, Coord, Sheet, Workbook};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum EvalValue {
    Number(f64),
    Text(String),
    Boolean(bool),
    Empty,
    Error(String),
}

impl EvalValue {
    pub fn as_f64(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        match self {
            EvalValue::Number(n) => Some(*n),
            EvalValue::Boolean(b) => Some(if *b { 1.0 } else { 0.0 }),
            EvalValue::Empty => Some(0.0),
            EvalValue::Text(t) => t.parse::<f64>().ok(),
            EvalValue::Error(_) => None,
        }
    }

    pub fn as_string(&self) -> String {
        match self {
            EvalValue::Number(n) => format!("{}", n),
            EvalValue::Text(t) => t.clone(),
            EvalValue::Boolean(b) => (if *b { "TRUE" } else { "FALSE" }).into(),
            EvalValue::Empty => String::new(),
            EvalValue::Error(e) => e.clone(),
        }
    }

    pub fn is_truthy(&self) -> bool {
        match self {
            EvalValue::Boolean(b) => *b,
            EvalValue::Number(n) => *n != 0.0,
            EvalValue::Text(t) => !t.is_empty(),
            EvalValue::Empty => false,
            EvalValue::Error(_) => false,
        }
    }
}

pub struct Evaluator<'a> {
    pub sheet: &'a Sheet,
    pub workbook: Option<&'a Workbook>,
}

impl<'a> Evaluator<'a> {
    pub fn new(sheet: &'a Sheet, workbook: Option<&'a Workbook>) -> Self {
        Self { sheet, workbook }
    }

    pub fn eval(&self, expr: &Expr) -> EvalValue {
        match expr {
            Expr::Number(n) => EvalValue::Number(*n),
            Expr::Text(t) => EvalValue::Text(t.clone()),
            Expr::Boolean(b) => EvalValue::Boolean(*b),

            Expr::CellRef(coord) => self.resolve_cell(*coord),

            Expr::NamedRef(name) => self.resolve_named(name),

            Expr::RangeRef { .. } => {
                // Standalone range reference without aggregation returns #VALUE!
                EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
            }

            Expr::Unary { op, expr } => {
                let val = self.eval(expr);
                match op {
                    UnaryOp::Neg => match val.as_f64() {
                        Some(n) => EvalValue::Number(-n),
                        None => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
                    },
                    UnaryOp::Not => EvalValue::Boolean(!val.is_truthy()),
                }
            }

            Expr::Binary { op, left, right } => {
                let l = self.eval(left);
                let r = self.eval(right);
                self.eval_binary(*op, l, r)
            }

            Expr::FunctionCall { name, args } => self.eval_function(name, args),
        }
    }

    fn resolve_cell(&self, coord: Coord) -> EvalValue {
        match self.sheet.get_cell(coord) {
            Some(cell) => match &cell.value {
                CellValue::Number(n) => EvalValue::Number(*n),
                CellValue::Text(t) => EvalValue::Text(t.clone()),
                CellValue::Boolean(b) => EvalValue::Boolean(*b),
                CellValue::Formula { cached_num, cached_text, cached_bool, .. } => {
                    if let Some(n) = cached_num {
                        EvalValue::Number(*n)
                    } else if let Some(value) = cached_bool {
                        EvalValue::Boolean(*value)
                    } else if let Some(t) = cached_text {
                        EvalValue::Text(t.clone())
                    } else {
                        EvalValue::Empty
                    }
                }
                CellValue::Empty => EvalValue::Empty,
                CellValue::Error(e) => EvalValue::Error(e.clone()),
                CellValue::Basic { .. } | CellValue::Blob { .. } => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
            },
            None => EvalValue::Empty,
        }
    }

    fn resolve_named(&self, name: &str) -> EvalValue {
        // First check current sheet
        for cell in self.sheet.cells.values() {
            if cell.name.as_deref() == Some(name) {
                return self.resolve_cell(cell.coord);
            }
        }

        // Then check full workbook if available
        if let Some(wb) = self.workbook {
            for sheet in &wb.sheets {
                for cell in sheet.cells.values() {
                    if cell.name.as_deref() == Some(name) {
                        let eval = Evaluator::new(sheet, Some(wb));
                        return eval.resolve_cell(cell.coord);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        EvalValue::Error("#NAME?".into())
    }

    fn eval_binary(&self, op: BinaryOp, l: EvalValue, r: EvalValue) -> EvalValue {
        match op {
            BinaryOp::Add => match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                (Some(a), Some(b)) => EvalValue::Number(a + b),
                _ => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
            },
            BinaryOp::Sub => match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                (Some(a), Some(b)) => EvalValue::Number(a - b),
                _ => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
            },
            BinaryOp::Mul => match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                (Some(a), Some(b)) => EvalValue::Number(a * b),
                _ => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
            },
            BinaryOp::Div => match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                (Some(_), Some(b)) if b == 0.0 => EvalValue::Error("#DIV/0!".into()),
                (Some(a), Some(b)) => EvalValue::Number(a / b),
                _ => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
            },
            BinaryOp::Mod => match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                (Some(_), Some(b)) if b == 0.0 => EvalValue::Error("#DIV/0!".into()),
                (Some(a), Some(b)) => EvalValue::Number(a % b),
                _ => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
            },
            BinaryOp::Pow => match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                (Some(a), Some(b)) => {
                    #[cfg(feature = "std")]
                    return EvalValue::Number(a.powf(b));
                    #[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
                    return EvalValue::Number(libm::pow(a, b));
                }
                _ => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
            },
            BinaryOp::Concat => EvalValue::Text(format!("{}{}", l.as_string(), r.as_string())),
            BinaryOp::Eq => EvalValue::Boolean(l == r),
            BinaryOp::NotEq => EvalValue::Boolean(l != r),
            BinaryOp::Lt => match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                (Some(a), Some(b)) => EvalValue::Boolean(a < b),
                _ => EvalValue::Boolean(l.as_string() < r.as_string()),
            },
            BinaryOp::LtEq => match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                (Some(a), Some(b)) => EvalValue::Boolean(a <= b),
                _ => EvalValue::Boolean(l.as_string() <= r.as_string()),
            },
            BinaryOp::Gt => match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                (Some(a), Some(b)) => EvalValue::Boolean(a > b),
                _ => EvalValue::Boolean(l.as_string() > r.as_string()),
            },
            BinaryOp::GtEq => match (l.as_f64(), r.as_f64()) {
                (Some(a), Some(b)) => EvalValue::Boolean(a >= b),
                _ => EvalValue::Boolean(l.as_string() >= r.as_string()),
            },
        }
    }

    fn collect_numbers_from_args(&self, args: &[Expr]) -> Vec<f64> {
        let mut numbers = Vec::new();
        for arg in args {
            match arg {
                Expr::RangeRef { top_left, bottom_right } => {
                    for col in top_left.col..=bottom_right.col {
                        for row in top_left.row..=bottom_right.row {
                            let val = self.resolve_cell(Coord::new(col, row));
                            if let Some(n) = val.as_f64() {
                                numbers.push(n);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                _ => {
                    let val = self.eval(arg);
                    if let Some(n) = val.as_f64() {
                        numbers.push(n);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        numbers
    }

    fn eval_function(&self, name: &str, args: &[Expr]) -> EvalValue {
        match name {
            "SUM" => {
                let nums = self.collect_numbers_from_args(args);
                let sum: f64 = nums.iter().sum();
                EvalValue::Number(sum)
            }
            "AVERAGE" | "AVG" => {
                let nums = self.collect_numbers_from_args(args);
                if nums.is_empty() {
                    EvalValue::Error("#DIV/0!".into())
                } else {
                    let sum: f64 = nums.iter().sum();
                    EvalValue::Number(sum / nums.len() as f64)
                }
            }
            "MIN" => {
                let nums = self.collect_numbers_from_args(args);
                if let Some(min) = nums.into_iter().reduce(f64::min) {
                    EvalValue::Number(min)
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Number(0.0)
                }
            }
            "MAX" => {
                let nums = self.collect_numbers_from_args(args);
                if let Some(max) = nums.into_iter().reduce(f64::max) {
                    EvalValue::Number(max)
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Number(0.0)
                }
            }
            "COUNT" => {
                let nums = self.collect_numbers_from_args(args);
                EvalValue::Number(nums.len() as f64)
            }
            "IF" => {
                if args.len() < 2 {
                    return EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into());
                }
                let cond = self.eval(&args[0]);
                if cond.is_truthy() {
                    self.eval(&args[1])
                } else if args.len() >= 3 {
                    self.eval(&args[2])
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Boolean(false)
                }
            }
            "ABS" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    match self.eval(arg).as_f64() {
                        Some(n) => EvalValue::Number(n.abs()),
                        None => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
                    }
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "ROUND" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    match self.eval(arg).as_f64() {
                        Some(n) => {
                            let decimals = args.get(1).and_then(|a| self.eval(a).as_f64()).unwrap_or(0.0) as i32;
                            let factor = 10f64.powi(decimals);
                            EvalValue::Number((n * factor).round() / factor)
                        }
                        None => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
                    }
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            // =============================
            // Scientific & Math Functions
            // =============================
            "SQRT" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    match self.eval(arg).as_f64() {
                        Some(n) if n >= 0.0 => EvalValue::Number(n.sqrt()),
                        Some(_) => EvalValue::Error("#NUM!".into()),
                        None => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
                    }
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "SIN" => self.eval_single_math(args, f64::sin),
            "COS" => self.eval_single_math(args, f64::cos),
            "TAN" => self.eval_single_math(args, f64::tan),
            "LN" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    match self.eval(arg).as_f64() {
                        Some(n) if n > 0.0 => EvalValue::Number(n.ln()),
                        Some(_) => EvalValue::Error("#NUM!".into()),
                        None => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
                    }
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "LOG" => {
                // LOG(number) = log10, LOG(number, base) = log_base
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    match self.eval(arg).as_f64() {
                        Some(n) if n > 0.0 => {
                            let base = args.get(1)
                                .and_then(|a| self.eval(a).as_f64())
                                .unwrap_or(10.0);
                            if base <= 0.0 || base == 1.0 {
                                EvalValue::Error("#NUM!".into())
                            } else {
                                EvalValue::Number(n.ln() / base.ln())
                            }
                        }
                        Some(_) => EvalValue::Error("#NUM!".into()),
                        None => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
                    }
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "LOG10" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    match self.eval(arg).as_f64() {
                        Some(n) if n > 0.0 => EvalValue::Number(n.log10()),
                        Some(_) => EvalValue::Error("#NUM!".into()),
                        None => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
                    }
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "EXP" => self.eval_single_math(args, f64::exp),
            "POW" | "POWER" => {
                if args.len() >= 2 {
                    match (self.eval(&args[0]).as_f64(), self.eval(&args[1]).as_f64()) {
                        (Some(base), Some(exp)) => EvalValue::Number(base.powf(exp)),
                        _ => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
                    }
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "MOD" => {
                if args.len() >= 2 {
                    match (self.eval(&args[0]).as_f64(), self.eval(&args[1]).as_f64()) {
                        (Some(_), Some(d)) if d == 0.0 => EvalValue::Error("#DIV/0!".into()),
                        (Some(n), Some(d)) => {
                            // Excel-compatible MOD: result has same sign as divisor
                            let r = n - d * (n / d).floor();
                            EvalValue::Number(r)
                        }
                        _ => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
                    }
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "INT" => self.eval_single_math(args, f64::floor),
            "CEILING" | "CEIL" => self.eval_single_math(args, f64::ceil),
            "FLOOR" => self.eval_single_math(args, f64::floor),
            "PI" => EvalValue::Number(core::f64::consts::PI),
            "SIGN" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    match self.eval(arg).as_f64() {
                        Some(n) if n > 0.0 => EvalValue::Number(1.0),
                        Some(n) if n < 0.0 => EvalValue::Number(-1.0),
                        Some(_) => EvalValue::Number(0.0),
                        None => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
                    }
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }

            // =============================
            // Logical Functions
            // =============================
            "AND" => {
                if args.is_empty() {
                    return EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into());
                }
                for arg in args {
                    if !self.eval(arg).is_truthy() {
                        return EvalValue::Boolean(false);
                    }
                }
                EvalValue::Boolean(true)
            }
            "OR" => {
                if args.is_empty() {
                    return EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into());
                }
                for arg in args {
                    if self.eval(arg).is_truthy() {
                        return EvalValue::Boolean(true);
                    }
                }
                EvalValue::Boolean(false)
            }
            "NOT" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    EvalValue::Boolean(!self.eval(arg).is_truthy())
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }

            // =============================
            // String Functions
            // =============================
            "LEN" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    let s = self.eval(arg).as_string();
                    EvalValue::Number(s.len() as f64)
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "LEFT" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    let s = self.eval(arg).as_string();
                    let count = args.get(1).and_then(|a| self.eval(a).as_f64()).unwrap_or(1.0) as usize;
                    let result: String = s.chars().take(count).collect();
                    EvalValue::Text(result)
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "RIGHT" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    let s = self.eval(arg).as_string();
                    let count = args.get(1).and_then(|a| self.eval(a).as_f64()).unwrap_or(1.0) as usize;
                    let chars: Vec<char> = s.chars().collect();
                    let start = chars.len().saturating_sub(count);
                    let result: String = chars[start..].iter().collect();
                    EvalValue::Text(result)
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "MID" => {
                if args.len() >= 3 {
                    let s = self.eval(&args[0]).as_string();
                    let start = self.eval(&args[1]).as_f64().unwrap_or(1.0) as usize;
                    let count = self.eval(&args[2]).as_f64().unwrap_or(0.0) as usize;
                    // MID is 1-based
                    let start_idx = start.saturating_sub(1);
                    let result: String = s.chars().skip(start_idx).take(count).collect();
                    EvalValue::Text(result)
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "CONCATENATE" | "CONCAT" => {
                let mut result = String::new();
                for arg in args {
                    result.push_str(&self.eval(arg).as_string());
                }
                EvalValue::Text(result)
            }
            "TRIM" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    let s = self.eval(arg).as_string();
                    // Excel TRIM: collapse all runs of spaces to single space, strip leading/trailing
                    let mut result = String::new();
                    let mut prev_space = true; // treat start as if preceded by space to strip leading
                    for c in s.chars() {
                        if c == ' ' {
                            if !prev_space {
                                result.push(' ');
                            }
                            prev_space = true;
                        } else {
                            result.push(c);
                            prev_space = false;
                        }
                    }
                    // strip trailing space
                    if result.ends_with(' ') {
                        result.pop();
                    }
                    EvalValue::Text(result)
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "UPPER" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    let s = self.eval(arg).as_string();
                    EvalValue::Text(s.to_ascii_uppercase())
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "LOWER" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    let s = self.eval(arg).as_string();
                    EvalValue::Text(s.to_ascii_lowercase())
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "REPT" | "REPEAT" => {
                if args.len() >= 2 {
                    let s = self.eval(&args[0]).as_string();
                    let count = self.eval(&args[1]).as_f64().unwrap_or(0.0) as usize;
                    EvalValue::Text(s.repeat(count))
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "SUBSTITUTE" => {
                // SUBSTITUTE(text, old, new, [instance_num])
                if args.len() >= 3 {
                    let text = self.eval(&args[0]).as_string();
                    let old = self.eval(&args[1]).as_string();
                    let new = self.eval(&args[2]).as_string();
                    if let Some(instance_arg) = args.get(3) {
                        let instance = self.eval(instance_arg).as_f64().unwrap_or(1.0) as usize;
                        // Replace only the Nth occurrence
                        let mut result = String::new();
                        let mut count = 0usize;
                        let mut remaining = text.as_str();
                        while let Some(pos) = remaining.find(&old) {
                            count += 1;
                            if count == instance {
                                result.push_str(&remaining[..pos]);
                                result.push_str(&new);
                                result.push_str(&remaining[pos + old.len()..]);
                                return EvalValue::Text(result);
                            } else {
                                result.push_str(&remaining[..pos + old.len()]);
                                remaining = &remaining[pos + old.len()..];
                            }
                        }
                        result.push_str(remaining);
                        EvalValue::Text(result)
                    } else {
                        // Replace all occurrences
                        EvalValue::Text(text.replace(&old, &new))
                    }
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "VALUE" => {
                if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
                    let s = self.eval(arg).as_string();
                    match s.trim().parse::<f64>() {
                        Ok(n) => EvalValue::Number(n),
                        Err(_) => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
                    }
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }
            "TEXT" => {
                // Simplified TEXT(value, format) - supports basic number formatting
                if args.len() >= 2 {
                    let val = self.eval(&args[0]).as_f64().unwrap_or(0.0);
                    let fmt = self.eval(&args[1]).as_string();
                    // Count decimal places from format like "0.00"
                    let decimals = if let Some(dot_pos) = fmt.find('.') {
                        fmt[dot_pos + 1..].chars().take_while(|c| *c == '0' || *c == '#').count()
                    } else {
                        0
                    };
                    EvalValue::Text(format!("{:.prec$}", val, prec = decimals))
                } else {
                    EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
                }
            }

            // =============================
            // Lookup Functions
            // =============================
            "VLOOKUP" => self.eval_vlookup(args),
            "HLOOKUP" => self.eval_hlookup(args),
            "INDEX" => self.eval_index(args),
            "MATCH" => self.eval_match(args),

            _ => EvalValue::Error(format!("#UNKNOWN_FN:{}!", name)),
        }
    }

    /// Helper for single-argument math functions (SIN, COS, etc.)
    fn eval_single_math(&self, args: &[Expr], f: fn(f64) -> f64) -> EvalValue {
        if let Some(arg) = args.first() {
            match self.eval(arg).as_f64() {
                Some(n) => EvalValue::Number(f(n)),
                None => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
            }
        } else {
            EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into())
        }
    }

    /// VLOOKUP(lookup_value, range, col_index, [range_lookup])
    fn eval_vlookup(&self, args: &[Expr]) -> EvalValue {
        if args.len() < 3 {
            return EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into());
        }
        let lookup = self.eval(&args[0]);
        let (top_left, bottom_right) = match &args[1] {
            Expr::RangeRef { top_left, bottom_right } => (*top_left, *bottom_right),
            _ => return EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
        };
        let col_index = match self.eval(&args[2]).as_f64() {
            Some(n) => n as usize,
            None => return EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
        };
        if col_index < 1 || col_index > (bottom_right.col - top_left.col + 1) as usize {
            return EvalValue::Error("#REF!".into());
        }
        let exact = if let Some(arg) = args.get(3) {
            !self.eval(arg).is_truthy() // FALSE = exact match
        } else {
            false // default is approximate (TRUE)
        };

        // Search down the first column
        for row in top_left.row..=bottom_right.row {
            let cell_val = self.resolve_cell(Coord::new(top_left.col, row));
            let found = if exact {
                cell_val == lookup
            } else {
                // Approximate: find last value <= lookup_value (assumes sorted)
                match (cell_val.as_f64(), lookup.as_f64()) {
                    (Some(a), Some(b)) => a <= b,
                    _ => cell_val.as_string() <= lookup.as_string(),
                }
            };
            if exact && found {
                let result_col = top_left.col + (col_index as u32 - 1);
                return self.resolve_cell(Coord::new(result_col, row));
            }
            if !exact && !found {
                // We've gone past; use previous row
                if row > top_left.row {
                    let result_col = top_left.col + (col_index as u32 - 1);
                    return self.resolve_cell(Coord::new(result_col, row - 1));
                } else {
                    return EvalValue::Error("#N/A".into());
                }
            }
        }
        if exact {
            EvalValue::Error("#N/A".into())
        } else {
            // Largest value in range
            let result_col = top_left.col + (col_index as u32 - 1);
            self.resolve_cell(Coord::new(result_col, bottom_right.row))
        }
    }

    /// HLOOKUP(lookup_value, range, row_index, [range_lookup])
    fn eval_hlookup(&self, args: &[Expr]) -> EvalValue {
        if args.len() < 3 {
            return EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into());
        }
        let lookup = self.eval(&args[0]);
        let (top_left, bottom_right) = match &args[1] {
            Expr::RangeRef { top_left, bottom_right } => (*top_left, *bottom_right),
            _ => return EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
        };
        let row_index = match self.eval(&args[2]).as_f64() {
            Some(n) => n as usize,
            None => return EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
        };
        if row_index < 1 || row_index > (bottom_right.row - top_left.row + 1) as usize {
            return EvalValue::Error("#REF!".into());
        }
        let exact = if let Some(arg) = args.get(3) {
            !self.eval(arg).is_truthy()
        } else {
            false
        };

        for col in top_left.col..=bottom_right.col {
            let cell_val = self.resolve_cell(Coord::new(col, top_left.row));
            let found = if exact {
                cell_val == lookup
            } else {
                match (cell_val.as_f64(), lookup.as_f64()) {
                    (Some(a), Some(b)) => a <= b,
                    _ => cell_val.as_string() <= lookup.as_string(),
                }
            };
            if exact && found {
                let result_row = top_left.row + (row_index as u32 - 1);
                return self.resolve_cell(Coord::new(col, result_row));
            }
            if !exact && !found {
                if col > top_left.col {
                    let result_row = top_left.row + (row_index as u32 - 1);
                    return self.resolve_cell(Coord::new(col - 1, result_row));
                } else {
                    return EvalValue::Error("#N/A".into());
                }
            }
        }
        if exact {
            EvalValue::Error("#N/A".into())
        } else {
            let result_row = top_left.row + (row_index as u32 - 1);
            self.resolve_cell(Coord::new(bottom_right.col, result_row))
        }
    }

    /// INDEX(range, row_num, [col_num])
    fn eval_index(&self, args: &[Expr]) -> EvalValue {
        if args.is_empty() {
            return EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into());
        }
        let (top_left, _bottom_right) = match &args[0] {
            Expr::RangeRef { top_left, bottom_right } => (*top_left, *bottom_right),
            _ => return EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
        };
        let row_num = if args.len() > 1 {
            self.eval(&args[1]).as_f64().unwrap_or(1.0) as u32
        } else {
            1
        };
        let col_num = if args.len() > 2 {
            self.eval(&args[2]).as_f64().unwrap_or(1.0) as u32
        } else {
            1
        };
        if row_num < 1 || col_num < 1 {
            return EvalValue::Error("#REF!".into());
        }
        let target = Coord::new(
            top_left.col + col_num - 1,
            top_left.row + row_num - 1,
        );
        self.resolve_cell(target)
    }

    /// MATCH(lookup_value, lookup_range, [match_type])
    fn eval_match(&self, args: &[Expr]) -> EvalValue {
        if args.len() < 2 {
            return EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into());
        }
        let lookup = self.eval(&args[0]);
        let (top_left, bottom_right) = match &args[1] {
            Expr::RangeRef { top_left, bottom_right } => (*top_left, *bottom_right),
            _ => return EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
        };
        let match_type = if args.len() > 2 {
            self.eval(&args[2]).as_f64().unwrap_or(1.0) as i32
        } else {
            1
        };

        // Determine if horizontal or vertical range
        let is_row = top_left.row == bottom_right.row;
        let count = if is_row {
            (bottom_right.col - top_left.col + 1) as usize
        } else {
            (bottom_right.row - top_left.row + 1) as usize
        };

        let get_val = |i: usize| -> EvalValue {
            if is_row {
                self.resolve_cell(Coord::new(top_left.col + i as u32, top_left.row))
            } else {
                self.resolve_cell(Coord::new(top_left.col, top_left.row + i as u32))
            }
        };

        match match_type {
            0 => {
                // Exact match
                for i in 0..count {
                    if get_val(i) == lookup {
                        return EvalValue::Number((i + 1) as f64);
                    }
                }
                EvalValue::Error("#N/A".into())
            }
            1 => {
                // Largest value <= lookup (sorted ascending)
                let mut last_match = None;
                for i in 0..count {
                    let v = get_val(i);
                    match (v.as_f64(), lookup.as_f64()) {
                        (Some(a), Some(b)) if a <= b => last_match = Some(i),
                        (Some(a), Some(b)) if a > b => break,
                        _ => {}
                    }
                }
                match last_match {
                    Some(i) => EvalValue::Number((i + 1) as f64),
                    None => EvalValue::Error("#N/A".into()),
                }
            }
            -1 => {
                // Smallest value >= lookup (sorted descending)
                let mut last_match = None;
                for i in 0..count {
                    let v = get_val(i);
                    match (v.as_f64(), lookup.as_f64()) {
                        (Some(a), Some(b)) if a >= b => last_match = Some(i),
                        (Some(a), Some(b)) if a < b => break,
                        _ => {}
                    }
                }
                match last_match {
                    Some(i) => EvalValue::Number((i + 1) as f64),
                    None => EvalValue::Error("#N/A".into()),
                }
            }
            _ => EvalValue::Error("#VALUE!".into()),
        }
    }
}

FILE: lotus-core/src/calc/lexer.rs

//! Lexer for spreadsheet formulas.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{string::String, vec::Vec};
#[cfg(feature = "std")]
use std::vec::Vec;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Token {
    Number(f64),
    StringLit(String),
    Ident(String),
    Colon,
    Comma,
    LParen,
    RParen,
    Plus,
    Minus,
    Star,
    Slash,
    Percent,
    Caret,
    Ampersand,
    Eq,
    NotEq,
    Lt,
    LtEq,
    Gt,
    GtEq,
}

pub struct Lexer<'a> {
    chars: core::str::CharIndices<'a>,
    peeked: Option<(usize, char)>,
}

impl<'a> Lexer<'a> {
    pub fn new(input: &'a str) -> Self {
        let input = input.trim().strip_prefix('=').unwrap_or(input.trim());
        let mut chars = input.char_indices();
        let peeked = chars.next();
        Self {
            chars,
            peeked,
        }
    }

    fn advance(&mut self) -> Option<(usize, char)> {
        let current = self.peeked;
        self.peeked = self.chars.next();
        current
    }

    fn peek(&self) -> Option<char> {
        self.peeked.map(|(_, c)| c)
    }

    fn skip_whitespace(&mut self) {
        while let Some(c) = self.peek() {
            if c.is_whitespace() {
                self.advance();
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn next_token(&mut self) -> Option<Token> {
        self.skip_whitespace();
        let (_start_idx, ch) = self.advance()?;

        let token = match ch {
            '+' => Token::Plus,
            '-' => Token::Minus,
            '*' => Token::Star,
            '/' => Token::Slash,
            '%' => Token::Percent,
            '^' => Token::Caret,
            '&' => Token::Ampersand,
            ':' => Token::Colon,
            ',' => Token::Comma,
            '(' => Token::LParen,
            ')' => Token::RParen,
            '=' => Token::Eq,
            '<' => {
                if self.peek() == Some('>') {
                    self.advance();
                    Token::NotEq
                } else if self.peek() == Some('=') {
                    self.advance();
                    Token::LtEq
                } else {
                    Token::Lt
                }
            }
            '>' => {
                if self.peek() == Some('=') {
                    self.advance();
                    Token::GtEq
                } else {
                    Token::Gt
                }
            }
            '"' => {
                let mut s = String::new();
                while let Some((_, c)) = self.advance() {
                    if c == '"' {
                        break;
                    }
                    s.push(c);
                }
                Token::StringLit(s)
            }
            c if c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '.' => {
                let mut num_str = String::from(c);
                while let Some(peek_ch) = self.peek() {
                    if peek_ch.is_ascii_digit() || peek_ch == '.' {
                        num_str.push(peek_ch);
                        self.advance();
                    } else {
                        break;
                    }
                }
                let num = num_str.parse::<f64>().unwrap_or(0.0);
                Token::Number(num)
            }
            c if c.is_alphabetic() || c == '_' => {
                let mut ident = String::from(c);
                while let Some(peek_ch) = self.peek() {
                    if peek_ch.is_alphanumeric() || peek_ch == '_' || peek_ch == '$' {
                        ident.push(peek_ch);
                        self.advance();
                    } else {
                        break;
                    }
                }
                Token::Ident(ident)
            }
            _ => return None,
        };

        Some(token)
    }

    pub fn tokenize(mut self) -> Vec<Token> {
        let mut tokens = Vec::new();
        while let Some(tok) = self.next_token() {
            tokens.push(tok);
        }
        tokens
    }
}

pub mod ast;
pub mod engine;
pub mod eval;
pub mod lexer;
pub mod parser;
pub mod refs;

pub use ast::{BinaryOp, Expr, UnaryOp};
pub use engine::CalcEngine;
pub use eval::{EvalValue, Evaluator};
pub use lexer::{Lexer, Token};
pub use parser::{ParseError, Parser};
pub use refs::{offset_refs, rewrite_refs, RefKind};

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::model::{Cell, CellValue, Coord, Sheet};

    #[test]
    fn test_formula_parsing_and_evaluation() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 10.0)); // A1 = 10
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 1), 20.0)); // A2 = 20
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 2), 30.0)); // A3 = 30

        // A4 = =A1 + A2 * 2 (Operator precedence: 10 + 40 = 50)
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(0, 3), "=A1 + A2 * 2"));

        // A5 = =SUM(A1:A3) (10 + 20 + 30 = 60)
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(0, 4), "=SUM(A1:A3)"));

        // A6 = =IF(A4 = 50, "PASS", "FAIL")
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(0, 5), "=IF(A4 = 50, \"PASS\", \"FAIL\")"));

        let count = CalcEngine::recalculate_sheet(&mut sheet, None).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(count, 3);

        // Check A4
        match &sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 3)).unwrap().value {
            CellValue::Formula { cached_num, .. } => assert_eq!(*cached_num, Some(50.0)),
            _ => panic!("Expected formula result"),
        }

        // Check A5
        match &sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 4)).unwrap().value {
            CellValue::Formula { cached_num, .. } => assert_eq!(*cached_num, Some(60.0)),
            _ => panic!("Expected formula result"),
        }

        // Check A6
        match &sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 5)).unwrap().value {
            CellValue::Formula { cached_text, .. } => assert_eq!(cached_text.as_deref(), Some("PASS")),
            _ => panic!("Expected formula result"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_cycle_detection() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        // A1 depends on B1
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(0, 0), "=B1"));
        // B1 depends on A1
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(1, 0), "=A1"));

        CalcEngine::recalculate_sheet(&mut sheet, None).unwrap();

        match &sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 0)).unwrap().value {
            CellValue::Formula { cached_text, .. } => assert_eq!(cached_text.as_deref(), Some("#CYCLE!")),
            _ => panic!("Expected #CYCLE!"),
        }

        match &sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(1, 0)).unwrap().value {
            CellValue::Formula { cached_text, .. } => assert_eq!(cached_text.as_deref(), Some("#CYCLE!")),
            _ => panic!("Expected #CYCLE!"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn boolean_formula_cache_keeps_boolean_type() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 3.0));
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(1, 0), "=A1>5"));
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(2, 0), "=IF(B1,\"big\",\"small\")"));
        CalcEngine::recalculate_sheet(&mut sheet, None).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(1, 0)).unwrap().value, CellValue::Formula { cached_bool: Some(false), .. }));
        assert!(matches!(&sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(2, 0)).unwrap().value, CellValue::Formula { cached_text: Some(v), .. } if v == "small"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn named_formula_dependencies_are_ordered_and_cycles_detected() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(0, 0), "=tax_amount*2"));
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(1, 0), "=2+3").with_name("tax_amount"));
        CalcEngine::recalculate_sheet(&mut sheet, None).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 0)).unwrap().value, CellValue::Formula { cached_num: Some(10.0), .. }));

        let mut cycle = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        cycle.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(0, 0), "=beta").with_name("alpha"));
        cycle.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(1, 0), "=alpha").with_name("beta"));
        CalcEngine::recalculate_sheet(&mut cycle, None).unwrap();
        for coord in [Coord::new(0, 0), Coord::new(1, 0)] {
            assert!(matches!(&cycle.get_cell(coord).unwrap().value, CellValue::Formula { cached_text: Some(v), .. } if v == "#CYCLE!"));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn coordinate_like_names_are_parsed_as_cell_refs() {
        assert_eq!(Coord::parse_a1("q3").unwrap(), Coord::new(16, 2));
    }
}
