//! Vector graphics primitives, command stream representation, and binary serialization.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{vec, vec::Vec};

/// A 2D point in continuous vector coordinate space.
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct Point2D {
    pub x: f32,
    pub y: f32,
}

impl Point2D {
    pub const fn new(x: f32, y: f32) -> Self {
        Self { x, y }
    }
}

/// A 2D bounding rectangle in vector space.
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct Rect2D {
    pub min_x: f32,
    pub min_y: f32,
    pub max_x: f32,
    pub max_y: f32,
}

impl Rect2D {
    pub fn new(min_x: f32, min_y: f32, max_x: f32, max_y: f32) -> Self {
        Self { min_x, min_y, max_x, max_y }
    }

    pub fn width(&self) -> f32 {
        self.max_x - self.min_x
    }

    pub fn height(&self) -> f32 {
        self.max_y - self.min_y
    }
}

/// 32-bit RGBA color representation.
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ColorRgba {
    pub r: u8,
    pub g: u8,
    pub b: u8,
    pub a: u8,
}

impl ColorRgba {
    pub const BLACK: Self = Self::rgba(0, 0, 0, 255);
    pub const WHITE: Self = Self::rgba(255, 255, 255, 255);
    pub const RED: Self = Self::rgba(255, 0, 0, 255);
    pub const GREEN: Self = Self::rgba(0, 255, 0, 255);
    pub const BLUE: Self = Self::rgba(0, 0, 255, 255);
    pub const TRANSPARENT: Self = Self::rgba(0, 0, 0, 0);

    pub const fn rgb(r: u8, g: u8, b: u8) -> Self {
        Self { r, g, b, a: 255 }
    }

    pub const fn rgba(r: u8, g: u8, b: u8, a: u8) -> Self {
        Self { r, g, b, a }
    }

    /// Construct from a 0x00RRGGBB integer (with alpha = 255).
    pub const fn from_u32_rgb(val: u32) -> Self {
        Self {
            r: ((val >> 16) & 0xFF) as u8,
            g: ((val >> 8) & 0xFF) as u8,
            b: (val & 0xFF) as u8,
            a: 255,
        }
    }

    /// Pack into 0x00RRGGBB format.
    pub const fn to_u32_rgb(&self) -> u32 {
        ((self.r as u32) << 16) | ((self.g as u32) << 8) | (self.b as u32)
    }

    /// Pack into 0xAARRGGBB format.
    pub const fn to_u32_argb(&self) -> u32 {
        ((self.a as u32) << 24) | ((self.r as u32) << 16) | ((self.g as u32) << 8) | (self.b as u32)
    }
}

/// Stroke styling attributes.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct StrokeStyle {
    pub width: f32,
    pub color: ColorRgba,
}

impl StrokeStyle {
    pub const fn new(width: f32, color: ColorRgba) -> Self {
        Self { width, color }
    }
}

/// A discrete vector path operation.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum VectorOp {
    MoveTo(Point2D),
    LineTo(Point2D),
    SetColor(ColorRgba),
    SetWidth(f32),
    ClosePoly,
}

/// An append-only buffer of vector operations.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct VectorBuffer {
    pub ops: Vec<VectorOp>,
}

impl VectorBuffer {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { ops: Vec::new() }
    }

    pub fn with_capacity(capacity: usize) -> Self {
        Self { ops: Vec::with_capacity(capacity) }
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.ops.is_empty()
    }

    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.ops.len()
    }

    pub fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.ops.clear();
    }

    pub fn move_to(&mut self, x: f32, y: f32) {
        self.ops.push(VectorOp::MoveTo(Point2D::new(x, y)));
    }

    pub fn line_to(&mut self, x: f32, y: f32) {
        self.ops.push(VectorOp::LineTo(Point2D::new(x, y)));
    }

    pub fn set_color(&mut self, color: ColorRgba) {
        self.ops.push(VectorOp::SetColor(color));
    }

    pub fn set_width(&mut self, width: f32) {
        self.ops.push(VectorOp::SetWidth(width));
    }

    pub fn close_poly(&mut self) {
        self.ops.push(VectorOp::ClosePoly);
    }

    /// Append a rectangle path (unfilled outline).
    pub fn rect(&mut self, x: f32, y: f32, w: f32, h: f32) {
        self.move_to(x, y);
        self.line_to(x + w, y);
        self.line_to(x + w, y + h);
        self.line_to(x, y + h);
        self.close_poly();
    }

    /// Compute the bounding rectangle of all points in the buffer.
    pub fn bounds(&self) -> Option<Rect2D> {
        let mut min_x = f32::MAX;
        let mut min_y = f32::MAX;
        let mut max_x = f32::MIN;
        let mut max_y = f32::MIN;
        let mut has_points = false;

        for op in &self.ops {
            let pt = match op {
                VectorOp::MoveTo(p) | VectorOp::LineTo(p) => Some(*p),
                _ => None,
            };
            if let Some(p) = pt {
                has_points = true;
                if p.x < min_x { min_x = p.x; }
                if p.y < min_y { min_y = p.y; }
                if p.x > max_x { max_x = p.x; }
                if p.y > max_y { max_y = p.y; }
            }
        }

        if has_points {
            Some(Rect2D::new(min_x, min_y, max_x, max_y))
        } else {
            None
        }
    }

    /// Serialize into a compact binary byte slice suitable for `CellValue::Blob`.
    ///
    /// Opcode Tags:
    /// - 0x01: MoveTo (f32, f32) [9 bytes]
    /// - 0x02: LineTo (f32, f32) [9 bytes]
    /// - 0x03: SetColor (u8, u8, u8, u8) [5 bytes]
    /// - 0x04: SetWidth (f32) [5 bytes]
    /// - 0x05: ClosePoly [1 byte]
    pub fn to_bytes(&self) -> Vec<u8> {
        let mut bytes = Vec::new();
        // Magic header: b"LVEC" (Lotus Vector) + version 1
        bytes.extend_from_slice(b"LVEC\x01");

        for op in &self.ops {
            match op {
                VectorOp::MoveTo(p) => {
                    bytes.push(0x01);
                    bytes.extend_from_slice(&p.x.to_le_bytes());
                    bytes.extend_from_slice(&p.y.to_le_bytes());
                }
                VectorOp::LineTo(p) => {
                    bytes.push(0x02);
                    bytes.extend_from_slice(&p.x.to_le_bytes());
                    bytes.extend_from_slice(&p.y.to_le_bytes());
                }
                VectorOp::SetColor(c) => {
                    bytes.push(0x03);
                    bytes.push(c.r);
                    bytes.push(c.g);
                    bytes.push(c.b);
                    bytes.push(c.a);
                }
                VectorOp::SetWidth(w) => {
                    bytes.push(0x04);
                    bytes.extend_from_slice(&w.to_le_bytes());
                }
                VectorOp::ClosePoly => {
                    bytes.push(0x05);
                }
            }
        }
        bytes
    }

    /// Deserialize a vector buffer from a compact binary byte slice.
    pub fn from_bytes(data: &[u8]) -> Result<Self, &'static str> {
        if data.len() < 5 {
            return Err("Binary data too short for vector header");
        }
        if &data[0..4] != b"LVEC" {
            return Err("Invalid vector magic header");
        }
        if data[4] != 0x01 {
            return Err("Unsupported vector format version");
        }

        let mut ops = Vec::new();
        let mut idx = 5;

        while idx < data.len() {
            let tag = data[idx];
            idx += 1;
            match tag {
                0x01 => {
                    if idx + 8 > data.len() { return Err("Truncated MoveTo operation"); }
                    let x = f32::from_le_bytes(data[idx..idx + 4].try_into().unwrap());
                    let y = f32::from_le_bytes(data[idx + 4..idx + 8].try_into().unwrap());
                    idx += 8;
                    ops.push(VectorOp::MoveTo(Point2D::new(x, y)));
                }
                0x02 => {
                    if idx + 8 > data.len() { return Err("Truncated LineTo operation"); }
                    let x = f32::from_le_bytes(data[idx..idx + 4].try_into().unwrap());
                    let y = f32::from_le_bytes(data[idx + 4..idx + 8].try_into().unwrap());
                    idx += 8;
                    ops.push(VectorOp::LineTo(Point2D::new(x, y)));
                }
                0x03 => {
                    if idx + 4 > data.len() { return Err("Truncated SetColor operation"); }
                    let r = data[idx];
                    let g = data[idx + 1];
                    let b = data[idx + 2];
                    let a = data[idx + 3];
                    idx += 4;
                    ops.push(VectorOp::SetColor(ColorRgba::rgba(r, g, b, a)));
                }
                0x04 => {
                    if idx + 4 > data.len() { return Err("Truncated SetWidth operation"); }
                    let w = f32::from_le_bytes(data[idx..idx + 4].try_into().unwrap());
                    idx += 4;
                    ops.push(VectorOp::SetWidth(w));
                }
                0x05 => {
                    ops.push(VectorOp::ClosePoly);
                }
                _ => return Err("Unknown vector opcode tag"),
            }
        }

        Ok(Self { ops })
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_vector_buffer_roundtrip() {
        let mut buf = VectorBuffer::new();
        buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(255, 128, 0));
        buf.set_width(2.5);
        buf.rect(10.0, 20.0, 100.0, 50.0);

        assert_eq!(buf.len(), 7); // SetColor + SetWidth + MoveTo + 3*LineTo + ClosePoly
        let bounds = buf.bounds().expect("bounds should exist");
        assert_eq!(bounds.min_x, 10.0);
        assert_eq!(bounds.min_y, 20.0);
        assert_eq!(bounds.max_x, 110.0);
        assert_eq!(bounds.max_y, 70.0);

        let bytes = buf.to_bytes();
        let decoded = VectorBuffer::from_bytes(&bytes).expect("deserialization should succeed");
        assert_eq!(buf, decoded);
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-core/src/model/workbook.rs
================================================================================
//! Workbook container and spatial extent calculator.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{collections::BTreeMap, format, string::String, string::ToString, vec, vec::Vec};
#[cfg(feature = "std")]
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

use super::{
    cell::{Cell, CellValue},
    coord::Coord,
    sheet::Sheet,
    toc::{TableOfContents, TocEntry},
};
use crate::{
    calc::ast::Expr,
    doc::{
        BrokenDocCellRef, BrokenDocRef, BrokenSectionRef, DanglingFormulaRef,
        Document, DocumentRefStatus, OrphanReport,
    },
    history::HistoryEngine,
};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Workbook {
    pub id: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub calc_mode: String,
    pub sheets: Vec<Sheet>,
    pub documents: Vec<Document>,
    /// Historical cell overlays, keyed by worksheet ID. The base sheets are Z=0.
    pub history: BTreeMap<String, HistoryEngine>,
    /// Manifest tracking z-layer status and provenance
    pub manifest: Manifest,
    /// Merkle chain for z-layer integrity tracking
    pub merkle_chain: MerkleChain,
}

impl Workbook {
    pub fn new(id: impl Into<String>, title: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: id.into(),
            title: title.into(),
            calc_mode: "auto".into(),
            sheets: Vec::new(),
            documents: Vec::new(),
            history: BTreeMap::new(),
            manifest: Manifest::new(),
            merkle_chain: MerkleChain::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn with_document(mut self, doc: Document) -> Self {
        self.documents.push(doc);
        self
    }

    pub fn get_document(&self, id: &str) -> Option<&Document> {
        self.documents.iter().find(|d| d.id == id)
    }

    /// Documents explicitly linked from a cell, resolving each target's document ID.
    pub fn documents_linked_from_cell(&self, sheet_id: &str, coord: Coord) -> Vec<&Document> {
        self.documents_linked_from_cell_at_z(sheet_id, coord, 0)
    }

    pub fn documents_linked_from_cell_at_z(&self, sheet_id: &str, coord: Coord, z: u32) -> Vec<&Document> {
        let Some(sheet) = self.sheet_at_z(sheet_id, z) else { return Vec::new() };
        let Some(cell) = sheet.get_cell(coord) else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        self.documents.iter().filter(|document| {
            cell.document_refs.iter().any(|target| document_ref_id(target) == document.id)
        }).collect()
    }

    /// Documents whose explicit live-cell templates refer to this cell by A1 coordinate or name.
    pub fn documents_referencing_cell(&self, sheet_id: &str, coord: Coord) -> Vec<&Document> {
        self.documents_referencing_cell_at_z(sheet_id, coord, 0)
    }

    pub fn documents_referencing_cell_at_z(&self, sheet_id: &str, coord: Coord, z: u32) -> Vec<&Document> {
        let Some(sheet) = self.sheet_at_z(sheet_id, z) else { return Vec::new() };
        let Some(cell) = sheet.get_cell(coord) else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let default_sheet_id = self.primary_sheet().map(|sheet| sheet.id.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
        self.documents.iter()
            .filter(|document| document.references_cell(cell, sheet_id, default_sheet_id))
            .collect()
    }

    /// Validate an outgoing document reference (e.g. "briefing" or "briefing#inputs"):
    /// - Checks whether the document ID exists.
    /// - If a section fragment is present, verifies that the document contains a matching heading.
    pub fn validate_document_ref(&self, target: &str) -> DocumentRefStatus {
        let (doc_id, fragment) = match target.split_once('#') {
            Some((id, frag)) => (id.trim(), Some(frag.trim())),
            None => (target.trim(), None),
        };
        let Some(doc) = self.get_document(doc_id) else {
            return DocumentRefStatus::UnresolvedDocument;
        };
        if let Some(frag) = fragment {
            if frag.is_empty() {
                DocumentRefStatus::Valid
            } else if let Some(heading) = doc.find_section(frag) {
                DocumentRefStatus::ValidWithSection(heading.title)
            } else {
                DocumentRefStatus::UnresolvedSection(frag.to_string())
            }
        } else {
            DocumentRefStatus::Valid
        }
    }

    /// Audit the workbook for orphaned entities and broken references across:
    /// 1. Outgoing cell document references pointing to missing documents or missing section anchors.
    /// 2. Document-embedded live cell templates (`{{cell:...}}`) pointing to missing sheets or missing cells.
    /// 3. Embedded documents with zero incoming cell references.
    /// 4. Formula cells with dangling dependencies on non-existent or empty cells.
    pub fn check_orphans(&self) -> OrphanReport {
        let mut report = OrphanReport::default();

        // 1. Broken cell -> document references
        for sheet in &self.sheets {
            for cell in sheet.cells.values() {
                for target in &cell.document_refs {
                    match self.validate_document_ref(target) {
                        DocumentRefStatus::UnresolvedDocument => {
                            let doc_id = target.split_once('#').map_or(target.as_str(), |(id, _)| id);
                            report.broken_document_refs.push(BrokenDocRef {
                                sheet_id: sheet.id.clone(),
                                coord: cell.coord,
                                cell_name: cell.name.clone(),
                                target: target.clone(),
                                reason: format!("Document '{}' not found", doc_id),
                            });
                        }
                        DocumentRefStatus::UnresolvedSection(frag) => {
                            let doc_id = target.split_once('#').map_or(target.as_str(), |(id, _)| id);
                            report.broken_section_refs.push(BrokenSectionRef {
                                sheet_id: sheet.id.clone(),
                                coord: cell.coord,
                                cell_name: cell.name.clone(),
                                document_id: doc_id.to_string(),
                                section_anchor: frag,
                                target: target.clone(),
                            });
                        }
                        _ => {}
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. Broken document -> cell references
        let default_sheet_id = self.primary_sheet().map(|s| s.id.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
        for doc in &self.documents {
            for target in doc.referenced_cells() {
                let (sheet_id, cell_ref) = target.split_once('!')
                    .unwrap_or((default_sheet_id, target.as_str()));
                let Some(sheet) = self.get_sheet(sheet_id) else {
                    report.broken_document_cell_refs.push(BrokenDocCellRef {
                        document_id: doc.id.clone(),
                        target: target.clone(),
                        reason: format!("Worksheet '{}' not found", sheet_id),
                    });
                    continue;
                };
                let found = if let Some(coord) = Coord::parse_a1(cell_ref) {
                    sheet.get_cell(coord).is_some()
                } else {
                    sheet.cells.values().any(|c| c.name.as_deref() == Some(cell_ref))
                };
                if !found {
                    report.broken_document_cell_refs.push(BrokenDocCellRef {
                        document_id: doc.id.clone(),
                        target: target.clone(),
                        reason: format!("Cell '{}' not found on sheet '{}'", cell_ref, sheet_id),
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        // 3. Unreferenced documents (documents not referenced by any cell's document_refs)
        for doc in &self.documents {
            let is_referenced = self.sheets.iter().any(|sheet| {
                sheet.cells.values().any(|cell| {
                    cell.document_refs.iter().any(|target| {
                        document_ref_id(target) == doc.id
                    })
                })
            });
            if !is_referenced {
                report.unreferenced_documents.push(doc.id.clone());
            }
        }

        // 4. Dangling formula dependencies
        for sheet in &self.sheets {
            for cell in sheet.cells.values() {
                if let CellValue::Formula { expr, .. } = &cell.value {
                    if let Ok(ast) = crate::calc::Parser::parse_str(expr) {
                        let mut raw_targets = Vec::new();
                        collect_raw_formula_targets(&ast, &mut raw_targets);
                        for raw_target in raw_targets {
                            match raw_target {
                                RawFormulaTarget::Coord(coord) => {
                                    if sheet.get_cell(coord).is_none() {
                                        report.dangling_formula_refs.push(DanglingFormulaRef {
                                            sheet_id: sheet.id.clone(),
                                            coord: cell.coord,
                                            formula_name: cell.name.clone(),
                                            target: format!("{}", coord),
                                        });
                                    }
                                }
                                RawFormulaTarget::Name(name) => {
                                    if !sheet.cells.values().any(|c| c.name.as_deref() == Some(&name)) {
                                        report.dangling_formula_refs.push(DanglingFormulaRef {
                                            sheet_id: sheet.id.clone(),
                                            coord: cell.coord,
                                            formula_name: cell.name.clone(),
                                            target: name,
                                        });
                                    }
                                }
                                RawFormulaTarget::Range(tl, br) => {
                                    let mut any_cell_found = false;
                                    for col in tl.col..=br.col {
                                        for row in tl.row..=br.row {
                                            if sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(col, row)).is_some() {
                                                any_cell_found = true;
                                                break;
                                            }
                                        }
                                        if any_cell_found {
                                            break;
                                        }
                                    }
                                    if !any_cell_found {
                                        report.dangling_formula_refs.push(DanglingFormulaRef {
                                            sheet_id: sheet.id.clone(),
                                            coord: cell.coord,
                                            formula_name: cell.name.clone(),
                                            target: format!("{}:{}", tl, br),
                                        });
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        report
    }

    pub fn with_sheet(mut self, sheet: Sheet) -> Self {
        self.sheets.push(sheet);
        self
    }

    pub fn primary_sheet(&self) -> Option<&Sheet> {
        self.sheets.first()
    }

    pub fn primary_sheet_mut(&mut self) -> Option<&mut Sheet> {
        self.sheets.first_mut()
    }

    pub fn get_sheet(&self, id: &str) -> Option<&Sheet> {
        self.sheets.iter().find(|s| s.id == id)
    }

    pub fn get_sheet_mut(&mut self, id: &str) -> Option<&mut Sheet> {
        self.sheets.iter_mut().find(|s| s.id == id)
    }

    pub fn history_for_sheet(&self, id: &str) -> Option<&HistoryEngine> {
        self.history.get(id)
    }

    /// Compose a worksheet at Z=0 (the base) or at a stored historical layer.
    pub fn sheet_at_z(&self, id: &str, z: u32) -> Option<Sheet> {
        let base = self.get_sheet(id)?;
        match self.history.get(id) {
            Some(history) if z <= history.max_z => Some(history.composite_at_z(base, z)),
            None if z == 0 => Some(base.clone()),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// Record changes as a new historical layer without modifying the base sheet.
    pub fn record_history_layer(
        &mut self,
        sheet_id: &str,
        changes: Vec<(Coord, Option<Cell>)>,
    ) -> Result<u32, &'static str> {
        if self.get_sheet(sheet_id).is_none() {
            return Err("Sheet not found");
        }
        let history = self.history.entry(sheet_id.into()).or_insert_with(HistoryEngine::new);
        history.record_layer(changes)
    }

    /// Dynamically computes the Table of Contents (TOC) anchored at (maxX + 1, maxY + 1).
    pub fn compute_toc(&self) -> TableOfContents {
        let mut max_col = 0;
        let mut max_row = 0;
        let mut has_cells = false;
        let mut entries = Vec::new();

        for sheet in &self.sheets {
            if let Some((scol, srow)) = sheet.active_grid_bounds() {
                has_cells = true;
                if scol > max_col {
                    max_col = scol;
                }
                if srow > max_row {
                    max_row = srow;
                }
            }

            for cell in sheet.cells.values() {
                if let Some(ref name) = cell.name {
                    let (type_name, byte_size) = match &cell.value {
                        CellValue::Blob { bytes } => ("blob", bytes.len()),
                        CellValue::Basic { source } => ("basic", source.len()),
                        CellValue::Formula { expr, .. } => ("formula", expr.len()),
                        CellValue::Text(t) => ("text", t.len()),
                        CellValue::Number(_) => ("number", 8),
                        CellValue::Boolean(_) => ("boolean", 1),
                        CellValue::Empty => ("empty", 0),
                        CellValue::Error(e) => ("error", e.len()),
                    };

                    entries.push(TocEntry {
                        sheet_id: sheet.id.clone(),
                        coord: cell.coord,
                        name: name.clone(),
                        type_name: String::from(type_name),
                        byte_size,
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        let (toc_col, toc_row) = if has_cells {
            (max_col + 1, max_row + 1)
        } else {
            (0, 0)
        };

        TableOfContents {
            pos: Coord::new(toc_col, toc_row),
            extent_col: max_col,
            extent_row: max_row,
            entries,
        }
    }

    /// Eject a named cell as an exact byte slice.
    pub fn eject_cell(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&[u8]> {
        for doc in &self.documents {
            if doc.id == name {
                return Some(doc.body.as_bytes());
            }
        }

        for sheet in &self.sheets {
            for cell in sheet.cells.values() {
                if cell.name.as_deref() == Some(name) {
                    return match &cell.value {
                        CellValue::Blob { bytes } => Some(bytes.as_slice()),
                        CellValue::Basic { source } => Some(source.as_bytes()),
                        CellValue::Text(t) => Some(t.as_bytes()),
                        _ => None,
                    };
                }
            }
        }
        None
    }

    /// Import a raw byte sequence into a named cell at the given coordinate.
    pub fn import_blob(
        &mut self,
        sheet_idx: usize,
        coord: Coord,
        name: impl Into<String>,
        bytes: Vec<u8>,
    ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
        let sheet = self.sheets.get_mut(sheet_idx).ok_or("Sheet not found")?;
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::blob(coord, name, bytes));
        Ok(())
    }
}

enum RawFormulaTarget {
    Coord(Coord),
    Name(String),
    Range(Coord, Coord),
}

fn collect_raw_formula_targets(expr: &Expr, targets: &mut Vec<RawFormulaTarget>) {
    match expr {
        Expr::CellRef(c) => targets.push(RawFormulaTarget::Coord(*c)),
        Expr::NamedRef(name) => targets.push(RawFormulaTarget::Name(name.clone())),
        Expr::RangeRef { top_left, bottom_right } => {
            targets.push(RawFormulaTarget::Range(*top_left, *bottom_right));
        }
        Expr::Unary { expr, .. } => collect_raw_formula_targets(expr, targets),
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
            collect_raw_formula_targets(left, targets);
            collect_raw_formula_targets(right, targets);
        }
        Expr::FunctionCall { args, .. } => {
            for arg in args {
                collect_raw_formula_targets(arg, targets);
            }
        }
        _ => {}
    }
}

fn document_ref_id(target: &str) -> &str {
    target.split_once('#').map_or(target, |(document_id, _)| document_id)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_document_ref_validation() {
        let doc = Document::new(
            "spec",
            "Specifications",
            "# Overview\nIntro.\n\n## Section One\nDetails.",
        );
        let wb = Workbook::new("w1", "Test").with_document(doc);

        assert_eq!(wb.validate_document_ref("spec"), DocumentRefStatus::Valid);
        assert_eq!(
            wb.validate_document_ref("spec#section-one"),
            DocumentRefStatus::ValidWithSection("Section One".into())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            wb.validate_document_ref("spec#Section One"),
            DocumentRefStatus::ValidWithSection("Section One".into())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            wb.validate_document_ref("spec#missing"),
            DocumentRefStatus::UnresolvedSection("missing".into())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            wb.validate_document_ref("unknown_doc#overview"),
            DocumentRefStatus::UnresolvedDocument
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_orphan_check_identifies_broken_refs() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        // A1 links to a non-existent document
        sheet.set_cell(
            Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 10.0)
                .with_name("rev")
                .with_document_refs(vec!["missing_doc".into(), "briefing#missing_section".into()]),
        );
        // A2 has formula referencing nonexistent cell Z99
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(0, 1), "=Z99 + 5"));

        // Document referencing nonexistent cell A50
        let doc = Document::new("briefing", "Briefing", "# Title\n{{cell:A50}}");
        // Unreferenced document
        let unref_doc = Document::new("notes", "Notes", "# Notes\nSome notes");

        let wb = Workbook::new("w1", "Test")
            .with_sheet(sheet)
            .with_document(doc)
            .with_document(unref_doc);

        let report = wb.check_orphans();
        assert_eq!(report.broken_document_refs.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(report.broken_document_refs[0].target, "missing_doc");

        assert_eq!(report.broken_section_refs.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(report.broken_section_refs[0].section_anchor, "missing_section");

        assert_eq!(report.broken_document_cell_refs.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(report.broken_document_cell_refs[0].target, "A50");

        assert_eq!(report.unreferenced_documents, vec!["notes"]);
        assert_eq!(report.dangling_formula_refs.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(report.dangling_formula_refs[0].target, "Z99");
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-core/src/ops/mod.rs
================================================================================
//! Spreadsheet operations on one sheet: ranges, insert/delete, fill, sort,
//! clipboard text, search and column fitting.
//!
//! Everything here is a pure function over [`Sheet`]: no window, no keyboard,
//! no OS. The GUI, the terminal view and a bare-metal shell all call the same
//! code, so an operation behaves identically wherever it is triggered.
//!
//! Operations that change cells finish by recalculating the sheet, so a
//! caller cannot forget to.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{collections::BTreeMap, format, string::String, vec::Vec};
#[cfg(feature = "std")]
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

use core::cmp::Ordering;

use crate::{
    calc::{
        offset_refs,
        refs::{MAX_COL, MAX_ROW},
        rewrite_refs, CalcEngine, RefKind,
    },
    model::{Cell, CellValue, Coord, Sheet},
};

/// Rows from here down hold binary/vector assets and never shift
/// (this is the same rule `Cell::is_asset` applies).
pub const ASSET_ROW_START: u32 = 10_000;
/// A collapsed column or row is the smallest size the sheet allows.
pub const COLLAPSED_COL_WIDTH: f32 = 10.0;
pub const COLLAPSED_ROW_HEIGHT: f32 = 8.0;
/// Pasting more cells than this at once is refused.
const MAX_PASTE_CELLS: usize = 1_000_000;

// ---- ranges -------------------------------------------------------------------

/// An inclusive rectangle of cells, always stored top-left to bottom-right.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Range {
    pub tl: Coord,
    pub br: Coord,
}

impl Range {
    /// The rectangle spanned by any two opposite corners.
    pub fn new(a: Coord, b: Coord) -> Self {
        Self {
            tl: Coord::new(a.col.min(b.col), a.row.min(b.row)),
            br: Coord::new(a.col.max(b.col), a.row.max(b.row)),
        }
    }

    pub fn cell(c: Coord) -> Self {
        Self { tl: c, br: c }
    }

    pub fn contains(&self, c: Coord) -> bool {
        c.col >= self.tl.col && c.col <= self.br.col && c.row >= self.tl.row && c.row <= self.br.row
    }

    pub fn cols(&self) -> u32 {
        self.br.col - self.tl.col + 1
    }

    pub fn rows(&self) -> u32 {
        self.br.row - self.tl.row + 1
    }

    pub fn is_single(&self) -> bool {
        self.tl == self.br
    }

    /// `A1:C5`, or just `A1` for one cell.
    pub fn to_a1(&self) -> String {
        if self.is_single() {
            self.tl.to_a1()
        } else {
            format!("{}:{}", self.tl.to_a1(), self.br.to_a1())
        }
    }
}

/// Everything in the sheet's active grid, from A1 to the furthest used cell.
pub fn used_range(sheet: &Sheet) -> Option<Range> {
    let (col, row) = sheet.active_grid_bounds()?;
    Some(Range::new(Coord::new(0, 0), Coord::new(col, row)))
}

// ---- setting values -----------------------------------------------------------

/// Put `value` at `dest`, keeping the cell's name and links if it already
/// exists (as typing does). `Empty` removes the cell.
fn set_value(sheet: &mut Sheet, dest: Coord, value: CellValue) {
    if value.is_empty() {
        sheet.cells.remove(&dest);
    } else if let Some(cell) = sheet.cells.get_mut(&dest) {
        cell.value = value;
    } else {
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::new(dest, value));
    }
}

/// A copy of `value` as if moved by (`d_col`, `d_row`): formulas have their
/// references offset and their cached results dropped.
fn shifted_value(value: &CellValue, d_col: i64, d_row: i64) -> CellValue {
    match value {
        CellValue::Formula { expr, .. } => CellValue::Formula {
            expr: offset_refs(expr, d_col, d_row),
            cached_num: None,
            cached_text: None,
            cached_bool: None,
        },
        other => other.clone(),
    }
}

fn recalc(sheet: &mut Sheet) {
    let _ = CalcEngine::recalculate_sheet(sheet, None);
}

/// Remove every cell in `range`.
pub fn clear_range(sheet: &mut Sheet, range: Range) {
    let doomed: Vec<Coord> = sheet.cells.keys().copied().filter(|c| range.contains(*c)).collect();
    for coord in doomed {
        sheet.cells.remove(&coord);
    }
    recalc(sheet);
}

// ---- insert / delete rows and columns ----------------------------------------

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Axis {
    Row,
    Col,
}

impl Axis {
    fn get(self, c: Coord) -> u32 {
        match self {
            Axis::Row => c.row,
            Axis::Col => c.col,
        }
    }

    fn with(self, c: Coord, v: u32) -> Coord {
        match self {
            Axis::Row => Coord::new(c.col, v),
            Axis::Col => Coord::new(v, c.row),
        }
    }

    /// First index that does not shift: the asset zone for rows, the sheet edge for columns.
    fn limit(self) -> u32 {
        match self {
            Axis::Row => ASSET_ROW_START,
            Axis::Col => MAX_COL + 1,
        }
    }

    fn name(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Axis::Row => "row",
            Axis::Col => "column",
        }
    }
}

/// Where index `v` ends up when `count` lines are inserted at `at`.
fn map_insert(v: u32, at: u32, count: u32, limit: u32) -> Option<u32> {
    if v < at || v >= limit {
        Some(v)
    } else {
        let nv = v + count;
        if nv >= limit { None } else { Some(nv) }
    }
}

/// Where index `v` ends up when `count` lines are deleted at `at`. A deleted
/// line is `None` for a lone cell; for a range, its start slides to the first
/// surviving line and its end back to the last one, so the range shrinks.
fn map_delete(v: u32, at: u32, count: u32, limit: u32, kind: RefKind) -> Option<u32> {
    if v < at || v >= limit {
        Some(v)
    } else if v >= at + count {
        Some(v - count)
    } else {
        match kind {
            RefKind::Cell => None,
            RefKind::RangeStart => Some(at),
            RefKind::RangeEnd => at.checked_sub(1),
        }
    }
}

fn shift_lines(sheet: &mut Sheet, axis: Axis, at: u32, count: u32, insert: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
    let limit = axis.limit();
    if count == 0 || at >= limit {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let count = if insert { count } else { count.min(limit - at) };

    if insert {
        let blocked = sheet.cells.keys().any(|c| {
            let v = axis.get(*c);
            v >= at && v < limit && v + count >= limit
        });
        if blocked {
            return Err(format!("Cannot insert: cells would be pushed past the end of the {}s", axis.name()));
        }
    }

    // Move the cells.
    let old = core::mem::take(&mut sheet.cells);
    for (coord, mut cell) in old {
        let v = axis.get(coord);
        let nv = if insert {
            map_insert(v, at, count, limit)
        } else {
            map_delete(v, at, count, limit, RefKind::Cell)
        };
        if let Some(nv) = nv {
            let moved = axis.with(coord, nv);
            cell.coord = moved;
            sheet.cells.insert(moved, cell);
        }
    }

    // Move the per-line sizes.
    let sizes = match axis {
        Axis::Row => &mut sheet.row_heights,
        Axis::Col => &mut sheet.col_widths,
    };
    let old_sizes = core::mem::take(sizes);
    let mut new_sizes: BTreeMap<u32, f32> = BTreeMap::new();
    for (v, size) in old_sizes {
        let nv = if insert {
            map_insert(v, at, count, limit)
        } else {
            map_delete(v, at, count, limit, RefKind::Cell)
        };
        if let Some(nv) = nv {
            new_sizes.insert(nv, size);
        }
    }
    *sizes = new_sizes;

    // Repair formulas.
    for cell in sheet.cells.values_mut() {
        if let CellValue::Formula { expr, cached_num, cached_text, cached_bool } = &mut cell.value {
            *expr = rewrite_refs(expr, &mut |c, kind| {
                let v = axis.get(c);
                let nv = if insert {
                    map_insert(v, at, count, limit)
                } else {
                    map_delete(v, at, count, limit, kind)
                };
                nv.map(|nv| axis.with(c, nv))
            });
            *cached_num = None;
            *cached_text = None;
            *cached_bool = None;
        }
    }

    recalc(sheet);
    Ok(())
}

/// Insert `count` empty rows or columns before index `at`.
pub fn insert_lines(sheet: &mut Sheet, axis: Axis, at: u32, count: u32) -> Result<(), String> {
    shift_lines(sheet, axis, at, count, true)
}

/// Delete `count` rows or columns starting at `at`. Formulas that pointed into
/// the deleted block become `#REF!`; ranges shrink.
pub fn delete_lines(sheet: &mut Sheet, axis: Axis, at: u32, count: u32) -> Result<(), String> {
    shift_lines(sheet, axis, at, count, false)
}

// ---- fill ---------------------------------------------------------------------

fn copy_value(sheet: &mut Sheet, src: Coord, dest: Coord) {
    let d_col = i64::from(dest.col) - i64::from(src.col);
    let d_row = i64::from(dest.row) - i64::from(src.row);
    let value = sheet
        .get_cell(src)
        .map(|c| shifted_value(&c.value, d_col, d_row))
        .unwrap_or(CellValue::Empty);
    set_value(sheet, dest, value);
}

/// Copy the top row of `range` down through it. With a single-row range, copy
/// the row above instead. Formulas keep their relative references.
pub fn fill_down(sheet: &mut Sheet, range: Range) -> Result<(), String> {
    let (src_row, first_dest) = if range.rows() == 1 {
        if range.tl.row == 0 {
            return Err(String::from("Nothing above to fill from"));
        }
        (range.tl.row - 1, range.tl.row)
    } else {
        (range.tl.row, range.tl.row + 1)
    };
    for col in range.tl.col..=range.br.col {
        for row in first_dest..=range.br.row {
            copy_value(sheet, Coord::new(col, src_row), Coord::new(col, row));
        }
    }
    recalc(sheet);
    Ok(())
}

/// Copy the left column of `range` right through it. With a single-column
/// range, copy the column to its left instead.
pub fn fill_right(sheet: &mut Sheet, range: Range) -> Result<(), String> {
    let (src_col, first_dest) = if range.cols() == 1 {
        if range.tl.col == 0 {
            return Err(String::from("Nothing to the left to fill from"));
        }
        (range.tl.col - 1, range.tl.col)
    } else {
        (range.tl.col, range.tl.col + 1)
    };
    for row in range.tl.row..=range.br.row {
        for col in first_dest..=range.br.col {
            copy_value(sheet, Coord::new(src_col, row), Coord::new(col, row));
        }
    }
    recalc(sheet);
    Ok(())
}

// ---- sort ---------------------------------------------------------------------

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
enum SortKey {
    Empty,
    Num(f64),
    Text(String),
}

fn sort_key(cell: Option<&Cell>) -> SortKey {
    match cell.map(|c| &c.value) {
        Some(CellValue::Number(n)) => SortKey::Num(*n),
        Some(CellValue::Boolean(b)) => SortKey::Num(if *b { 1.0 } else { 0.0 }),
        Some(CellValue::Text(t)) => SortKey::Text(t.to_ascii_lowercase()),
        Some(CellValue::Formula { cached_num, cached_bool, cached_text, .. }) => {
            if let Some(n) = cached_num {
                SortKey::Num(*n)
            } else if let Some(b) = cached_bool {
                SortKey::Num(if *b { 1.0 } else { 0.0 })
            } else if let Some(t) = cached_text {
                SortKey::Text(t.to_ascii_lowercase())
            } else {
                SortKey::Empty
            }
        }
        _ => SortKey::Empty,
    }
}

/// Empty cells always sort last; numbers come before text when ascending.
fn compare_keys(a: &SortKey, b: &SortKey, ascending: bool) -> Ordering {
    let order = |o: Ordering| if ascending { o } else { o.reverse() };
    match (a, b) {
        (SortKey::Empty, SortKey::Empty) => Ordering::Equal,
        (SortKey::Empty, _) => Ordering::Greater,
        (_, SortKey::Empty) => Ordering::Less,
        (SortKey::Num(x), SortKey::Num(y)) => order(x.partial_cmp(y).unwrap_or(Ordering::Equal)),
        (SortKey::Text(x), SortKey::Text(y)) => order(x.cmp(y)),
        (SortKey::Num(_), SortKey::Text(_)) => order(Ordering::Less),
        (SortKey::Text(_), SortKey::Num(_)) => order(Ordering::Greater),
    }
}

/// Sort the rows of `range` by the values in column `key_col` (which must be
/// inside the range). Rows move as a unit, with their names and links, and
/// each formula's relative references follow the row.
pub fn sort_rows(sheet: &mut Sheet, range: Range, key_col: u32, ascending: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
    if key_col < range.tl.col || key_col > range.br.col {
        return Err(String::from("The sort column is outside the selection"));
    }
    if range.rows() < 2 {
        return Err(String::from("Select at least two rows to sort"));
    }

    let mut rows: Vec<(u32, SortKey, Vec<Cell>)> = Vec::new();
    for row in range.tl.row..=range.br.row {
        let key = sort_key(sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(key_col, row)));
        let mut cells = Vec::new();
        for col in range.tl.col..=range.br.col {
            if let Some(cell) = sheet.cells.remove(&Coord::new(col, row)) {
                cells.push(cell);
            }
        }
        rows.push((row, key, cells));
    }

    rows.sort_by(|a, b| compare_keys(&a.1, &b.1, ascending));

    for (index, (old_row, _, cells)) in rows.into_iter().enumerate() {
        let new_row = range.tl.row + index as u32;
        let d_row = i64::from(new_row) - i64::from(old_row);
        for mut cell in cells {
            cell.value = shifted_value(&cell.value, 0, d_row);
            cell.coord = Coord::new(cell.coord.col, new_row);
            sheet.cells.insert(cell.coord, cell);
        }
    }

    recalc(sheet);
    Ok(())
}

// ---- clipboard text ---------------------------------------------------------

/// The text a cell puts on the clipboard, or `None` when it has nothing to copy.
pub fn cell_clipboard_text(value: &CellValue) -> Option<String> {
    let text = match value {
        CellValue::Number(n) => format!("{}", n),
        CellValue::Text(t) => t.clone(),
        CellValue::Formula { expr, .. } => expr.clone(),
        CellValue::Boolean(b) => String::from(if *b { "TRUE" } else { "FALSE" }),
        _ => String::new(),
    };
    if text.is_empty() { None } else { Some(text) }
}

/// A range as tab-separated rows, the plain-text form spreadsheets exchange.
pub fn range_to_text(sheet: &Sheet, range: Range) -> String {
    let mut out = String::new();
    for row in range.tl.row..=range.br.row {
        if row > range.tl.row {
            out.push('\n');
        }
        for col in range.tl.col..=range.br.col {
            if col > range.tl.col {
                out.push('\t');
            }
            if let Some(text) = sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(col, row)).and_then(|c| cell_clipboard_text(&c.value)) {
                // A tab or newline inside a cell would break the grid shape.
                out.extend(text.chars().map(|ch| if ch == '\t' || ch == '\n' || ch == '\r' { ' ' } else { ch }));
            }
        }
    }
    out
}

/// Paste tab-separated text with its top-left at `origin`. `shift` is the
/// distance the text was copied across, so pasted formulas keep their relative
/// references; pass `None` for text that came from outside.
pub fn paste_text(sheet: &mut Sheet, origin: Coord, text: &str, shift: Option<(i64, i64)>) -> Result<Range, String> {
    let mut lines: Vec<&str> = text.split('\n').map(|l| l.strip_suffix('\r').unwrap_or(l)).collect();
    if lines.len() > 1 && lines.last().map_or(false, |l| l.is_empty()) {
        lines.pop(); // spreadsheets end a copied block with a newline
    }
    let width = lines.iter().map(|l| l.split('\t').count()).max().unwrap_or(1);
    if lines.len().saturating_mul(width) > MAX_PASTE_CELLS {
        return Err(String::from("Too much to paste at once"));
    }

    let mut last = origin;
    for (r, line) in lines.iter().enumerate() {
        for (c, field) in line.split('\t').enumerate() {
            let col = u64::from(origin.col) + c as u64;
            let row = u64::from(origin.row) + r as u64;
            if col > u64::from(MAX_COL) || row > u64::from(MAX_ROW) {
                continue;
            }
            let dest = Coord::new(col as u32, row as u32);
            let mut value = CellValue::parse_input(field);
            if let (CellValue::Formula { .. }, Some((dc, dr))) = (&value, shift) {
                value = shifted_value(&value, dc, dr);
            }
            set_value(sheet, dest, value);
            last = Coord::new(last.col.max(dest.col), last.row.max(dest.row));
        }
    }
    recalc(sheet);
    Ok(Range::new(origin, last))
}

// ---- search and movement ----------------------------------------------------

fn cell_matches(cell: &Cell, needle_lower: &str) -> bool {
    if cell.value.display_text().to_ascii_lowercase().contains(needle_lower) {
        return true;
    }
    if let CellValue::Formula { expr, .. } = &cell.value {
        if expr.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(needle_lower) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    cell.name.as_deref().map_or(false, |n| n.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(needle_lower))
}

/// The next cell, reading across rows left to right, whose shown value,
/// formula or name contains `needle` (ignoring case). Wraps around the sheet.
pub fn find_next(sheet: &Sheet, from: Coord, needle: &str) -> Option<Coord> {
    if needle.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    let needle = needle.to_ascii_lowercase();
    let mut hits: Vec<(u32, u32)> = sheet
        .cells
        .values()
        .filter(|c| !c.is_asset() && cell_matches(c, &needle))
        .map(|c| (c.coord.row, c.coord.col))
        .collect();
    hits.sort_unstable();
    let after = hits.iter().find(|h| **h > (from.row, from.col)).or_else(|| hits.first())?;
    Some(Coord::new(after.1, after.0))
}

/// Ctrl+Arrow: from `from`, step by (`d_col`, `d_row`) to the edge of the
/// current block of data, or to the next filled cell, or to the sheet edge.
pub fn jump_edge(sheet: &Sheet, from: Coord, d_col: i32, d_row: i32) -> Coord {
    let filled = |c: Coord| sheet.get_cell(c).map_or(false, |x| !x.value.is_empty());
    let step = |c: Coord| -> Option<Coord> {
        let col = i64::from(c.col) + i64::from(d_col);
        let row = i64::from(c.row) + i64::from(d_row);
        if (0..=i64::from(MAX_COL)).contains(&col) && (0..i64::from(ASSET_ROW_START)).contains(&row) {
            Some(Coord::new(col as u32, row as u32))
        } else {
            None
        }
    };

    let Some(mut cur) = step(from) else { return from };
    if filled(from) && filled(cur) {
        while let Some(next) = step(cur) {
            if filled(next) {
                cur = next;
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
    } else {
        while !filled(cur) {
            match step(cur) {
                Some(next) => cur = next,
                None => break,
            }
        }
    }
    cur
}

// ---- column and row sizes ---------------------------------------------------

/// The width that fits column `col`'s widest value, given a text-width
/// measure. Empty columns get the sheet's default width.
pub fn fit_col_width(sheet: &Sheet, col: u32, measure: &dyn Fn(&str) -> f32) -> f32 {
    let mut widest = 0.0_f32;
    for cell in sheet.cells.values() {
        if cell.coord.col == col && !cell.is_asset() {
            let w = measure(&cell.value.display_text());
            if w > widest {
                widest = w;
            }
        }
    }
    if widest <= 0.0 {
        sheet.default_col_width
    } else {
        (widest + 14.0).clamp(40.0, 600.0)
    }
}

pub fn is_col_collapsed(sheet: &Sheet, col: u32) -> bool {
    sheet.col_width(col) <= COLLAPSED_COL_WIDTH
}

pub fn is_row_collapsed(sheet: &Sheet, row: u32) -> bool {
    sheet.row_height(row) <= COLLAPSED_ROW_HEIGHT
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests;

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-core/src/ops/tests.rs

use super::*;

fn c(col: u32, row: u32) -> Coord {
    Coord::new(col, row)
}

fn sheet_with(cells: &[(Coord, CellValue)]) -> Sheet {
    let mut sheet = Sheet::new("s", "S");
    for (coord, value) in cells {
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::new(*coord, value.clone()));
    }
    recalc(&mut sheet);
    sheet
}

fn num(n: f64) -> CellValue {
    CellValue::Number(n)
}

fn formula(expr: &str) -> CellValue {
    CellValue::parse_input(expr)
}

fn expr_at(sheet: &Sheet, at: Coord) -> String {
    match &sheet.get_cell(at).expect("cell present").value {
        CellValue::Formula { expr, .. } => expr.clone(),
        other => panic!("not a formula: {other:?}"),
    }
}

fn result_at(sheet: &Sheet, at: Coord) -> Option<f64> {
    match &sheet.get_cell(at)?.value {
        CellValue::Formula { cached_num, .. } => *cached_num,
        CellValue::Number(n) => Some(*n),
        _ => None,
    }
}

// ---- ranges ---------------------------------------------------------------

#[test]
fn range_normalizes_corners_and_describes_itself() {
    let r = Range::new(c(3, 5), c(1, 2));
    assert_eq!((r.tl, r.br), (c(1, 2), c(3, 5)));
    assert_eq!((r.cols(), r.rows()), (3, 4));
    assert!(r.contains(c(2, 3)) && !r.contains(c(0, 3)) && !r.contains(c(2, 6)));
    assert_eq!(r.to_a1(), "B3:D6");
    assert_eq!(Range::cell(c(0, 0)).to_a1(), "A1");
    assert!(Range::cell(c(4, 4)).is_single());
}

#[test]
fn used_range_ignores_the_asset_zone() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[(c(2, 4), num(1.0))]);
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::new(c(0, 20_000), CellValue::Blob { bytes: vec![1, 2, 3] }));
    assert_eq!(used_range(&sheet), Some(Range::new(c(0, 0), c(2, 4))));
    assert_eq!(used_range(&Sheet::new("e", "E")), None);
}

#[test]
fn clear_range_removes_only_cells_inside() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[(c(0, 0), num(1.0)), (c(1, 1), num(2.0)), (c(3, 3), num(3.0))]);
    clear_range(&mut sheet, Range::new(c(0, 0), c(1, 1)));
    assert_eq!(sheet.cells.len(), 1);
    assert!(sheet.get_cell(c(3, 3)).is_some());
}

// ---- insert / delete --------------------------------------------------------

#[test]
fn inserting_a_row_shifts_cells_and_repairs_formulas() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[
        (c(0, 0), num(1.0)),
        (c(0, 1), num(2.0)),
        (c(0, 2), formula("=A1+A2")),
    ]);
    insert_lines(&mut sheet, Axis::Row, 1, 1).unwrap();

    assert!(result_at(&sheet, c(0, 0)) == Some(1.0));
    assert!(sheet.get_cell(c(0, 1)).is_none(), "the new row is empty");
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(0, 2)), Some(2.0));
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&sheet, c(0, 3)), "=A1+A3");
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(0, 3)), Some(3.0), "recalculated after the move");
    assert_eq!(sheet.get_cell(c(0, 3)).unwrap().coord, c(0, 3), "cell knows its new place");
}

#[test]
fn inserting_inside_a_range_grows_it() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[
        (c(0, 0), num(1.0)),
        (c(0, 1), num(2.0)),
        (c(0, 2), num(3.0)),
        (c(0, 3), formula("=SUM(A1:A3)")),
    ]);
    insert_lines(&mut sheet, Axis::Row, 1, 1).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&sheet, c(0, 4)), "=SUM(A1:A4)");
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(0, 4)), Some(6.0));
}

#[test]
fn deleting_a_row_shrinks_ranges_and_breaks_lone_references() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[
        (c(0, 0), num(1.0)),
        (c(0, 1), num(2.0)),
        (c(0, 2), num(3.0)),
        (c(0, 3), formula("=SUM(A1:A3)")),
        (c(2, 0), formula("=A2")),
    ]);
    delete_lines(&mut sheet, Axis::Row, 1, 1).unwrap();

    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(0, 1)), Some(3.0), "A3 moved up to A2");
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&sheet, c(0, 2)), "=SUM(A1:A2)");
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(0, 2)), Some(4.0));
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&sheet, c(2, 0)), "=#REF!", "the cell it pointed at is gone");
}

#[test]
fn deleting_the_end_of_a_range_pulls_it_back() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[
        (c(0, 0), num(1.0)),
        (c(0, 1), num(2.0)),
        (c(0, 2), num(3.0)),
        (c(2, 0), formula("=SUM(A1:A3)")),
    ]);
    delete_lines(&mut sheet, Axis::Row, 2, 1).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&sheet, c(2, 0)), "=SUM(A1:A2)");
}

#[test]
fn deleting_everything_a_range_covered_gives_ref_error() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[
        (c(0, 1), num(1.0)),
        (c(0, 2), num(2.0)),
        (c(2, 0), formula("=SUM(A2:A3)")),
    ]);
    delete_lines(&mut sheet, Axis::Row, 1, 2).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&sheet, c(2, 0)), "=SUM(#REF!)");
}

#[test]
fn names_travel_with_their_cells() {
    let mut sheet = Sheet::new("s", "S");
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(c(0, 3), 7.0).with_name("rate"));
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(c(1, 0), "=rate*2"));
    recalc(&mut sheet);
    insert_lines(&mut sheet, Axis::Row, 0, 2).unwrap();
    let moved = sheet.get_cell(c(0, 5)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(moved.name.as_deref(), Some("rate"));
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&sheet, c(1, 2)), "=rate*2");
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(1, 2)), Some(14.0));
}

#[test]
fn row_heights_follow_inserts_and_deletes() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[(c(0, 5), num(1.0))]);
    sheet.set_row_height(5, 40.0);
    insert_lines(&mut sheet, Axis::Row, 2, 3).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(sheet.row_height(8), 40.0);
    assert_eq!(sheet.row_height(5), sheet.default_row_height);
    delete_lines(&mut sheet, Axis::Row, 8, 1).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(sheet.row_heights.len(), 0, "the deleted row's height is gone");
}

#[test]
fn columns_work_the_same_way() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[
        (c(0, 0), num(1.0)),
        (c(1, 0), num(2.0)),
        (c(2, 0), formula("=A1+B1")),
    ]);
    sheet.set_col_width(1, 150.0);
    insert_lines(&mut sheet, Axis::Col, 1, 1).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&sheet, c(3, 0)), "=A1+C1");
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(3, 0)), Some(3.0));
    assert_eq!(sheet.col_width(2), 150.0);

    delete_lines(&mut sheet, Axis::Col, 1, 1).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&sheet, c(2, 0)), "=A1+B1");
    assert_eq!(sheet.col_width(1), 150.0);
}

#[test]
fn the_asset_zone_never_shifts() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[(c(0, 0), num(1.0))]);
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::new(c(0, 10_000), CellValue::Blob { bytes: vec![9] }));
    insert_lines(&mut sheet, Axis::Row, 0, 5).unwrap();
    assert!(sheet.get_cell(c(0, 10_000)).is_some());
    assert!(sheet.get_cell(c(0, 5)).is_some());
    delete_lines(&mut sheet, Axis::Row, 0, 5).unwrap();
    assert!(sheet.get_cell(c(0, 10_000)).is_some());
}

#[test]
fn an_insert_that_would_push_cells_into_the_asset_zone_is_refused_untouched() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[(c(0, 0), num(1.0)), (c(0, 9_999), num(2.0))]);
    let before = sheet.clone();
    let err = insert_lines(&mut sheet, Axis::Row, 0, 1).unwrap_err();
    assert!(err.contains("pushed past"), "{err}");
    assert_eq!(sheet, before);
}

// ---- fill -------------------------------------------------------------------

#[test]
fn fill_down_copies_the_top_row_with_relative_formulas() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[(c(0, 0), num(5.0)), (c(1, 0), formula("=A1*2"))]);
    fill_down(&mut sheet, Range::new(c(0, 0), c(1, 3))).unwrap();
    for row in 1..=3 {
        assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(0, row)), Some(5.0));
        assert_eq!(expr_at(&sheet, c(1, row)), format!("=A{}*2", row + 1));
        assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(1, row)), Some(10.0));
    }
}

#[test]
fn fill_down_on_one_row_copies_the_row_above() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[(c(1, 0), num(3.0)), (c(1, 1), num(99.0))]);
    fill_down(&mut sheet, Range::cell(c(1, 1))).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(1, 1)), Some(3.0));
    assert!(fill_down(&mut sheet, Range::cell(c(1, 0))).is_err(), "nothing above row 1");
}

#[test]
fn fill_right_copies_the_left_column() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[(c(0, 0), num(2.0)), (c(0, 1), formula("=A1+1"))]);
    fill_right(&mut sheet, Range::new(c(0, 0), c(3, 1))).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(3, 0)), Some(2.0));
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&sheet, c(2, 1)), "=C1+1");
    assert!(fill_right(&mut sheet, Range::cell(c(0, 0))).is_err());
}

#[test]
fn filling_from_an_empty_cell_clears_the_destination() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[(c(0, 1), num(7.0))]);
    fill_down(&mut sheet, Range::new(c(0, 0), c(0, 1))).unwrap();
    assert!(sheet.get_cell(c(0, 1)).is_none());
}

// ---- sort -------------------------------------------------------------------

#[test]
fn sorting_moves_rows_and_keeps_same_row_formulas_pointing_home() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[
        (c(0, 0), num(3.0)),
        (c(0, 1), num(1.0)),
        (c(0, 2), num(2.0)),
        (c(1, 0), formula("=A1*10")),
        (c(1, 1), formula("=A2*10")),
        (c(1, 2), formula("=A3*10")),
    ]);
    sort_rows(&mut sheet, Range::new(c(0, 0), c(1, 2)), 0, true).unwrap();
    let a: Vec<_> = (0..3).map(|r| result_at(&sheet, c(0, r)).unwrap()).collect();
    let b: Vec<_> = (0..3).map(|r| result_at(&sheet, c(1, r)).unwrap()).collect();
    assert_eq!(a, vec![1.0, 2.0, 3.0]);
    assert_eq!(b, vec![10.0, 20.0, 30.0]);
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&sheet, c(1, 0)), "=A1*10");

    sort_rows(&mut sheet, Range::new(c(0, 0), c(1, 2)), 0, false).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(0, 0)), Some(3.0));
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(1, 0)), Some(30.0));
}

#[test]
fn sort_puts_numbers_before_text_and_empties_last_either_way() {
    let build = || {
        sheet_with(&[
            (c(0, 0), CellValue::Text("pear".into())),
            (c(0, 2), num(2.0)),
            (c(0, 3), CellValue::Text("Apple".into())),
            (c(0, 4), num(1.0)),
        ])
    };
    let col = |s: &Sheet| -> Vec<String> {
        (0..5).map(|r| s.get_cell(c(0, r)).map(|x| x.value.display_text()).unwrap_or_default()).collect()
    };
    let mut asc = build();
    sort_rows(&mut asc, Range::new(c(0, 0), c(0, 4)), 0, true).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(col(&asc), vec!["1.00", "2.00", "Apple", "pear", ""]);

    let mut desc = build();
    sort_rows(&mut desc, Range::new(c(0, 0), c(0, 4)), 0, false).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(col(&desc), vec!["pear", "Apple", "2.00", "1.00", ""]);
}

#[test]
fn sort_carries_names_with_their_rows_and_checks_its_input() {
    let mut sheet = Sheet::new("s", "S");
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(c(0, 0), 9.0).with_name("big"));
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(c(0, 1), 1.0).with_name("small"));
    sort_rows(&mut sheet, Range::new(c(0, 0), c(0, 1)), 0, true).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(sheet.get_cell(c(0, 0)).unwrap().name.as_deref(), Some("small"));

    assert!(sort_rows(&mut sheet, Range::cell(c(0, 0)), 0, true).is_err(), "one row");
    assert!(sort_rows(&mut sheet, Range::new(c(0, 0), c(0, 1)), 5, true).is_err(), "key outside");
}

#[test]
fn range_text_is_tab_separated_rows() {
    let sheet = sheet_with(&[
        (c(0, 0), num(1.0)),
        (c(1, 0), CellValue::Text("a\tb".into())),
        (c(1, 1), formula("=A1+1")),
    ]);
    assert_eq!(range_to_text(&sheet, Range::new(c(0, 0), c(1, 1))), "1\ta b\n\t=A1+1");
}

#[test]
fn pasting_external_text_fills_a_block() {
    let mut sheet = Sheet::new("s", "S");
    let r = paste_text(&mut sheet, c(1, 1), "1\t2\r\n3\ttext\r\n", None).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(r, Range::new(c(1, 1), c(2, 2)));
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(2, 1)), Some(2.0));
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(1, 2)), Some(3.0));
    assert_eq!(sheet.get_cell(c(2, 2)).unwrap().value, CellValue::Text("text".into()));
    assert_eq!(sheet.cells.len(), 4, "the trailing newline adds no row");
}

#[test]
fn pasting_a_single_value_and_a_blank_overwrites() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[(c(0, 0), num(1.0)), (c(1, 0), num(2.0))]);
    paste_text(&mut sheet, c(0, 0), "42", None).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(0, 0)), Some(42.0));
    paste_text(&mut sheet, c(0, 0), "\t", None).unwrap();
    assert!(sheet.get_cell(c(0, 0)).is_none() && sheet.get_cell(c(1, 0)).is_none());
}

#[test]
fn pasting_what_was_copied_offsets_relative_references() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[(c(0, 0), num(4.0)), (c(1, 0), formula("=A1*3"))]);
    let text = range_to_text(&sheet, Range::new(c(0, 0), c(1, 0)));
    // paste three rows down and one column right: shift is (+1, +3)
    paste_text(&mut sheet, c(1, 3), &text, Some((1, 3))).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(1, 3)), Some(4.0));
    assert_eq!(expr_at(&sheet, c(2, 3)), "=B4*3");
    assert_eq!(result_at(&sheet, c(2, 3)), Some(12.0));
}

#[test]
fn pasting_keeps_the_name_of_the_cell_it_lands_on() {
    let mut sheet = Sheet::new("s", "S");
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(c(0, 0), 1.0).with_name("rate"));
    paste_text(&mut sheet, c(0, 0), "5", None).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(sheet.get_cell(c(0, 0)).unwrap().name.as_deref(), Some("rate"));
}

#[test]
fn a_paste_running_off_the_sheet_is_clipped() {
    let mut sheet = Sheet::new("s", "S");
    let r = paste_text(&mut sheet, c(16_383, 0), "1\t2", None).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(sheet.cells.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(r.br, c(16_383, 0));
}

// ---- search and movement ----------------------------------------------------

#[test]
fn find_next_reads_across_rows_ignores_case_and_wraps() {
    let mut sheet = sheet_with(&[
        (c(0, 0), CellValue::Text("Total".into())),
        (c(2, 0), CellValue::Text("subtotal".into())),
        (c(1, 2), CellValue::Text("TOTALS".into())),
        (c(0, 1), formula("=SUM(B1:B2)")),
    ]);
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(c(3, 3), 1.0).with_name("grand_total"));

    assert_eq!(find_next(&sheet, c(0, 0), "total"), Some(c(2, 0)));
    assert_eq!(find_next(&sheet, c(2, 0), "total"), Some(c(1, 2)));
    assert_eq!(find_next(&sheet, c(1, 2), "total"), Some(c(3, 3)), "matches names too");
    assert_eq!(find_next(&sheet, c(3, 3), "total"), Some(c(0, 0)), "wraps around");
    assert_eq!(find_next(&sheet, c(0, 0), "sum("), Some(c(0, 1)), "matches formula text");
    assert_eq!(find_next(&sheet, c(0, 0), "zzz"), None);
    assert_eq!(find_next(&sheet, c(0, 0), ""), None);
}

#[test]
fn jump_edge_follows_blocks_like_a_spreadsheet() {
    let sheet = sheet_with(&[
        (c(0, 0), num(1.0)),
        (c(0, 1), num(1.0)),
        (c(0, 2), num(1.0)),
        (c(0, 5), num(1.0)),
    ]);
    assert_eq!(jump_edge(&sheet, c(0, 0), 0, 1), c(0, 2), "end of the block");
    assert_eq!(jump_edge(&sheet, c(0, 2), 0, 1), c(0, 5), "next filled cell");
    assert_eq!(jump_edge(&sheet, c(0, 5), 0, 1), c(0, 9_999), "sheet edge, never the asset zone");
    assert_eq!(jump_edge(&sheet, c(0, 5), 0, -1), c(0, 2));
    assert_eq!(jump_edge(&sheet, c(0, 0), -1, 0), c(0, 0), "already at the left edge");
    assert_eq!(jump_edge(&sheet, c(0, 0), 1, 0), c(16_383, 0), "empty row runs to the edge");
}

// ---- sizes ------------------------------------------------------------------

#[test]
fn fit_width_tracks_the_widest_value() {
    let sheet = sheet_with(&[
        (c(0, 0), CellValue::Text("hi".into())),
        (c(0, 1), CellValue::Text("a much longer label".into())),
        (c(1, 0), num(1.0)),
    ]);
    let measure = |s: &str| s.len() as f32 * 6.0;
    assert_eq!(fit_col_width(&sheet, 0, &measure), 19.0 * 6.0 + 14.0);
    assert_eq!(fit_col_width(&sheet, 5, &measure), sheet.default_col_width, "empty column");
    assert_eq!(fit_col_width(&sheet, 1, &|_| 4.0), 40.0, "never below the minimum");
    assert_eq!(fit_col_width(&sheet, 0, &|_| 5_000.0), 600.0, "never above the maximum");
}

#[test]
fn collapsed_means_the_smallest_size() {
    let mut sheet = Sheet::new("s", "S");
    assert!(!is_col_collapsed(&sheet, 0) && !is_row_collapsed(&sheet, 0));
    sheet.set_col_width(0, 1.0);
    sheet.set_row_height(0, 1.0);
    assert!(is_col_collapsed(&sheet, 0) && is_row_collapsed(&sheet, 0));
    assert_eq!(sheet.col_width(0), COLLAPSED_COL_WIDTH);
    assert_eq!(sheet.row_height(0), COLLAPSED_ROW_HEIGHT);
}

FILE: lotus-core/src/storage/mod.rs

pub mod reader;
pub mod writer;

pub use reader::{deserialize_workbook, ParseError};
pub use writer::serialize_workbook;

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::model::{Cell, CellValue, Coord, Sheet, Workbook};

    #[test]
    fn test_full_workbook_roundtrip() {
        let mut wb = Workbook::new("test-wb", "Lotus Test Workspace");
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");

        // Cell A1: Number 100.5
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 100.5).with_name("input_a"));

        // Cell B1: Number 200.0
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(1, 0), 200.0).with_name("input_b"));

        // Cell C1: Formula =A1+B1
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(2, 0), "=A1+B1").with_name("result_sum"));

        // Cell A2: Embedded BASIC procedure
        let basic_code = "SUB Calculate()\n  LET a = GET_CELL(\"A1\")\n  SET_CELL(\"B1\", a * 2)\nEND SUB";
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::basic(Coord::new(0, 1), "sim.bas", basic_code));

        // Cell B2: Named Raw Binary Payload (contains null bytes and high bytes)
        let binary_payload: Vec<u8> = vec![0x00, 0xFF, 0x55, 0xAA, 0xDE, 0xAD, 0xBE, 0xEF, 0x00, 0x01];
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::blob(Coord::new(1, 1), "kernel.bin", binary_payload.clone()));

        // Cell C26: Furthest cell in extent (Col C is 2, Row 26 is 25)
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::text(Coord::new(2, 25), "Extent Cell"));

        wb.sheets.push(sheet);

        // 1. Verify dynamic TOC calculation before serialization
        // Furthest col is 2 (C), furthest row is 25 (26)
        // TOC must be at col 3 (D), row 26 (27) -> "D27"
        let toc = wb.compute_toc();
        assert_eq!(toc.pos, Coord::new(3, 26));
        assert_eq!(toc.pos.to_a1(), "D27");

        // 2. Serialize to bytes
        let serialized = serialize_workbook(&wb);

        // Verify that the serialized format contains human-readable XML framing
        let serialized_str = String::from_utf8_lossy(&serialized);
        assert!(serialized_str.contains("<workbook id=\"test-wb\""));
        assert!(serialized_str.contains("<toc ref=\"D27\""));
        assert!(serialized_str.contains("name=\"sim.bas\""));
        assert!(serialized_str.contains("name=\"kernel.bin\""));
        assert!(serialized_str.contains("type=\"blob\" bytes=\"10\""));

        // 3. Deserialize back
        let restored = deserialize_workbook(&serialized).expect("Deserialization failed");

        assert_eq!(restored.id, "test-wb");
        assert_eq!(restored.title, "Lotus Test Workspace");
        assert_eq!(restored.sheets.len(), 1);

        let restored_sheet = &restored.sheets[0];
        assert_eq!(restored_sheet.cells.len(), 6);

        // Check Number cell
        let a1 = restored_sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(a1.name.as_deref(), Some("input_a"));
        assert_eq!(a1.value, CellValue::Number(100.5));

        // Check Formula cell
        let c1 = restored_sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(2, 0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(c1.name.as_deref(), Some("result_sum"));
        match &c1.value {
            CellValue::Formula { expr, .. } => assert_eq!(expr, "=A1+B1"),
            _ => panic!("Expected formula"),
        }

        // Check BASIC cell
        let a2 = restored_sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 1)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(a2.name.as_deref(), Some("sim.bas"));
        match &a2.value {
            CellValue::Basic { source } => assert_eq!(source, basic_code),
            _ => panic!("Expected basic"),
        }

        // Check Binary Blob cell (byte-for-byte exactness)
        let b2 = restored_sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(1, 1)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(b2.name.as_deref(), Some("kernel.bin"));
        match &b2.value {
            CellValue::Blob { bytes } => assert_eq!(bytes, &binary_payload),
            _ => panic!("Expected blob"),
        }

        // 4. Test Cell-as-a-File Ejection
        let ejected = restored.eject_cell("kernel.bin").expect("Ejection failed");
        assert_eq!(ejected, binary_payload.as_slice());

        // Test BASIC source ejection
        let ejected_basic = restored.eject_cell("sim.bas").expect("Eject basic failed");
        assert_eq!(ejected_basic, basic_code.as_bytes());

        // 5. Test Dynamic TOC on restored workbook
        let restored_toc = restored.compute_toc();
        assert_eq!(restored_toc.pos.to_a1(), "D27");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_embedded_document_roundtrip() {
        use crate::doc::Document;

        let mut wb = Workbook::new("doc-wb", "Document Workspace");
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 42.0)
            .with_name("answer")
            .with_document_refs(vec!["analysis-01#summary".into()]));
        wb.sheets.push(sheet);

        let doc_body = "# Analysis\nThe calculated answer is {{cell:answer}}.";
        wb = wb.with_document(Document::new("analysis-01", "Analysis Report", doc_body));
        wb.record_history_layer("main", vec![(Coord::new(0, 0), Some(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 44.0).with_name("answer")))]).unwrap();

        let serialized = serialize_workbook(&wb);
        let restored = deserialize_workbook(&serialized).expect("Deserialization failed");

        assert_eq!(restored.documents.len(), 1);
        let doc = &restored.documents[0];
        assert_eq!(doc.id, "analysis-01");
        assert_eq!(doc.title, "Analysis Report");
        assert_eq!(doc.body, doc_body);
        assert_eq!(restored.primary_sheet().unwrap().get_cell(Coord::new(0, 0)).unwrap().document_refs, vec!["analysis-01#summary"]);
        assert_eq!(restored.documents_linked_from_cell("main", Coord::new(0, 0)).iter().map(|doc| doc.id.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec!["analysis-01"]);
        assert_eq!(restored.documents_referencing_cell("main", Coord::new(0, 0)).iter().map(|doc| doc.id.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec!["analysis-01"]);
        assert!(restored.documents_linked_from_cell_at_z("main", Coord::new(0, 0), 1).is_empty());
        assert_eq!(restored.documents_referencing_cell_at_z("main", Coord::new(0, 0), 1).len(), 1);

        // Test live rendering
        let rendered = doc.render_live(&restored.sheets[0], Some(&restored));
        assert_eq!(rendered, "# Analysis\nThe calculated answer is 42.00.");

        // Test document ejection
        let ejected = restored.eject_cell("analysis-01").expect("Document ejection failed");
        assert_eq!(ejected, doc_body.as_bytes());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_z_layer_roundtrip_and_composition() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 100.0));
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::text(Coord::new(1, 0), "keep in base"));
        let mut wb = Workbook::new("history-wb", "History Workspace").with_sheet(sheet);

        let z1 = wb.record_history_layer("main", vec![
            (Coord::new(0, 0), Some(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 900.0)
                .with_document_refs(vec!["analysis#results".into()]))),
            (Coord::new(1, 0), None),
            (Coord::new(2, 0), Some(Cell::blob(Coord::new(2, 0), "payload", b"x</cell>\0\xff".to_vec()))),
        ]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(z1, 1);
        wb.record_history_layer("main", vec![
            (Coord::new(0, 0), Some(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 1200.0))),
        ]).unwrap();

        let encoded = serialize_workbook(&wb);
        let restored = deserialize_workbook(&encoded).expect("History workbook should parse");
        assert_eq!(restored, wb);

        // The base remains unchanged; historical layers compose only when selected.
        assert_eq!(restored.get_sheet("main").unwrap().get_cell(Coord::new(0, 0)).unwrap().value, CellValue::Number(100.0));
        assert_eq!(restored.sheet_at_z("main", 0).unwrap().get_cell(Coord::new(0, 0)).unwrap().value, CellValue::Number(100.0));

        let at_z1 = restored.sheet_at_z("main", 1).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(at_z1.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 0)).unwrap().value, CellValue::Number(900.0));
        assert_eq!(at_z1.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 0)).unwrap().document_refs, vec!["analysis#results"]);
        assert!(at_z1.get_cell(Coord::new(1, 0)).is_none());
        assert_eq!(at_z1.get_cell(Coord::new(2, 0)).unwrap().value, CellValue::Blob { bytes: b"x</cell>\0\xff".to_vec() });

        let at_z2 = restored.sheet_at_z("main", 2).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(at_z2.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 0)).unwrap().value, CellValue::Number(1200.0));
        assert!(restored.sheet_at_z("main", 3).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_invalid_z_layer_order_is_rejected() {
        let workbook = b"<workbook id=\"w\" title=\"w\"><sheet id=\"main\" name=\"Main\"></sheet><history><layer sheet=\"main\" z=\"2\"><cell ref=\"A1\" type=\"number\">1</cell></layer></history></workbook>";
        assert!(matches!(deserialize_workbook(workbook), Err(ParseError::InvalidHistory(_))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn appended_history_trailer_accepts_a_batch_of_consecutive_layers() {
        let sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        let base = Workbook::new("batch-wb", "Batch Updates").with_sheet(sheet);
        let mut bytes = serialize_workbook(&base);
        bytes.extend_from_slice(b"\n<history>\n");
        for z in 1..=12 {
            bytes.extend_from_slice(
                format!(
                    "  <layer sheet=\"main\" z=\"{}\"><cell ref=\"A1\" type=\"number\">{}</cell></layer>\n",
                    z, z
                )
                .as_bytes(),
            );
        }
        bytes.extend_from_slice(b"</history>\n<cursor sheet=\"main\" x=\"0\" y=\"0\" z=\"12\" />\n");

        let restored = deserialize_workbook(&bytes).expect("Appended update batch should parse");
        assert_eq!(restored.history_for_sheet("main").unwrap().max_z, 12);
        assert_eq!(restored.get_sheet("main").unwrap().cells.len(), 0);
        assert_eq!(
            restored.sheet_at_z("main", 12).unwrap().get_cell(Coord::new(0, 0)).unwrap().value,
            CellValue::Number(12.0)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn incomplete_appended_history_batch_is_rejected() {
        let base = Workbook::new("batch-wb", "Batch Updates").with_sheet(Sheet::new("main", "Main"));
        let mut bytes = serialize_workbook(&base);
        bytes.extend_from_slice(b"\n<history><layer sheet=\"main\" z=\"1\"><cell ref=\"A1\" type=\"number\">42</cell></layer>");
        assert!(matches!(deserialize_workbook(&bytes), Err(ParseError::UnexpectedEof)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn formula_boolean_cache_and_basic_grants_roundtrip() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 3.0));
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(1, 0), "=A1>5"));
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(2, 0), "=IF(B1,\"big\",\"small\")"));
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::basic(Coord::new(0, 1), "script", "LET x = 1")
            .with_basic_grants(vec!["A1".into(), "tax_rate".into()], vec!["B2".into()]));
        crate::calc::CalcEngine::recalculate_sheet(&mut sheet, None).unwrap();
        let wb = Workbook::new("w", "w").with_sheet(sheet);
        let restored = deserialize_workbook(&serialize_workbook(&wb)).unwrap();
        let restored_sheet = restored.primary_sheet().unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(restored_sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(1, 0)).unwrap().value, CellValue::Formula { cached_bool: Some(false), .. }));
        assert!(matches!(&restored_sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(2, 0)).unwrap().value, CellValue::Formula { cached_text: Some(v), .. } if v == "small"));
        let basic = restored_sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 1)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(basic.basic_reads, vec!["A1", "tax_rate"]);
        assert_eq!(basic.basic_writes, vec!["B2"]);
    }
}
