//! Deserialization of Lotus 5-7-11 workbooks from the uncompressed hybrid format.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{string::String, string::ToString, vec::Vec};

use crate::model::{
    zlayer::{ZLayer, Manifest},

    cell::{Cell, CellValue},
    coord::Coord,
    sheet::Sheet,
    workbook::Workbook,
};

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum ParseError {
    UnexpectedEof,
    InvalidTag(usize),
    MalformedAttribute(usize),
    MissingAttribute(&'static str),
    InvalidHistory(String),
    InvalidCoordinate(String),
    InvalidNumber(String),
    InvalidFormulaCache(String),
    BlobOverflow { expected: usize, available: usize },
}

/// Process pending z-layers and update workbook manifest during reload. This is where the AI's Z-layer updates are detected and staged for review.
pub fn process_pending_zlayers(workbook: &mut Workbook) {
    // In a real implementation this would:
    // 1. Check if new z-layers have been appended to file
    // 2. Parse them into proper structures 
    // 3. Add entries to the manifest for user review
    // 4. Mark them as pending status in the manifest
    
    // For now, just demonstrate the interface structure
}

pub fn deserialize_workbook(input: &[u8]) -> Result<Workbook, ParseError> {
    let mut scanner = Scanner::new(input);
    let mut workbook = Workbook::new("wb", "Lotus Workbook");
    let mut current_sheet: Option<Sheet> = None;
    let mut current_layer: Option<(String, u32, Vec<(Coord, Option<Cell>)>)> = None;
    let mut open_history_containers = 0usize;

    while !scanner.is_eof() {
        scanner.skip_whitespace_and_comments();
        if scanner.is_eof() {
            break;
        }

        if scanner.peek() == Some(b'<') {
            let tag = scanner.parse_tag_header()?;

            match tag.name {
                "workbook" => {
                    if !tag.is_closing {
                        if let Some(id) = tag.get_attr_unescaped("id") {
                            workbook.id = id;
                        }
                        if let Some(title) = tag.get_attr_unescaped("title") {
                            workbook.title = title;
                        }
                        if let Some(calc) = tag.get_attr("calculation") {
                            workbook.calc_mode = calc.to_string();
                        }
                    }
                }
                "toc" => {
                    // Skip over TOC contents during read, as TOC is calculated dynamically on demand
                    if !tag.is_self_closing && !tag.is_closing {
                        scanner.skip_until_closing_tag("toc")?;
                    }
                }
                "sheet" => {
                    if tag.is_closing {
                        if let Some(sheet) = current_sheet.take() {
                            workbook.sheets.push(sheet);
                        }
                    } else {
                        let id = tag.get_attr_unescaped("id").unwrap_or_else(|| "sheet".to_string());
                        let name = tag.get_attr_unescaped("name").unwrap_or_else(|| "Sheet".to_string());
                        let mut sheet = Sheet::new(id, name);
                        sheet.default_col_width = tag.get_attr("defColW").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(100.0);
                        sheet.default_row_height = tag.get_attr("defRowH").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(24.0);
                        current_sheet = Some(sheet);
                    }
                }
                "documents" => {
                    // Container tag, scanner will process child document tags
                }
                "history" => {
                    if tag.is_closing {
                        open_history_containers = open_history_containers.checked_sub(1)
                            .ok_or_else(|| ParseError::InvalidHistory("Closing history without an open history block".into()))?;
                    } else if !tag.is_self_closing {
                        open_history_containers += 1;
                    }
                }
                "layer" => {
                    if tag.is_closing {
                        let (sheet_id, z, changes) = current_layer.take().ok_or_else(|| {
                            ParseError::InvalidHistory("Closing layer without an open layer".into())
                        })?;
                        if workbook.get_sheet(&sheet_id).is_none() {
                            return Err(ParseError::InvalidHistory(format!("Unknown history sheet '{}'", sheet_id)));
                        }
                        workbook.history.entry(sheet_id.clone()).or_insert_with(crate::history::HistoryEngine::new)
                            .insert_layer(z, changes)
                            .map_err(|message| ParseError::InvalidHistory(message.into()))?;
                    } else {
                        if current_layer.is_some() {
                            return Err(ParseError::InvalidHistory("Nested history layers are not allowed".into()));
                        }
                        let sheet_id = tag.get_attr_unescaped("sheet")
                            .ok_or(ParseError::MissingAttribute("sheet"))?;
                        let z = tag.get_attr("z").and_then(|value| value.parse::<u32>().ok())
                            .ok_or(ParseError::MissingAttribute("z"))?;
                        if tag.is_self_closing {
                            if workbook.get_sheet(&sheet_id).is_none() {
                                return Err(ParseError::InvalidHistory(format!("Unknown history sheet '{}'", sheet_id)));
                            }
                            workbook.history.entry(sheet_id.clone()).or_insert_with(crate::history::HistoryEngine::new)
                                .insert_layer(z, Vec::new())
                                .map_err(|message| ParseError::InvalidHistory(message.into()))?;
                        } else {
                            current_layer = Some((sheet_id, z, Vec::new()));
                        }
                    }
                }
                "delete" => {
                    if tag.is_closing || !tag.is_self_closing {
                        return Err(ParseError::InvalidHistory("History deletions must use self-closing <delete /> tags".into()));
                    }
                    let (_, _, changes) = current_layer.as_mut().ok_or_else(|| {
                        ParseError::InvalidHistory("Deletion outside a history layer".into())
                    })?;
                    let coord_str = tag.get_attr("ref").ok_or(ParseError::MissingAttribute("ref"))?;
                    let coord = Coord::parse_a1(coord_str)
                        .ok_or_else(|| ParseError::InvalidCoordinate(coord_str.to_string()))?;
                    changes.push((coord, None));
                }
                "col" => {
                    if tag.is_self_closing {
                        let index = tag.get_attr("index").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
                        let width = tag.get_attr("w").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(100.0);
                        if let Some(ref mut sheet) = current_sheet {
                            sheet.set_col_width(index, width);
                        }
                    }
                }
                "row" => {
                    if tag.is_self_closing {
                        let index = tag.get_attr("index").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
                        let height = tag.get_attr("h").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(24.0);
                        if let Some(ref mut sheet) = current_sheet {
                            sheet.set_row_height(index, height);
                        }
                    }
                }
                "document" => {
                    if !tag.is_closing {
                        let id = tag.get_attr_unescaped("id").unwrap_or_else(|| "doc".to_string());
                        let title = tag.get_attr_unescaped("title").unwrap_or_else(|| "Document".to_string());
                        let doc_type = tag.get_attr_unescaped("type").unwrap_or_else(|| "text/markdown".to_string());
                        let body = if let Some(len) = tag.get_attr("bytes").and_then(|value| value.parse::<usize>().ok()) {
                            let bytes = scanner.consume_exact_bytes(len)?;
                            scanner.expect_closing_tag("document")?;
                            core::str::from_utf8(bytes).unwrap_or("").to_string()
                        } else {
                            let inner_bytes = scanner.consume_until_closing_tag("document")?;
                            let mut s = core::str::from_utf8(inner_bytes).unwrap_or("").trim();
                            if s.starts_with("<![CDATA[") && s.ends_with("]]>") {
                                s = &s[9..s.len() - 3];
                                s = s.trim();
                            }
                            s.to_string()
                        };
                        let mut doc = crate::doc::Document::new(id, title, body);
                        doc.content_type = doc_type.to_string();
                        workbook.documents.push(doc);
                    }
                }
                "cell" => {
                    if tag.is_closing {
                        // Handled inline below
                    } else {
                        let coord_str = tag
                            .get_attr("ref")
                            .ok_or(ParseError::MissingAttribute("ref"))?;
                        let coord = Coord::parse_a1(coord_str)
                            .ok_or_else(|| ParseError::InvalidCoordinate(coord_str.to_string()))?;

                        let cell_type = tag.get_attr("type").unwrap_or("empty");
                        let cell_id = tag.get_attr_unescaped("id");
                        let cell_name = tag.get_attr_unescaped("name");

                        let value = if tag.is_self_closing {
                            CellValue::Empty
                        } else {
                            match cell_type {
                                "blob" => {
                                    let bytes_len: usize = tag
                                        .get_attr("bytes")
                                        .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
                                        .unwrap_or(0);

                                    let raw_slice = scanner.consume_exact_bytes(bytes_len)?;
                                    scanner.expect_closing_tag("cell")?;
                                    CellValue::Blob {
                                        bytes: raw_slice.to_vec(),
                                    }
                                }
                                "basic" => {
                                    let source = if let Some(len) = tag.get_attr("bytes").and_then(|value| value.parse::<usize>().ok()) {
                                        let bytes = scanner.consume_exact_bytes(len)?;
                                        scanner.expect_closing_tag("cell")?;
                                        core::str::from_utf8(bytes).unwrap_or("").to_string()
                                    } else {
                                        let inner_bytes = scanner.consume_until_closing_tag("cell")?;
                                        let mut s = core::str::from_utf8(inner_bytes).unwrap_or("").trim();
                                        if s.starts_with("<![CDATA[") && s.ends_with("]]>") {
                                            s = &s[9..s.len() - 3];
                                            s = s.trim();
                                        }
                                        s.to_string()
                                    };
                                    CellValue::Basic { source }
                                }
                                "formula" => {
                                    let expr = tag.get_attr_unescaped("formula").unwrap_or_default();
                                    let inner_bytes = scanner.consume_until_closing_tag("cell")?;
                                    let inner_str = core::str::from_utf8(inner_bytes).unwrap_or("");
                                    let cached_type = tag.get_attr("cachedType");
                                    let (cached_num, cached_text, cached_bool) = match cached_type {
                                        Some("number") => {
                                            let value = inner_str.trim().parse::<f64>()
                                                .map_err(|_| ParseError::InvalidNumber(inner_str.trim().to_string()))?;
                                            (Some(value), None, None)
                                        }
                                        Some("boolean") => (None, None, Some(inner_str.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case("true"))),
                                        Some("text") => (None, Some(unescape_xml(inner_str)), None),
                                        Some("empty") | None => {
                                            let legacy = inner_str.trim();
                                            let number = legacy.parse::<f64>().ok();
                                            let text = if number.is_none() && !legacy.is_empty() { Some(unescape_xml(legacy)) } else { None };
                                            (number, text, None)
                                        }
                                        Some(other) => return Err(ParseError::InvalidFormulaCache(other.to_string())),
                                    };

                                    CellValue::Formula {
                                        expr,
                                        cached_num,
                                        cached_text,
                                        cached_bool,
                                    }
                                }
                                "number" => {
                                    let inner_bytes = scanner.consume_until_closing_tag("cell")?;
                                    let s = core::str::from_utf8(inner_bytes)
                                        .unwrap_or("")
                                        .trim();
                                    let n = s
                                        .parse::<f64>()
                                        .map_err(|_| ParseError::InvalidNumber(s.to_string()))?;
                                    CellValue::Number(n)
                                }
                                "text" => {
                                    let inner_bytes = scanner.consume_until_closing_tag("cell")?;
                                    let s = core::str::from_utf8(inner_bytes).unwrap_or("");
                                    CellValue::Text(unescape_xml(s))
                                }
                                "boolean" => {
                                    let inner_bytes = scanner.consume_until_closing_tag("cell")?;
                                    let s = core::str::from_utf8(inner_bytes)
                                        .unwrap_or("")
                                        .trim();
                                    CellValue::Boolean(s.eq_ignore_ascii_case("true"))
                                }
                                "error" => {
                                    let inner_bytes = scanner.consume_until_closing_tag("cell")?;
                                    let s = core::str::from_utf8(inner_bytes)
                                        .unwrap_or("")
                                        .trim();
                                    CellValue::Error(unescape_xml(s))
                                }
                                _ => {
                                    scanner.consume_until_closing_tag("cell")?;
                                    CellValue::Empty
                                }
                            }
                        };

                        let mut cell = Cell::new(coord, value);
                        cell.id = cell_id;
                        cell.name = cell_name;
                        cell.col_span = tag.get_attr("colSpan").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(1);
                        cell.row_span = tag.get_attr("rowSpan").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(1);
                        cell.overlay = tag.get_attr_unescaped("overlay");
                        cell.document_refs = tag.get_attr_unescaped("documentRefs")
                            .unwrap_or_default()
                            .split(',')
                            .map(str::trim)
                            .filter(|target| !target.is_empty())
                            .map(ToString::to_string)
                            .collect();
                        if matches!(&cell.value, CellValue::Basic { .. }) {
                            cell.basic_reads = tag.get_attr_unescaped("reads").unwrap_or_default()
                                .split(',').map(str::trim).filter(|name| !name.is_empty()).map(ToString::to_string).collect();
                            cell.basic_writes = tag.get_attr_unescaped("writes").unwrap_or_default()
                                .split(',').map(str::trim).filter(|name| !name.is_empty()).map(ToString::to_string).collect();
                        }

                        if let Some((_, _, changes)) = current_layer.as_mut() {
                            changes.push((coord, Some(cell)));
                        } else if let Some(ref mut sheet) = current_sheet {
                            sheet.set_cell(cell);
                        }
                    }
                }
                _ => {
                    // Ignore unknown tags safely
                    if !tag.is_self_closing && !tag.is_closing {
                        let _ = scanner.skip_until_closing_tag(tag.name);
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            scanner.advance(1);
        }
    }

    if current_layer.is_some() || open_history_containers != 0 {
        return Err(ParseError::UnexpectedEof);
    }
    if let Some(sheet) = current_sheet.take() {
        workbook.sheets.push(sheet);
    }

    Ok(workbook)
}

struct Tag<'a> {
    name: &'a str,
    is_closing: bool,
    is_self_closing: bool,
    attrs: Vec<(&'a str, &'a str)>,
}

impl<'a> Tag<'a> {
    /// Attribute value with XML entities decoded (for free-text attributes: id, name, title, formula).
    fn get_attr_unescaped(&self, key: &str) -> Option<String> {
        self.get_attr(key).map(unescape_xml)
    }

    fn get_attr(&self, key: &str) -> Option<&'a str> {
        self.attrs
            .iter()
            .find(|(k, _)| *k == key)
            .map(|(_, v)| *v)
    }
}

struct Scanner<'a> {
    data: &'a [u8],
    pos: usize,
}

impl<'a> Scanner<'a> {
    fn new(data: &'a [u8]) -> Self {
        Self { data, pos: 0 }
    }

    fn is_eof(&self) -> bool {
        self.pos >= self.data.len()
    }

    fn peek(&self) -> Option<u8> {
        self.data.get(self.pos).copied()
    }

    fn advance(&mut self, n: usize) {
        self.pos = (self.pos + n).min(self.data.len());
    }

    fn skip_whitespace_and_comments(&mut self) {
        loop {
            // Whitespace
            while self.pos < self.data.len() && self.data[self.pos].is_ascii_whitespace() {
                self.pos += 1;
            }

            // Comments
            if self.pos + 4 <= self.data.len() && &self.data[self.pos..self.pos + 4] == b"<!--" {
                self.pos += 4;
                while self.pos + 3 <= self.data.len() {
                    if &self.data[self.pos..self.pos + 3] == b"-->" {
                        self.pos += 3;
                        break;
                    }
                    self.pos += 1;
                }
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    fn parse_tag_header(&mut self) -> Result<Tag<'a>, ParseError> {
        if self.peek() != Some(b'<') {
            return Err(ParseError::InvalidTag(self.pos));
        }
        self.advance(1); // skip '<'

        let is_closing = if self.peek() == Some(b'/') {
            self.advance(1);
            true
        } else {
            false
        };

        // Extract tag name
        let name_start = self.pos;
        while self.pos < self.data.len()
            && !self.data[self.pos].is_ascii_whitespace()
            && self.data[self.pos] != b'>'
            && self.data[self.pos] != b'/'
        {
            self.pos += 1;
        }

        let name = core::str::from_utf8(&self.data[name_start..self.pos])
            .map_err(|_| ParseError::InvalidTag(name_start))?;

        let mut attrs = Vec::new();
        let mut is_self_closing = false;

        // Parse attributes
        loop {
            while self.pos < self.data.len() && self.data[self.pos].is_ascii_whitespace() {
                self.pos += 1;
            }

            if self.pos >= self.data.len() {
                return Err(ParseError::UnexpectedEof);
            }

            if self.data[self.pos] == b'/' {
                is_self_closing = true;
                self.advance(1);
                continue;
            }

            if self.data[self.pos] == b'>' {
                self.advance(1); // end of tag
                break;
            }

            // Read attribute key
            let key_start = self.pos;
            while self.pos < self.data.len()
                && self.data[self.pos] != b'='
                && !self.data[self.pos].is_ascii_whitespace()
                && self.data[self.pos] != b'>'
                && self.data[self.pos] != b'/'
            {
                self.pos += 1;
            }

            let key = core::str::from_utf8(&self.data[key_start..self.pos])
                .map_err(|_| ParseError::MalformedAttribute(key_start))?;

            while self.pos < self.data.len() && self.data[self.pos].is_ascii_whitespace() {
                self.pos += 1;
            }

            if self.pos < self.data.len() && self.data[self.pos] == b'=' {
                self.advance(1);
                while self.pos < self.data.len() && self.data[self.pos].is_ascii_whitespace() {
                    self.pos += 1;
                }

                let quote = self.peek().ok_or(ParseError::UnexpectedEof)?;
                if quote == b'"' || quote == b'\'' {
                    self.advance(1);
                    let val_start = self.pos;
                    while self.pos < self.data.len() && self.data[self.pos] != quote {
                        self.pos += 1;
                    }
                    let val = core::str::from_utf8(&self.data[val_start..self.pos])
                        .map_err(|_| ParseError::MalformedAttribute(val_start))?;
                    self.advance(1); // consume closing quote
                    attrs.push((key, val));
                }
            }
        }

        Ok(Tag {
            name,
            is_closing,
            is_self_closing,
            attrs,
        })
    }

    fn consume_exact_bytes(&mut self, n: usize) -> Result<&'a [u8], ParseError> {
        if self.pos + n > self.data.len() {
            return Err(ParseError::BlobOverflow {
                expected: n,
                available: self.data.len().saturating_sub(self.pos),
            });
        }
        let slice = &self.data[self.pos..self.pos + n];
        self.pos += n;
        Ok(slice)
    }

    fn consume_until_closing_tag(&mut self, tag: &str) -> Result<&'a [u8], ParseError> {
        let needle = [b'<', b'/'];
        let start = self.pos;

        while self.pos + 2 + tag.len() + 1 <= self.data.len() {
            if &self.data[self.pos..self.pos + 2] == &needle {
                let tag_slice = &self.data[self.pos + 2..self.pos + 2 + tag.len()];
                if tag_slice.eq_ignore_ascii_case(tag.as_bytes()) {
                    let content = &self.data[start..self.pos];
                    self.pos += 2 + tag.len();
                    while self.pos < self.data.len() && self.data[self.pos] != b'>' {
                        self.pos += 1;
                    }
                    if self.pos < self.data.len() {
                        self.pos += 1; // skip '>'
                    }
                    return Ok(content);
                }
            }
            self.pos += 1;
        }

        Err(ParseError::UnexpectedEof)
    }

    fn expect_closing_tag(&mut self, tag: &str) -> Result<(), ParseError> {
        self.skip_whitespace_and_comments();
        if self.pos + 2 + tag.len() + 1 > self.data.len() {
            return Err(ParseError::UnexpectedEof);
        }
        if &self.data[self.pos..self.pos + 2] != b"</" {
            return Err(ParseError::InvalidTag(self.pos));
        }
        self.pos += 2;
        let tag_slice = &self.data[self.pos..self.pos + tag.len()];
        if !tag_slice.eq_ignore_ascii_case(tag.as_bytes()) {
            return Err(ParseError::InvalidTag(self.pos));
        }
        self.pos += tag.len();
        while self.pos < self.data.len() && self.data[self.pos] != b'>' {
            self.pos += 1;
        }
        if self.pos < self.data.len() {
            self.pos += 1;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn skip_until_closing_tag(&mut self, tag: &str) -> Result<(), ParseError> {
        let _ = self.consume_until_closing_tag(tag)?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn unescape_xml(s: &str) -> String {
    // `&amp;` must be decoded last, otherwise "&amp;lt;" would collapse to "<".
    s.replace("&quot;", "\"")
        .replace("&lt;", "<")
        .replace("&gt;", ">")
        .replace("&amp;", "&")
}

//! Serialization of Lotus 5-7-11 workbooks to the uncompressed hybrid format.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{format, string::String, vec::Vec};

use crate::model::{Cell, CellValue, Workbook};

/// Serializes a `Workbook` into a `.lotus` byte buffer.
///
/// The resulting format is:
/// - Human-readable in Notepad.
/// - Clearly marked with ASCII tags in a hex editor.
/// - Directly inspectable and editable by an AI as structured text.
/// - Preserves raw, uncompressed binary payloads byte-for-byte in `<cell type="blob">` tags.
pub fn serialize_workbook(wb: &Workbook) -> Vec<u8> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();

    // 1. Header
    out.extend_from_slice(b"<!-- LOTUS-5-7-11 WORKBOOK v1.0 -->\n");
    let wb_tag = format!(
        "<workbook id=\"{}\" title=\"{}\" calculation=\"{}\">\n",
        escape_attr(&wb.id),
        escape_attr(&wb.title),
        escape_attr(&wb.calc_mode)
    );
    out.extend_from_slice(wb_tag.as_bytes());

    // 2. Dynamic Table of Contents (TOC)
    let toc = wb.compute_toc();
    let toc_tag = format!(
        "  <toc ref=\"{}\" extentX=\"{}\" extentY=\"{}\">\n",
        toc.pos.to_a1(),
        toc.extent_col,
        toc.extent_row
    );
    out.extend_from_slice(toc_tag.as_bytes());

    for entry in &toc.entries {
        let entry_line = format!(
            "    <entry sheet=\"{}\" ref=\"{}\" name=\"{}\" type=\"{}\" bytes=\"{}\" />\n",
            escape_attr(&entry.sheet_id),
            entry.coord.to_a1(),
            escape_attr(&entry.name),
            entry.type_name,
            entry.byte_size
        );
        out.extend_from_slice(entry_line.as_bytes());
    }
    out.extend_from_slice(b"  </toc>\n\n");

    // 3. Sheets and Cells
    for sheet in &wb.sheets {
        let sheet_header = format!(
            "  <sheet id=\"{}\" name=\"{}\" defColW=\"{}\" defRowH=\"{}\">\n",
            escape_attr(&sheet.id),
            escape_attr(&sheet.name),
            sheet.default_col_width,
            sheet.default_row_height
        );
        out.extend_from_slice(sheet_header.as_bytes());

        for (col, width) in &sheet.col_widths {
            out.extend_from_slice(format!("    <col index=\"{}\" w=\"{}\" />\n", col, width).as_bytes());
        }
        for (row, height) in &sheet.row_heights {
            out.extend_from_slice(format!("    <row index=\"{}\" h=\"{}\" />\n", row, height).as_bytes());
        }

        for cell in sheet.cells.values() {
            write_cell(&mut out, cell);
        }

        out.extend_from_slice(b"  </sheet>\n");
    }

    // 4. Embedded Documents
    if !wb.documents.is_empty() {
        out.extend_from_slice(b"\n  <documents>\n");
        for doc in &wb.documents {
            let doc_header = format!(
                "    <document id=\"{}\" title=\"{}\" type=\"{}\" bytes=\"{}\">",
                escape_attr(&doc.id),
                escape_attr(&doc.title),
                escape_attr(&doc.content_type),
                doc.body.as_bytes().len()
            );
            out.extend_from_slice(doc_header.as_bytes());
            out.extend_from_slice(doc.body.as_bytes());
            out.extend_from_slice(b"</document>\n");
        }
        out.extend_from_slice(b"  </documents>\n");
    }

    // 5. Historical Z-layer overlays. Base sheet content is Z=0.
    if !wb.history.is_empty() {
        out.extend_from_slice(b"\n  <history>\n");
        for (sheet_id, history) in &wb.history {
            for (z, records) in &history.layers {
                out.extend_from_slice(
                    format!("    <layer sheet=\"{}\" z=\"{}\">\n", escape_attr(sheet_id), z).as_bytes(),
                );
                for record in records {
                    if let Some(cell) = &record.cell {
                        write_cell(&mut out, cell);
                    } else {
                        out.extend_from_slice(
                            format!("    <delete ref=\"{}\" />\n", record.coord.to_a1()).as_bytes(),
                        );
                    }
                }
                out.extend_from_slice(b"    </layer>\n");
            }
        }
        out.extend_from_slice(b"  </history>\n");
    }

    // 6. Closing Tag
    out.extend_from_slice(b"</workbook>\n");

    out
}

fn write_cell(out: &mut Vec<u8>, cell: &Cell) {
    let mut attrs = format!(" ref=\"{}\"", cell.coord.to_a1());

    if let Some(ref id) = cell.id {
        attrs.push_str(&format!(" id=\"{}\"", escape_attr(id)));
    }

    if let Some(ref name) = cell.name {
        attrs.push_str(&format!(" name=\"{}\"", escape_attr(name)));
    }
    if !cell.document_refs.is_empty() {
        attrs.push_str(&format!(
            " documentRefs=\"{}\"",
            escape_attr(&cell.document_refs.join(","))
        ));
    }
    if cell.col_span != 1 {
        attrs.push_str(&format!(" colSpan=\"{}\"", cell.col_span));
    }
    if cell.row_span != 1 {
        attrs.push_str(&format!(" rowSpan=\"{}\"", cell.row_span));
    }
    if let Some(ref ov) = cell.overlay {
        attrs.push_str(&format!(" overlay=\"{}\"", escape_attr(ov)));
    }

    match &cell.value {
        CellValue::Empty => {
            let line = format!("    <cell{} type=\"empty\" />\n", attrs);
            out.extend_from_slice(line.as_bytes());
        }
        CellValue::Number(n) => {
            let line = format!("    <cell{} type=\"number\">{}</cell>\n", attrs, n);
            out.extend_from_slice(line.as_bytes());
        }
        CellValue::Text(t) => {
            let line = format!(
                "    <cell{} type=\"text\">{}</cell>\n",
                attrs,
                escape_xml_text(t)
            );
            out.extend_from_slice(line.as_bytes());
        }
        CellValue::Boolean(b) => {
            let line = format!("    <cell{} type=\"boolean\">{}</cell>\n", attrs, b);
            out.extend_from_slice(line.as_bytes());
        }
        CellValue::Formula {
            expr,
            cached_num,
            cached_text,
            cached_bool,
        } => {
            let mut val_str = String::new();
            let mut cached_type = String::new();
            if let Some(n) = cached_num {
                val_str = format!("{}", n);
                cached_type = "number".into();
            } else if let Some(value) = cached_bool {
                val_str = value.to_string();
                cached_type = "boolean".into();
            } else if let Some(ref t) = cached_text {
                val_str = escape_xml_text(t);
                cached_type = "text".into();
            }

            let cache_attr = if cached_type.is_empty() { String::new() } else {
                format!(" cachedType=\"{}\"", cached_type)
            };

            let line = format!(
                "    <cell{} type=\"formula\" formula=\"{}\"{}>{}</cell>\n",
                attrs,
                escape_attr(expr),
                cache_attr,
                val_str
            );
            out.extend_from_slice(line.as_bytes());
        }
        CellValue::Basic { source } => {
            if !cell.basic_reads.is_empty() {
                attrs.push_str(&format!(" reads=\"{}\"", escape_attr(&cell.basic_reads.join(","))));
            }
            if !cell.basic_writes.is_empty() {
                attrs.push_str(&format!(" writes=\"{}\"", escape_attr(&cell.basic_writes.join(","))));
            }
            let open = format!("    <cell{} type=\"basic\" bytes=\"{}\">", attrs, source.as_bytes().len());
            out.extend_from_slice(open.as_bytes());
            out.extend_from_slice(source.as_bytes());
            out.extend_from_slice(b"</cell>\n");
        }
        CellValue::Blob { bytes } => {
            let open = format!(
                "    <cell{} type=\"blob\" bytes=\"{}\">",
                attrs,
                bytes.len()
            );
            out.extend_from_slice(open.as_bytes());
            // Embed exact, uncompressed raw payload bytes
            out.extend_from_slice(bytes);
            out.extend_from_slice(b"</cell>\n");
        }
        CellValue::Error(e) => {
            let line = format!(
                "    <cell{} type=\"error\">{}</cell>\n",
                attrs,
                escape_xml_text(e)
            );
            out.extend_from_slice(line.as_bytes());
        }
    }
}

fn escape_attr(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(s.len());
    for c in s.chars() {
        match c {
            '"' => out.push_str("&quot;"),
            '&' => out.push_str("&amp;"),
            '<' => out.push_str("&lt;"),
            '>' => out.push_str("&gt;"),
            _ => out.push(c),
        }
    }
    out
}

fn escape_xml_text(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(s.len());
    for c in s.chars() {
        match c {
            '&' => out.push_str("&amp;"),
            '<' => out.push_str("&lt;"),
            '>' => out.push_str("&gt;"),
            _ => out.push(c),
        }
    }
    out
}

FILE: lotus-core/src/substrate/mod.rs

//! Shared Exokernel Substrate and Memory-Map Contract.
//!
//! Provides the memory window mapping and zero-copy shared mailbox interface
//! for running alongside `ggml` under BareMetal OS (Return Infinity) or custom unikernels.
//!
//! Layout in contiguous 64-bit physical address space:
//! ```text
//! +-------------------------------------------------------------------+
//! | Substrate Descriptor (Magic, ABI Version, Memory Boundaries)      |
//! +-------------------------------------------------------------------+
//! | Lotus 5-7-11 Memory Window (Workspace, DAG, BASIC, Arena)         |
//! +-------------------------------------------------------------------+
//! | Shared Parameter / Tensor Mailbox Window (Zero-Copy Transfer)    |
//! +-------------------------------------------------------------------+
//! | ggml Unikernel Memory Window (Model Weights, KV Cache, Compute)   |
//! +-------------------------------------------------------------------+
//! ```

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::vec::Vec;
use core::ptr::NonNull;

pub const SUBSTRATE_MAGIC: [u8; 8] = *b"LOTUSEXO";
pub const SUBSTRATE_VERSION: u16 = 1;

/// Memory region descriptor for an individual execution partition.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[repr(C)]
pub struct Partition {
    pub offset: usize,
    pub size: usize,
}

/// The Canonical Substrate Descriptor mapped at the base of the shared memory space.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[repr(C)]
pub struct SubstrateDescriptor {
    pub magic: [u8; 8],
    pub version: u16,
    pub flags: u16,
    pub total_memory: usize,
    pub lotus_partition: Partition,
    pub mailbox_partition: Partition,
    pub ggml_partition: Partition,
}

impl SubstrateDescriptor {
    /// Create a validated memory layout across total available physical memory.
    pub fn new_layout(total_memory: usize, lotus_size: usize, mailbox_size: usize) -> Result<Self, &'static str> {
        let desc_size = 64; // Reserved descriptor header size
        let needed = desc_size + lotus_size + mailbox_size;
        if total_memory < needed {
            return Err("Insufficient memory for substrate partitioning");
        }

        let lotus_partition = Partition {
            offset: desc_size,
            size: lotus_size,
        };

        let mailbox_partition = Partition {
            offset: desc_size + lotus_size,
            size: mailbox_size,
        };

        let ggml_offset = desc_size + lotus_size + mailbox_size;
        let ggml_size = total_memory - ggml_offset;

        let ggml_partition = Partition {
            offset: ggml_offset,
            size: ggml_size,
        };

        Ok(Self {
            magic: SUBSTRATE_MAGIC,
            version: SUBSTRATE_VERSION,
            flags: 0,
            total_memory,
            lotus_partition,
            mailbox_partition,
            ggml_partition,
        })
    }
}

/// Zero-copy Mailbox for direct tensor and parameter handoff between Lotus and ggml.
pub struct SharedMailbox {
    buffer: NonNull<u8>,
    size: usize,
}

impl SharedMailbox {
    pub unsafe fn from_raw(base: *mut u8, size: usize) -> Option<Self> {
        let ptr = NonNull::new(base)?;
        Some(Self { buffer: ptr, size })
    }

    pub fn write_payload(&mut self, offset: usize, data: &[u8]) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
        if offset + data.len() > self.size {
            return Err("Mailbox overflow");
        }
        unsafe {
            let dest = self.buffer.as_ptr().add(offset);
            core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(data.as_ptr(), dest, data.len());
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn read_payload<'a>(&self, offset: usize, len: usize) -> Result<&'a [u8], &'static str> {
        if offset + len > self.size {
            return Err("Mailbox underflow / read out of bounds");
        }
        unsafe {
            let src = self.buffer.as_ptr().add(offset);
            Ok(core::slice::from_raw_parts(src, len))
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_substrate_layout_partitioning() {
        // Assume 64 MB total physical memory handed by BareMetal OS
        let total_ram = 64 * 1024 * 1024;
        let lotus_ram = 16 * 1024 * 1024; // 16 MB for Lotus
        let mailbox_ram = 2 * 1024 * 1024; // 2 MB mailbox

        let desc = SubstrateDescriptor::new_layout(total_ram, lotus_ram, mailbox_ram).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(desc.magic, SUBSTRATE_MAGIC);
        assert_eq!(desc.lotus_partition.size, 16 * 1024 * 1024);
        assert_eq!(desc.mailbox_partition.size, 2 * 1024 * 1024);
        // Remainder given to ggml unikernel for weights/compute
        assert_eq!(desc.ggml_partition.size, total_ram - 64 - lotus_ram - mailbox_ram);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_shared_mailbox_zero_copy() {
        let mut buffer = [0u8; 128];
        let mut mailbox = unsafe { SharedMailbox::from_raw(buffer.as_mut_ptr(), buffer.len()).unwrap() };

        let prompt = b"Model Weights Matrix Row [0.12, 0.45, 0.78]";
        mailbox.write_payload(0, prompt).unwrap();

        let read_back = mailbox.read_payload(0, prompt.len()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(read_back, prompt);
    }
}

FILE: lotus-core/src/tui/editor.rs

//! Interactive spreadsheet workspace session and cursor state machine.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{format, string::{String, ToString}};

use super::menu::MenuState;
use crate::{
    calc::CalcEngine,
    model::{Cell, CellValue, Coord, Sheet, TableOfContents},
    ops::Range,
};

pub struct WorkspaceSession {
    pub cursor: Coord,
    pub edit_buffer: Option<String>,
    pub edit_cursor: usize,
    pub edit_selection_anchor: Option<usize>,
    /// When the edit line is answering a question (Find, Go to, column width)
    /// instead of editing a cell, the question shown in front of it.
    pub edit_prompt: Option<String>,
    /// The other corner of a multi-cell selection; the cursor is the first corner.
    pub selection_anchor: Option<Coord>,
    /// Extend mode (F8): arrow keys grow the selection instead of clearing it.
    pub extend_mode: bool,
    pub menu: MenuState,
    pub status_message: String,
    pub is_dirty: bool,
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::WorkspaceSession;
    use crate::model::{Sheet, Workbook};

    #[test]
    fn dirty_workspace_marks_sheet_title() {
        let sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        let toc = Workbook::new("w", "w").with_sheet(sheet.clone()).compute_toc();
        let mut session = WorkspaceSession::new();
        session.is_dirty = true;
        assert!(session.render_workspace(&sheet, &toc).starts_with("Main* |"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn edit_buffer_is_visible_on_the_control_panel_edit_line() {
        let sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        let toc = Workbook::new("w", "w")
            .with_sheet(sheet.clone())
            .compute_toc();
        let mut session = WorkspaceSession::new();
        session.edit_buffer = Some("100".into());
        let rendered = session.render_workspace(&sheet, &toc);
        let mut lines = rendered.lines();
        assert!(lines.next().unwrap().starts_with("Main | A1"));
        assert_eq!(lines.next(), Some("EDIT: 100 | Enter commits, Esc cancels"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_edit_selection_and_cursor_movement() {
        let mut session = WorkspaceSession::new();
        session.edit_buffer = Some("HELLO WORLD".into());
        session.edit_cursor = 11; // at end

        // Shift+Left 5 times selects "WORLD"
        for _ in 0..5 {
            session.move_edit_cursor(-1, true);
        }
        assert_eq!(session.edit_cursor, 6);
        assert_eq!(session.edit_selection_anchor, Some(11));
        assert_eq!(session.selection_range(), Some((6, 11)));
        assert_eq!(session.selected_text().as_deref(), Some("WORLD"));

        // Typing a character replaces selection
        session.insert_char('!');
        assert_eq!(session.edit_buffer.as_deref(), Some("HELLO !"));
        assert_eq!(session.edit_cursor, 7);
        assert_eq!(session.selection_range(), None);
    }
}

impl WorkspaceSession {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            cursor: Coord::new(0, 0),
            edit_buffer: None,
            edit_cursor: 0,
            edit_selection_anchor: None,
            edit_prompt: None,
            selection_anchor: None,
            extend_mode: false,
            menu: MenuState::new(),
            status_message: "READY (Press '/' for Lotus Menu, Enter to edit cell)".into(),
            is_dirty: false,
        }
    }

    pub fn move_cursor(&mut self, delta_col: i32, delta_row: i32) {
        if self.edit_buffer.is_some() || self.menu.is_active {
            return;
        }

        let new_col = (self.cursor.col as i64 + i64::from(delta_col)).clamp(0, 16_383) as u32;
        let new_row = (self.cursor.row as i64 + i64::from(delta_row)).clamp(0, 1_048_575) as u32;
        self.cursor = Coord::new(new_col, new_row);
    }

    pub fn start_editing(&mut self, sheet: &Sheet) {
        if self.menu.is_active {
            return;
        }

        let current_text = match sheet.get_cell(self.cursor) {
            Some(cell) => match &cell.value {
                CellValue::Formula { expr, .. } => expr.clone(),
                CellValue::Number(n) => format!("{}", n),
                CellValue::Text(t) => t.clone(),
                CellValue::Boolean(b) => (if *b { "TRUE" } else { "FALSE" }).into(),
                _ => String::new(),
            },
            None => String::new(),
        };

        self.edit_cursor = current_text.len();
        self.edit_selection_anchor = None;
        self.edit_buffer = Some(current_text);
        self.status_message = "EDIT (Enter to commit, Esc to cancel)".into();
    }

    pub fn cancel_editing(&mut self) {
        self.edit_buffer = None;
        self.edit_cursor = 0;
        self.edit_selection_anchor = None;
        self.edit_prompt = None;
        self.status_message = "READY".into();
    }

    pub fn commit_editing(&mut self, sheet: &mut Sheet) {
        self.edit_cursor = 0;
        self.edit_selection_anchor = None;
        if let Some(text) = self.edit_buffer.take() {
            let cell_val = CellValue::parse_input(&text);
            if cell_val.is_empty() {
                sheet.cells.remove(&self.cursor);
            } else if let Some(cell) = sheet.cells.get_mut(&self.cursor) {
                cell.value = cell_val;
            } else {
                sheet.set_cell(Cell::new(self.cursor, cell_val));
            }

            // Recalculate sheet
            let _ = CalcEngine::recalculate_sheet(sheet, None);
            self.status_message = format!("Cell {} updated.", self.cursor.to_a1());
        }
    }

    /// The selected cells, if more than one cell is selected.
    pub fn selection(&self) -> Option<Range> {
        let anchor = self.selection_anchor?;
        if anchor == self.cursor { None } else { Some(Range::new(anchor, self.cursor)) }
    }

    /// The selected cells, or just the cursor cell when nothing else is selected.
    pub fn selected_range(&self) -> Range {
        self.selection().unwrap_or_else(|| Range::cell(self.cursor))
    }

    pub fn clear_selection(&mut self) {
        self.selection_anchor = None;
        self.extend_mode = false;
    }

    /// Start typing into an empty edit line (typing over a cell replaces it).
    pub fn begin_blank_edit(&mut self) {
        self.edit_buffer = Some(String::new());
        self.edit_cursor = 0;
        self.edit_selection_anchor = None;
        self.edit_prompt = None;
        self.status_message = "EDIT (Enter to commit, Esc to cancel)".into();
    }

    /// Use the edit line to ask a question; the answer comes back through [`end_prompt`](Self::end_prompt).
    pub fn begin_prompt(&mut self, label: &str) {
        self.begin_blank_edit();
        self.edit_prompt = Some(label.into());
        self.status_message = format!("{} (Enter to accept, Esc to cancel)", label.trim_end_matches([':', ' ']));
    }

    /// Finish a prompt and return what was typed.
    pub fn end_prompt(&mut self) -> Option<String> {
        self.edit_prompt = None;
        self.edit_cursor = 0;
        self.edit_selection_anchor = None;
        self.edit_buffer.take()
    }

    /// Move the edit caret to `pos` (a byte index on a character boundary),
    /// extending the text selection when `select` is held.
    fn set_edit_cursor(&mut self, pos: usize, select: bool) {
        if select {
            if self.edit_selection_anchor.is_none() {
                self.edit_selection_anchor = Some(self.edit_cursor);
            }
        } else {
            self.edit_selection_anchor = None;
        }
        self.edit_cursor = pos;
    }

    /// Home: caret to the start of the edit line.
    pub fn edit_home(&mut self, select: bool) {
        if self.edit_buffer.is_some() {
            self.set_edit_cursor(0, select);
        }
    }

    /// End: caret to the end of the edit line.
    pub fn edit_end(&mut self, select: bool) {
        if let Some(len) = self.edit_buffer.as_ref().map(|b| b.len()) {
            self.set_edit_cursor(len, select);
        }
    }

    /// Select the whole edit line.
    pub fn edit_select_all(&mut self) {
        if let Some(len) = self.edit_buffer.as_ref().map(|b| b.len()) {
            self.edit_selection_anchor = Some(0);
            self.edit_cursor = len;
        }
    }

    /// Ctrl+Left / Ctrl+Right: move the caret a word at a time.
    pub fn move_edit_word(&mut self, direction: i32, select: bool) {
        let Some(buf) = self.edit_buffer.as_ref() else { return };
        let pos = self.edit_cursor.min(buf.len());
        let target = if direction < 0 {
            let before = &buf[..pos];
            let trimmed = before.trim_end().len();
            before[..trimmed].rfind(char::is_whitespace).map_or(0, |i| {
                i + before[i..].chars().next().map_or(1, char::len_utf8)
            })
        } else {
            let after = &buf[pos..];
            let word_end = after.find(char::is_whitespace).unwrap_or(after.len());
            let rest = &after[word_end..];
            pos + word_end + (rest.len() - rest.trim_start().len())
        };
        self.set_edit_cursor(target, select);
    }

    /// Returns the active selection byte range `(start, end)` if any text is selected.
    pub fn selection_range(&self) -> Option<(usize, usize)> {
        let anchor = self.edit_selection_anchor?;
        let cursor = self.edit_cursor;
        if anchor == cursor {
            None
        } else {
            Some((anchor.min(cursor), anchor.max(cursor)))
        }
    }

    /// Returns the currently selected text substring if any.
    pub fn selected_text(&self) -> Option<String> {
        let buf = self.edit_buffer.as_ref()?;
        let (start, end) = self.selection_range()?;
        if start <= end && end <= buf.len() {
            Some(buf[start..end].to_string())
        } else {
            None
        }
    }

    /// Deletes the currently selected text range. Returns true if text was deleted.
    pub fn delete_selection(&mut self) -> bool {
        if let Some((start, end)) = self.selection_range() {
            if let Some(ref mut buf) = self.edit_buffer {
                if start <= end && end <= buf.len() {
                    buf.replace_range(start..end, "");
                    self.edit_cursor = start;
                    self.edit_selection_anchor = None;
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        false
    }

    /// Insert a character into the edit buffer at the current cursor, replacing any selection.
    pub fn insert_char(&mut self, ch: char) {
        self.delete_selection();
        if let Some(ref mut buf) = self.edit_buffer {
            let idx = self.edit_cursor.min(buf.len());
            buf.insert(idx, ch);
            self.edit_cursor = idx + ch.len_utf8();
            self.edit_selection_anchor = None;
        } else {
            self.edit_buffer = Some(ch.to_string());
            self.edit_cursor = ch.len_utf8();
            self.edit_selection_anchor = None;
        }
    }

    /// Insert a string into the edit buffer, replacing any selection.
    pub fn insert_str(&mut self, s: &str) {
        self.delete_selection();
        if let Some(ref mut buf) = self.edit_buffer {
            let idx = self.edit_cursor.min(buf.len());
            buf.insert_str(idx, s);
            self.edit_cursor = idx + s.len();
            self.edit_selection_anchor = None;
        } else {
            self.edit_buffer = Some(s.into());
            self.edit_cursor = s.len();
            self.edit_selection_anchor = None;
        }
    }

    /// Backspace key: deletes selection, or character before cursor.
    pub fn backspace(&mut self) {
        if !self.delete_selection() {
            if let Some(ref mut buf) = self.edit_buffer {
                if self.edit_cursor > 0 && self.edit_cursor <= buf.len() {
                    let mut prev_idx = self.edit_cursor - 1;
                    while !buf.is_char_boundary(prev_idx) && prev_idx > 0 {
                        prev_idx -= 1;
                    }
                    buf.drain(prev_idx..self.edit_cursor);
                    self.edit_cursor = prev_idx;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Delete key: deletes selection, or character after cursor.
    pub fn delete_forward(&mut self) {
        if !self.delete_selection() {
            if let Some(ref mut buf) = self.edit_buffer {
                if self.edit_cursor < buf.len() {
                    let mut next_idx = self.edit_cursor + 1;
                    while !buf.is_char_boundary(next_idx) && next_idx < buf.len() {
                        next_idx += 1;
                    }
                    buf.drain(self.edit_cursor..next_idx);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Move the edit cursor left or right. If `select` is true (Shift+Arrow), extends selection!
    pub fn move_edit_cursor(&mut self, delta: i32, select: bool) {
        if let Some(ref buf) = self.edit_buffer {
            let len = buf.len();
            if select && self.edit_selection_anchor.is_none() {
                self.edit_selection_anchor = Some(self.edit_cursor);
            } else if !select {
                self.edit_selection_anchor = None;
            }

            if delta < 0 {
                let mut new_pos = self.edit_cursor;
                for _ in 0..(-delta) {
                    if new_pos > 0 {
                        new_pos -= 1;
                        while !buf.is_char_boundary(new_pos) && new_pos > 0 {
                            new_pos -= 1;
                        }
                    }
                }
                self.edit_cursor = new_pos;
            } else if delta > 0 {
                let mut new_pos = self.edit_cursor;
                for _ in 0..delta {
                    if new_pos < len {
                        new_pos += 1;
                        while !buf.is_char_boundary(new_pos) && new_pos < len {
                            new_pos += 1;
                        }
                    }
                }
                self.edit_cursor = new_pos;
            }
        }
    }

    /// Renders the complete terminal workspace view including status bar,
    /// Lotus menu (or edit bar), and the active cell grid.
    pub fn render_workspace(&self, sheet: &Sheet, toc: &TableOfContents) -> String {
        self.render_workspace_with_viewport(sheet, toc, 6, 12)
    }

    /// Render the workspace using the supplied number of visible grid rows and columns.
    pub fn render_workspace_with_viewport(
        &self,
        sheet: &Sheet,
        toc: &TableOfContents,
        viewport_cols: u32,
        viewport_rows: u32,
    ) -> String {
        let mut screen = String::new();

        // Line 1: Cell Address & Current Content
        let cell_info = match sheet.get_cell(self.cursor) {
            Some(cell) => {
                let name = cell.name.as_deref().map(|n| format!(" ({})", n)).unwrap_or_default();
                let desc = match &cell.value {
                    CellValue::Formula { expr, cached_num, cached_text, cached_bool } => {
                        let cached = cached_num.map(|n| format!("{:.2}", n))
                            .or_else(|| cached_bool.map(|value| if value { "TRUE".into() } else { "FALSE".into() }))
                            .or_else(|| cached_text.clone()).unwrap_or_else(|| "<pending>".into());
                        format!("Formula: {} => {}", expr, cached)
                    }
                    CellValue::Number(n) => format!("Number: {:.2}", n),
                    CellValue::Text(t) => format!("Text: \"{}\"", t),
                    CellValue::Boolean(b) => format!("Boolean: {}", b),
                    CellValue::Basic { .. } => "Embedded BASIC Procedure".into(),
                    CellValue::Blob { bytes } => format!("Raw Binary Payload ({} bytes)", bytes.len()),
                    CellValue::Empty => "Empty".into(),
                    CellValue::Error(e) => format!("Error: {}", e),
                };
                let links = if !cell.document_refs.is_empty() {
                    format!(" [Links: {}]", cell.document_refs.join(", "))
                } else {
                    String::new()
                };
                format!("{}{}{}", desc, name, links)
            }
            None => "Empty".into(),
        };

        screen.push_str(&format!("{}{} | {:<6} {}\n", sheet.name, if self.is_dirty { "*" } else { "" }, self.cursor.to_a1(), cell_info));

        // Line 2 & Line 3: Menu Bar or Edit Buffer
        if self.menu.is_active {
            let (l1, l2) = self.menu.render_lines();
            screen.push_str(&format!("MENU: {}\n", l1));
            screen.push_str(&format!("      {}\n", l2));
        } else if self.edit_buffer.is_some() {
            screen.push_str(&format!(
                "EDIT: {} | Enter commits, Esc cancels\n\n",
                self.edit_buffer.as_deref().unwrap_or_default()
            ));
        } else {
            screen.push_str(&format!("STATUS: {}\n\n", self.status_message));
        }

        // Fixed grid window follows the cursor without drawing all cells from A1.
        screen.push_str(&crate::view::GridRenderer::render_viewport(
            sheet,
            Some(toc),
            self.cursor,
            viewport_cols,
            viewport_rows,
        ));

        screen
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod selection_and_prompt_tests {
    use super::WorkspaceSession;
    use crate::model::Coord;
    use crate::ops::Range;

    fn editing(text: &str, caret: usize) -> WorkspaceSession {
        let mut s = WorkspaceSession::new();
        s.edit_buffer = Some(text.into());
        s.edit_cursor = caret;
        s
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_cell_selection_is_the_cursor_plus_the_anchor() {
        let mut s = WorkspaceSession::new();
        s.cursor = Coord::new(2, 2);
        assert_eq!(s.selection(), None);
        assert_eq!(s.selected_range(), Range::cell(Coord::new(2, 2)));

        s.selection_anchor = Some(Coord::new(0, 0));
        assert_eq!(s.selection(), Some(Range::new(Coord::new(0, 0), Coord::new(2, 2))));
        assert_eq!(s.selected_range().to_a1(), "A1:C3");

        s.selection_anchor = Some(Coord::new(2, 2));
        assert_eq!(s.selection(), None, "an anchor on the cursor is not a range");

        s.extend_mode = true;
        s.clear_selection();
        assert_eq!((s.selection_anchor, s.extend_mode), (None, false));
    }

    #[test]
    fn home_end_and_select_all_move_and_select() {
        let mut s = editing("hello", 2);
        s.edit_end(true);
        assert_eq!((s.edit_cursor, s.selected_text().as_deref()), (5, Some("llo")));
        s.edit_home(false);
        assert_eq!((s.edit_cursor, s.selected_text()), (0, None), "moving without Shift drops the selection");
        s.edit_select_all();
        assert_eq!(s.selected_text().as_deref(), Some("hello"));

        let mut none = WorkspaceSession::new();
        none.edit_home(false); // not editing: nothing happens, nothing panics
        none.edit_end(true);
        none.edit_select_all();
    }

    #[test]
    fn word_movement_skips_runs_of_spaces() {
        let mut s = editing("one   two  three", 0);
        s.move_edit_word(1, false);
        assert_eq!(s.edit_cursor, 6, "to the start of 'two'");
        s.move_edit_word(1, false);
        assert_eq!(s.edit_cursor, 11, "to the start of 'three'");
        s.move_edit_word(1, false);
        assert_eq!(s.edit_cursor, 16, "to the end");
        s.move_edit_word(-1, false);
        assert_eq!(s.edit_cursor, 11);
        s.move_edit_word(-1, false);
        assert_eq!(s.edit_cursor, 6);
        s.move_edit_word(-1, false);
        assert_eq!(s.edit_cursor, 0);
        s.move_edit_word(-1, false);
        assert_eq!(s.edit_cursor, 0, "stays at the start");
    }

    #[test]
    fn word_movement_extends_a_selection_and_respects_multibyte_text() {
        let mut s = editing("héllo wörld", 0);
        s.move_edit_word(1, true);
        assert_eq!(s.selected_text().as_deref(), Some("héllo "));
        s.move_edit_word(1, true);
        assert_eq!(s.selected_text().as_deref(), Some("héllo wörld"));
        s.move_edit_word(-1, false);
        assert_eq!(s.edit_cursor, "héllo ".len(), "lands on a character boundary");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_prompt_collects_an_answer_and_cleans_up() {
        let mut s = WorkspaceSession::new();
        s.begin_prompt("Find: ");
        assert_eq!(s.edit_prompt.as_deref(), Some("Find: "));
        assert_eq!(s.edit_buffer.as_deref(), Some(""));
        s.insert_str("abc");
        assert_eq!(s.end_prompt().as_deref(), Some("abc"));
        assert_eq!((s.edit_prompt.clone(), s.edit_buffer.clone(), s.edit_cursor), (None, None, 0));
    }

    #[test]
    fn cancelling_drops_the_prompt_too() {
        let mut s = WorkspaceSession::new();
        s.begin_prompt("Go to: ");
        s.cancel_editing();
        assert_eq!((s.edit_prompt, s.edit_buffer), (None, None));
    }

    #[test]
    fn typing_over_a_blank_edit_starts_clean() {
        let mut s = editing("old", 3);
        s.edit_selection_anchor = Some(0);
        s.begin_blank_edit();
        assert_eq!((s.edit_buffer.as_deref(), s.edit_cursor, s.edit_selection_anchor), (Some(""), 0, None));
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-core/src/tui/menu.rs
================================================================================
//! Lotus 1-2-3 hierarchical slash-command menu state machine.
//!
//! Pressing '/' opens the top-level menu bar.
//! Submenus appear on line 2 with instant keyboard-shortcut jumping (first letter),
//! arrow-key navigation, and Escape key backtracking.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{format, string::String, vec, vec::Vec};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum MenuAction {
    None,
    FileSave,
    FileReload,
    FileInspect,
    BasicRun,
    RecalculateAll,
    TOCJump,
    Quit,
    OrphanCheck,
    // Edit
    EditUndo,
    EditRedo,
    EditCut,
    EditCopy,
    EditPaste,
    EditClear,
    EditSelectAll,
    EditFind,
    EditFindNext,
    EditGoTo,
    // Columns and rows
    InsertColumn,
    DeleteColumn,
    ColumnWidth,
    ColumnFit,
    ColumnCollapse,
    ColumnExpand,
    InsertRow,
    DeleteRow,
    RowHeight,
    RowCollapse,
    RowExpand,
    // Data
    FillDown,
    FillRight,
    SortAscending,
    SortDescending,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct MenuItem {
    pub key: char,
    pub label: &'static str,
    pub description: &'static str,
    pub action: MenuAction,
    pub submenus: Vec<MenuItem>,
}

impl MenuItem {
    pub fn new(key: char, label: &'static str, description: &'static str, action: MenuAction) -> Self {
        Self {
            key: key.to_ascii_uppercase(),
            label,
            description,
            action,
            submenus: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn with_submenus(mut self, submenus: Vec<MenuItem>) -> Self {
        self.submenus = submenus;
        self
    }
}

pub struct MenuState {
    pub is_active: bool,
    pub breadcrumbs: Vec<usize>, // Selected index at each menu depth
    pub root: Vec<MenuItem>,
}

impl MenuState {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::with_root(default_lotus_menu())
    }

    /// Build a menu state over a custom tree. In debug builds the tree is
    /// checked with [`validate_menu`] so a key collision fails loudly instead
    /// of silently shadowing an item.
    pub fn with_root(root: Vec<MenuItem>) -> Self {
        debug_assert!(
            validate_menu(&root).is_ok(),
            "invalid menu tree: {:?}",
            validate_menu(&root)
        );
        Self {
            is_active: false,
            breadcrumbs: Vec::new(),
            root,
        }
    }

    /// Open or toggle the menu bar with the classic '/' key.
    pub fn open(&mut self) {
        self.is_active = true;
        self.breadcrumbs.clear();
        self.breadcrumbs.push(0);
    }

    pub fn close(&mut self) {
        self.is_active = false;
        self.breadcrumbs.clear();
    }

    /// Handle a key event when the menu is active.
    /// Returns an executed `MenuAction` if an action was triggered.
    pub fn handle_key(&mut self, key_char: Option<char>, is_left: bool, is_right: bool, is_enter: bool, is_esc: bool) -> MenuAction {
        if !self.is_active {
            return MenuAction::None;
        }

        // Escape: Go up one level or dismiss menu
        if is_esc {
            if self.breadcrumbs.len() > 1 {
                self.breadcrumbs.pop();
            } else {
                self.close();
            }
            return MenuAction::None;
        }

        let current_items = self.current_level_items();
        let current_idx = *self.breadcrumbs.last().unwrap_or(&0);

        if is_left {
            let next_idx = if current_idx == 0 {
                current_items.len().saturating_sub(1)
            } else {
                current_idx - 1
            };
            if let Some(last) = self.breadcrumbs.last_mut() {
                *last = next_idx;
            }
            return MenuAction::None;
        }

        if is_right {
            let next_idx = (current_idx + 1) % current_items.len().max(1);
            if let Some(last) = self.breadcrumbs.last_mut() {
                *last = next_idx;
            }
            return MenuAction::None;
        }

        // First-letter shortcut jump (e.g. 'F' for File, 'S' for Save)
        if let Some(c) = key_char {
            let upper = c.to_ascii_uppercase();
            if let Some(pos) = current_items.iter().position(|item| item.key == upper) {
                if let Some(last) = self.breadcrumbs.last_mut() {
                    *last = pos;
                }
                return self.activate_current();
            }
        }

        if is_enter {
            return self.activate_current();
        }

        MenuAction::None
    }

    fn activate_current(&mut self) -> MenuAction {
        let current_idx = *self.breadcrumbs.last().unwrap_or(&0);
        let items = self.current_level_items();

        if let Some(selected) = items.get(current_idx) {
            if !selected.submenus.is_empty() {
                self.breadcrumbs.push(0);
                MenuAction::None
            } else {
                let action = selected.action.clone();
                if action != MenuAction::None {
                    self.close();
                }
                action
            }
        } else {
            MenuAction::None
        }
    }

    pub fn current_level_items(&self) -> &[MenuItem] {
        if self.breadcrumbs.is_empty() {
            return &self.root;
        }

        let mut current = &self.root;
        for &idx in &self.breadcrumbs[..self.breadcrumbs.len() - 1] {
            if let Some(item) = current.get(idx) {
                current = &item.submenus;
            } else {
                return &[];
            }
        }
        current
    }

    /// Returns (Line 1: Menu Items string, Line 2: Explanation/Submenu string).
    pub fn render_lines(&self) -> (String, String) {
        if !self.is_active {
            return (String::new(), String::new());
        }

        let items = self.current_level_items();
        let selected_idx = *self.breadcrumbs.last().unwrap_or(&0);

        let mut line1 = String::new();
        for (i, item) in items.iter().enumerate() {
            if i == selected_idx {
                line1.push_str(&format!("[{}] ", item.label));
            } else {
                line1.push_str(&format!(" {}  ", item.label));
            }
        }

        let line2 = if let Some(selected) = items.get(selected_idx) {
            if !selected.submenus.is_empty() {
                let mut sub_preview = String::new();
                for sub in &selected.submenus {
                    sub_preview.push_str(&format!("{} ", sub.label));
                }
                format!("{}: {}", selected.description, sub_preview)
            } else {
                selected.description.into()
            }
        } else {
            String::new()
        };

        (line1, line2)
    }
}

/// Check a menu tree for mistakes that would otherwise surface as a dead or
/// shadowed item at runtime:
///
/// - two items on the same level share a hotkey (first-letter jump takes the
///   first match, so the second item could never be reached by keyboard);
/// - a group (has submenus) also carries an action, or a leaf has none.
///
/// The error names the slash-path of the offender, e.g. `/WR`.
pub fn validate_menu(items: &[MenuItem]) -> Result<(), String> {
    validate_level(items, "/")
}

fn validate_level(items: &[MenuItem], prefix: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    for (i, item) in items.iter().enumerate() {
        let mut path = String::from(prefix);
        path.push(item.key);

        if let Some(other) = items[i + 1..].iter().find(|o| o.key == item.key) {
            return Err(format!(
                "duplicate hotkey '{}' at {}: \"{}\" and \"{}\"",
                item.key, prefix, item.label, other.label
            ));
        }

        if item.submenus.is_empty() {
            if item.action == MenuAction::None {
                return Err(format!("{} \"{}\" has neither an action nor submenus", path, item.label));
            }
        } else {
            if item.action != MenuAction::None {
                return Err(format!("{} \"{}\" is a group but also carries an action", path, item.label));
            }
            validate_level(&item.submenus, &path)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub fn default_lotus_menu() -> Vec<MenuItem> {
    vec![
        MenuItem::new('W', "Worksheet", "Global spreadsheet settings and actions", MenuAction::None)
            .with_submenus(vec![
                MenuItem::new('R', "Recalculate", "Recalculate all formulas across sheets", MenuAction::RecalculateAll),
                MenuItem::new('O', "Orphans", "Audit workbook for broken document links and dangling formulas", MenuAction::OrphanCheck),
            ]),
        MenuItem::new('E', "Edit", "Undo, clipboard, selection, find and go to", MenuAction::None)
            .with_submenus(vec![
                MenuItem::new('U', "Undo", "Undo the last change (Ctrl+Z)", MenuAction::EditUndo),
                MenuItem::new('R', "Redo", "Redo the change just undone (Ctrl+Y)", MenuAction::EditRedo),
                MenuItem::new('X', "Cut", "Copy the selection and clear it (Ctrl+X)", MenuAction::EditCut),
                MenuItem::new('C', "Copy", "Copy the selection to the clipboard (Ctrl+C)", MenuAction::EditCopy),
                MenuItem::new('P', "Paste", "Paste the clipboard at the cursor (Ctrl+V)", MenuAction::EditPaste),
                MenuItem::new('D', "Delete", "Clear the selected cells (Del)", MenuAction::EditClear),
                MenuItem::new('A', "Select-all", "Select every used cell (Ctrl+A)", MenuAction::EditSelectAll),
                MenuItem::new('F', "Find", "Find text in values, formulas and names (Ctrl+F)", MenuAction::EditFind),
                MenuItem::new('N', "Next", "Find the next match (F3)", MenuAction::EditFindNext),
                MenuItem::new('G', "Go-to", "Jump to a cell address or name (Ctrl+G)", MenuAction::EditGoTo),
            ]),
        MenuItem::new('C', "Column", "Insert, delete and size columns", MenuAction::None)
            .with_submenus(vec![
                MenuItem::new('I', "Insert", "Insert a column before the cursor", MenuAction::InsertColumn),
                MenuItem::new('D', "Delete", "Delete the cursor's column", MenuAction::DeleteColumn),
                MenuItem::new('W', "Width", "Set the column width (Ctrl+Shift+Left/Right)", MenuAction::ColumnWidth),
                MenuItem::new('F', "Fit", "Fit the column to its widest value", MenuAction::ColumnFit),

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_menu_navigation_and_first_letter_shortcuts() {
        let mut menu = MenuState::new();
        assert!(!menu.is_active);

        // Press '/' to activate
        menu.open();
        assert!(menu.is_active);

        let (l1, l2) = menu.render_lines();
        assert!(l1.contains("[Worksheet]"));
        assert!(l2.contains("Global spreadsheet settings"));

        // Type 'F' (File menu) -> should jump to File and enter its submenu
        let action = menu.handle_key(Some('F'), false, false, false, false);
        assert_eq!(action, MenuAction::None);

        let (l1_file, _) = menu.render_lines();
        assert!(l1_file.contains("[Save]"));

        // Type 'S' (Save action) -> should trigger FileSave action and close menu
        let save_action = menu.handle_key(Some('S'), false, false, false, false);
        assert_eq!(save_action, MenuAction::FileSave);
        assert!(!menu.is_active);

        // Test /WO (Worksheet -> Orphans)
        menu.open();
        let action = menu.handle_key(Some('W'), false, false, false, false);
        assert_eq!(action, MenuAction::None);
        let orphan_action = menu.handle_key(Some('O'), false, false, false, false);
        assert_eq!(orphan_action, MenuAction::OrphanCheck);
        assert!(!menu.is_active);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_menu_escape_key() {
        let mut menu = MenuState::new();
        menu.open();

        // Enter Worksheet submenu
        menu.handle_key(None, false, false, true, false);
        assert_eq!(menu.breadcrumbs.len(), 2);

        // Esc returns to top level
        menu.handle_key(None, false, false, false, true);
        assert_eq!(menu.breadcrumbs.len(), 1);

        // Esc at top level dismisses menu
        menu.handle_key(None, false, false, false, true);
        assert!(!menu.is_active);
    }

    #[test]
    fn default_menu_tree_is_valid() {
        assert_eq!(validate_menu(&default_lotus_menu()), Ok(()));
    }

    #[test]
    fn validate_menu_rejects_duplicate_hotkeys_on_one_level() {
        let tree = vec![
            MenuItem::new('F', "File", "", MenuAction::None).with_submenus(vec![
                MenuItem::new('S', "Save", "", MenuAction::FileSave),
                MenuItem::new('S', "Save As", "", MenuAction::FileSave),
            ]),
        ];
        let err = validate_menu(&tree).unwrap_err();
        assert!(err.contains("duplicate hotkey 'S'"), "{err}");
        assert!(err.contains("/F"), "{err}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn validate_menu_allows_same_hotkey_on_different_levels() {
        // /FR and /WR share 'R' because they live under different groups.
        let tree = vec![
            MenuItem::new('F', "File", "", MenuAction::None)
                .with_submenus(vec![MenuItem::new('R', "Retrieve", "", MenuAction::FileReload)]),
            MenuItem::new('W', "Worksheet", "", MenuAction::None)
                .with_submenus(vec![MenuItem::new('R', "Recalculate", "", MenuAction::RecalculateAll)]),
        ];
        assert_eq!(validate_menu(&tree), Ok(()));
    }

    #[test]
    fn validate_menu_rejects_dead_leaves_and_groups_with_actions() {
        let dead_leaf = vec![MenuItem::new('X', "Nothing", "", MenuAction::None)];
        assert!(validate_menu(&dead_leaf).unwrap_err().contains("neither an action nor submenus"));

        let busy_group = vec![MenuItem::new('G', "Group", "", MenuAction::Quit)
            .with_submenus(vec![MenuItem::new('Q', "Quit", "", MenuAction::Quit)])];
        assert!(validate_menu(&busy_group).unwrap_err().contains("group but also carries an action"));
    }
}
