//! Font scaling utilities for vector-based UI elements.
//!
//! Provides functions to calculate appropriate text sizes based on available space,
//! content length, and rendering constraints.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{format, string::String, vec::Vec};

use crate::view::HersheyFont;

/// Calculate the optimal font size for a given text to fit within specified dimensions.
///
/// # Arguments
/// * `text` - The text to render
/// * `max_width` - Maximum width available for rendering (in pixels)
/// * `max_height` - Maximum height available for rendering (in pixels)  
/// * `font` - Font to use for measurements
/// * `min_scale` - Minimum scaling factor allowed (default: 0.5)
/// * `max_scale` - Maximum scaling factor allowed (default: 2.0)
///
/// # Returns
/// The appropriate scale factor for the font to fit within constraints.
pub fn calculate_font_scale(
    text: &str,
    max_width: f32,
    max_height: f32,
    font: &HersheyFont,
    min_scale: f32,
    max_scale: f32,
) -> f32 {
    // If no content, return default scale
    if text.is_empty() {
        return 1.0;
    }
    
    let mut best_scale = 1.0;
    let mut current_scale = min_scale;
    
    // Binary search for the optimal scale within bounds  
    while current_scale <= max_scale {
        let (width, height) = font.measure_string(text, current_scale);
        
        if width <= max_width && height <= max_height {
            best_scale = current_scale;
            
            // Try a slightly larger scale to see if it still fits
            let next_scale = current_scale + 0.1;
            if next_scale > max_scale {
                break;
            }
            
            let (next_width, next_height) = font.measure_string(text, next_scale);
            if next_width <= max_width && next_height <= max_height {
                current_scale = next_scale;
            } else {
                // No larger scale fits - we found the optimal
                break;
            }
        } else {
            // Scale too large - try smaller
            let next_scale = current_scale - 0.1;
            if next_scale < min_scale {
                break;
            }
            current_scale = next_scale;
        }
    }
    
    best_scale
}

/// Calculate the dimensions needed for text rendering at a specific scale.
///
/// # Arguments
/// * `text` - The text to measure  
/// * `scale` - Scaling factor to apply to font size
/// * `font` - Font to use for measurements
///
/// # Returns
/// Tuple of (width, height) in pixels needed for rendering the text
pub fn calculate_text_dimensions(
    text: &str,
    scale: f32,
    font: &HersheyFont,
) -> (f32, f32) {
    if text.is_empty() {
        return (0.0, 0.0);
    }
    
    font.measure_string(text, scale)
}

/// Scale text to fit within a given rectangle while maintaining aspect ratio.
///
/// # Arguments
/// * `text` - Text to render
/// * `rect_width` - Width of target rectangle  
/// * `rect_height` - Height of target rectangle
/// * `font` - Font to use for measurements
///
/// # Returns
/// The optimal scale factor and the actual dimensions that will be used
pub fn fit_text_to_rectangle(
    text: &str,
    rect_width: f32,
    rect_height: f32,
    font: &HersheyFont,
) -> (f32, f32, f32) {
    // Start with default scale and calculate how much we can increase it
    let mut current_scale = 1.0;
    
    loop {
        let (width, height) = font.measure_string(text, current_scale);
        
        if width <= rect_width && height <= rect_height {
            // Fits - try to make it as large as possible while still fitting
            let next_scale = current_scale + 0.1;
            let (next_width, next_height) = font.measure_string(text, next_scale);
            
            if next_width <= rect_width && next_height <= rect_height {
                current_scale = next_scale; // Try larger
            } else {
                return (current_scale, width, height); // Found optimal size
            }
        } else {
            // Doesn't fit - need to make smaller 
            let next_scale = current_scale - 0.1;
            if next_scale <= 0.0 {
                return (0.05, rect_width.min(rect_height), rect_height.min(rect_width)); // Minimum scale fallback
            }
            current_scale = next_scale;
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_calculate_font_scale_empty() {
        let font = HersheyFont::load_simplex();
        let scale = calculate_font_scale("", 100.0, 100.0, &font, 0.5, 2.0);
        assert_eq!(scale, 1.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_calculate_text_dimensions() {
        let font = HersheyFont::load_simplex();
        let (width, height) = calculate_text_dimensions("Hello", 1.0, &font);
        assert!(width > 0.0);
        assert!(height > 0.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_fit_text_to_rectangle() {
        let font = HersheyFont::load_simplex();
        let (scale, width, height) = fit_text_to_rectangle("Hello World", 200.0, 50.0, &font);
        assert!(scale > 0.0);
        assert!(width <= 200.0);
        assert!(height <= 50.0);
    }
}

//! Terminal grid rasterizer and visual spreadsheet formatter.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{format, string::String, vec, vec::Vec};

use crate::model::{CellValue, Coord, Sheet, TableOfContents};

pub struct GridRenderer;

impl GridRenderer {
    /// Render a fixed-size window around the active cell.
    pub fn render_viewport(
        sheet: &Sheet,
        toc: Option<&TableOfContents>,
        cursor: Coord,
        viewport_cols: u32,
        viewport_rows: u32,
    ) -> String {
        let cols = viewport_cols.max(1);
        let rows = viewport_rows.max(1);
        let start_col = cursor.col.saturating_sub(cols / 2);
        let start_row = cursor.row.saturating_sub(rows / 2);
        let row_header_width = format!("{}", start_row.saturating_add(rows)).len().max(3) + 1;
        // Fixed-width cells keep the viewport within the terminal width; long values
        // are truncated in-grid while the status line shows the selected cell in full.
        let inner_widths = vec![10usize; cols as usize];

        let mut out = String::new();
        out.push_str(&format!("{:>width$} │", "", width = row_header_width));
        for offset in 0..cols {
            let col = start_col.saturating_add(offset);
            let label: String = Coord::new(col, 0).to_a1().chars().filter(|c| c.is_alphabetic()).collect();
            let width = inner_widths[offset as usize] + 2;
            out.push_str(&format!(" {:^width$} │", label, width = width));
        }
        out.push('\n');
        out.push_str(&format!("{:─>width$}─┼", "", width = row_header_width));
        for width in &inner_widths {
            out.push_str(&format!("{:─>width$}─┼", "", width = width + 2));
        }
        out.push('\n');

        for row_offset in 0..rows {
            let row = start_row.saturating_add(row_offset);
            let marker = if row == cursor.row { ">" } else { " " };
            let row_label = format!("{}{}", marker, row + 1);
            out.push_str(&format!("{:>width$} │", row_label, width = row_header_width));

            for col_offset in 0..cols {
                let col = start_col.saturating_add(col_offset);
                let coord = Coord::new(col, row);
                let inner_width = inner_widths[col_offset as usize];
                let width = inner_width + 2;
                let is_toc = toc.is_some_and(|table| table.pos == coord);
                if is_toc {
                    out.push_str(&format!(" {:^width$} │", "«TOC»", width = width));
                } else if let Some(cell) = sheet.get_cell(coord) {
                    let display = fit_cell(&cell_display_string(&cell.value), inner_width);
                    if coord == cursor {
                        out.push_str(&format!(" [{:<width$}] │", display, width = inner_width));
                    } else {
                        out.push_str(&format!(" {:<width$} │", display, width = width));
                    }
                } else if coord == cursor {
                    out.push_str(&format!(" [{:width$}] │", "", width = inner_width));
                } else {
                    out.push_str(&format!(" {:width$} │", "", width = width));
                }
            }
            out.push('\n');
        }
        out
    }

    /// Renders a worksheet as a formatted ASCII/Unicode terminal grid.
    pub fn render_sheet(sheet: &Sheet, toc: Option<&TableOfContents>, max_display_cols: Option<u32>, max_display_rows: Option<u32>) -> String {
        let (actual_max_col, actual_max_row) = sheet.bounds().unwrap_or((0, 0));
        let toc_col = toc.map(|t| t.pos.col).unwrap_or(0);
        let toc_row = toc.map(|t| t.pos.row).unwrap_or(0);

        let max_col = max_display_cols
            .unwrap_or(actual_max_col.max(4))
            .max(actual_max_col)
            .max(toc_col);
        let max_row = max_display_rows
            .unwrap_or(actual_max_row.max(9))
            .max(actual_max_row)
            .max(toc_row);

        // Determine column widths (default 12 chars)
        let mut col_widths: Vec<usize> = vec![12; (max_col + 1) as usize];

        // Header width for row numbers
        let row_num_width = format!("{}", max_row + 1).len().max(3);

        // Check if any cell content requires wider columns
        for cell in sheet.cells.values() {
            let col = cell.coord.col as usize;
            if col < col_widths.len() {
                let text = cell_display_string(&cell.value);
                if text.len() + 2 > col_widths[col] {
                    col_widths[col] = (text.len() + 2).min(30);
                }
            }
        }

        let mut out = String::new();

        // 1. Column Headers (A, B, C...)
        out.push_str(&format!("{:>width$} │", "", width = row_num_width));
        for col in 0..=max_col {
            let col_name = Coord::new(col, 0).to_a1();
            let col_letter: String = col_name.chars().filter(|c| c.is_alphabetic()).collect();
            let w = col_widths[col as usize];
            out.push_str(&format!(" {:^width$} │", col_letter, width = w));
        }
        out.push('\n');

        // Divider
        out.push_str(&format!("{:─>width$}─┼", "", width = row_num_width));
        for col in 0..=max_col {
            let w = col_widths[col as usize];
            out.push_str(&format!("{:─>width$}─┼", "", width = w));
        }
        out.push('\n');

        // 2. Rows
        for row in 0..=max_row {
            out.push_str(&format!("{:>width$} │", row + 1, width = row_num_width));

            for col in 0..=max_col {
                let coord = Coord::new(col, row);
                let w = col_widths[col as usize];

                let is_toc = toc.map(|t| t.pos == coord).unwrap_or(false);

                if is_toc {
                    out.push_str(&format!(" {:^width$} │", "«TOC»", width = w));
                } else if let Some(cell) = sheet.get_cell(coord) {
                    let display = cell_display_string(&cell.value);
                    let is_numeric = match &cell.value {
                        CellValue::Number(_) => true,
                        CellValue::Formula { cached_num: Some(_), .. } => true,
                        _ => false,
                    };

                    if is_numeric {
                        out.push_str(&format!(" {:>width$} │", display, width = w));
                    } else {
                        out.push_str(&format!(" {:<width$} │", display, width = w));
                    }
                } else {
                    out.push_str(&format!(" {:width$} │", "", width = w));
                }
            }
            out.push('\n');
        }

        out
    }
}

fn fit_cell(value: &str, width: usize) -> String {
    let char_count = value.chars().count();
    if char_count <= width {
        return value.to_string();
    }
    if width == 0 {
        return String::new();
    }
    let mut fitted: String = value.chars().take(width - 1).collect();
    fitted.push('…');
    fitted
}

fn cell_display_string(val: &CellValue) -> String {
    match val {
        CellValue::Empty => String::new(),
        CellValue::Number(n) => format!("{:.2}", n),
        CellValue::Text(t) => t.clone(),
        CellValue::Boolean(b) => (if *b { "TRUE" } else { "FALSE" }).into(),
        CellValue::Formula { cached_num, cached_text, cached_bool, expr } => {
            if let Some(n) = cached_num {
                format!("{:.2}", n)
            } else if let Some(value) = cached_bool {
                (if *value { "TRUE" } else { "FALSE" }).into()
            } else if let Some(ref t) = cached_text {
                t.clone()
            } else {
                format!("={}", expr)
            }
        }
        CellValue::Basic { .. } => "[BASIC]".into(),
        CellValue::Blob { bytes } => format!("[BLOB:{}B]", bytes.len()),
        CellValue::Error(e) => e.clone(),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::model::Cell;

    #[test]
    fn viewport_stays_fixed_and_centers_remote_cursor() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
        let cursor = Coord::new(100, 500);
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::text(cursor, "active"));

        let rendered = GridRenderer::render_viewport(&sheet, None, cursor, 4, 4);
        assert_eq!(rendered.lines().count(), 6);
        assert!(rendered.contains("CW"));
        assert!(rendered.contains("[active"));
        assert!(rendered.contains(">501"));
    }
}

================================================================================

//! High-level GUI primitives and Context Menu built on top of VectorBuffer and Bitmap.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{format, string::String, vec, vec::Vec};

use crate::{
    model::{
        coord::Coord,
        vector::{ColorRgba, Rect2D, VectorBuffer},
    },
    tui::menu::{default_lotus_menu, MenuAction, MenuItem},
    view::{Bitmap, HersheyFont},
};

pub struct Button {
    pub ops: VectorBuffer,
    pub bounds: Rect2D,
    pub cell_ref: Coord,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum ContextMenuAction {
    None,
    Copy,
    Paste,
    Cut,
    Clear,
    Lotus(MenuAction),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct ContextMenuItem {
    pub id: &'static str,
    pub label: &'static str,
    pub shortcut: String,
    pub action: ContextMenuAction,
    pub is_separator: bool,
    pub submenus: Vec<ContextMenuItem>,
}

impl ContextMenuItem {
    pub fn item(label: &'static str, shortcut: impl Into<String>, action: ContextMenuAction) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: label,
            label,
            shortcut: shortcut.into(),
            action,
            is_separator: false,
            submenus: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn with_submenus(label: &'static str, shortcut: impl Into<String>, submenus: Vec<ContextMenuItem>) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: label,
            label,
            shortcut: shortcut.into(),
            action: ContextMenuAction::None,
            is_separator: false,
            submenus,
        }
    }

    pub fn separator() -> Self {
        Self {
            id: "---",
            label: "",
            shortcut: String::new(),
            action: ContextMenuAction::None,
            is_separator: true,
            submenus: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    /// Convert a node of the `/` command tree. `prefix` is the slash path of
    /// the parent (`"/"` at the root); the item's own shortcut is that path
    /// plus its hotkey, so `File -> Save` becomes `/FS` without anyone typing it.
    pub fn from_lotus(item: &MenuItem, prefix: &str) -> Self {
        let mut shortcut = String::from(prefix);
        shortcut.push(item.key);
        if item.submenus.is_empty() {
            Self::item(item.label, shortcut, ContextMenuAction::Lotus(item.action.clone()))
        } else {
            let subs = item.submenus.iter().map(|s| Self::from_lotus(s, &shortcut)).collect();
            Self::with_submenus(item.label, shortcut, subs)
        }
    }
}

/// One open column of the menu: the root list, or the submenu of the item
/// highlighted in the column before it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Panel<'a> {
    pub depth: usize,
    pub rect: Rect2D,
    pub items: &'a [ContextMenuItem],
    pub highlighted: Option<usize>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct ContextMenu {
    pub x: f32,
    pub y: f32,
    pub width: f32,
    pub item_height: f32,
    pub is_visible: bool,
    pub items: Vec<ContextMenuItem>,
    /// Highlighted item at each open level, root first. A level is open when
    /// the item highlighted on the level before it has submenus, so this one
    /// list drives hover, click and rendering at any depth.
    pub path: Vec<usize>,
    /// Window size used to keep panels on screen. `(0.0, 0.0)` means unconstrained.
    pub viewport: (f32, f32),
}

impl ContextMenu {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default_context_menu()
    }

    /// Standard clipboard actions plus the full nested Lotus `/` command tree.
    /// The command part is generated from [`default_lotus_menu`], so the two
    /// menus cannot drift apart.
    pub fn default_context_menu() -> Self {
        Self::from_lotus_menu(&default_lotus_menu())
    }

    /// Build a context menu whose "Lotus Commands" submenu mirrors `root`.
    pub fn from_lotus_menu(root: &[MenuItem]) -> Self {
        let commands = root.iter().map(|m| ContextMenuItem::from_lotus(m, "/")).collect();
        let items = vec![
            ContextMenuItem::item("Cut", "Ctrl+X", ContextMenuAction::Cut),
            ContextMenuItem::item("Copy", "Ctrl+C", ContextMenuAction::Copy),
            ContextMenuItem::item("Paste", "Ctrl+V", ContextMenuAction::Paste),
            ContextMenuItem::item("Clear", "Del", ContextMenuAction::Clear),
            ContextMenuItem::separator(),
            ContextMenuItem::with_submenus("Lotus", "/", commands),
        ];

        Self {
            x: 0.0,
            y: 0.0,
            width: 200.0,
            item_height: 22.0,
            is_visible: false,
            items,
            path: Vec::new(),
            viewport: (0.0, 0.0),
        }
    }

    /// Tell the menu how big the window is so panels can flip and shift to stay visible.
    pub fn set_viewport(&mut self, width: f32, height: f32) {
        self.viewport = (width, height);
    }

    pub fn open_at(&mut self, x: f32, y: f32) {
        let (vw, vh) = self.viewport;
        let h = self.menu_height(&self.items);
        let mut x = x.max(0.0);
        let mut y = y.max(0.0);
        if vw > 0.0 && x + self.width > vw {
            x = (vw - self.width).max(0.0);
        }
        if vh > 0.0 && y + h > vh {
            y = (vh - h).max(0.0);
        }
        self.x = x;
        self.y = y;
        self.is_visible = true;
        self.path.clear();
    }

    pub fn close(&mut self) {
        self.is_visible = false;
        self.path.clear();
    }

    fn row_height(&self, item: &ContextMenuItem) -> f32 {
        if item.is_separator { 6.0 } else { self.item_height }
    }

    pub fn menu_height(&self, items: &[ContextMenuItem]) -> f32 {
        let mut total = 8.0; // padding
        for item in items {
            total += self.row_height(item);
        }
        total
    }

    pub fn main_bounds(&self) -> Rect2D {
        let h = self.menu_height(&self.items);
        Rect2D::new(self.x, self.y, self.x + self.width, self.y + h)
    }

    /// Item under the pointer in the root list, if any (kept for callers that
    /// only care about the first level).
    pub fn hovered_idx(&self) -> Option<usize> {
        self.path.first().copied()
    }

    /// Root item whose submenu is currently open, if any.
    pub fn active_submenu_idx(&self) -> Option<usize> {
        let i = *self.path.first()?;
        if self.items.get(i)?.submenus.is_empty() { None } else { Some(i) }
    }

    /// Every open panel, root first. Submenus open to the right of their
    /// parent, flip to its left edge when they would leave the window, and
    /// shift up when they would run off the bottom.
    pub fn panels(&self) -> Vec<Panel<'_>> {
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        if !self.is_visible {
            return out;
        }
        let main = self.main_bounds();
        let mut rect = main;
        let mut items: &[ContextMenuItem] = &self.items;
        let mut depth = 0;
        loop {
            let highlighted = self.path.get(depth).copied().filter(|&i| i < items.len());
            out.push(Panel { depth, rect, items, highlighted });

            let Some(hi) = highlighted else { break };
            let item = &items[hi];
            if item.submenus.is_empty() {
                break;
            }

            let mut item_y = rect.min_y + 4.0;
            for it in &items[..hi] {
                item_y += self.row_height(it);
            }
            let mut nx = rect.max_x - 4.0;
            if self.viewport.0 > 0.0 && nx + self.width > self.viewport.0 {
                nx = (rect.min_x - self.width + 4.0).max(0.0);
            }
            let nh = self.menu_height(&item.submenus);
            let mut ny = item_y;
            if self.viewport.1 > 0.0 && ny + nh > self.viewport.1 {
                ny = (self.viewport.1 - nh).max(0.0);
            }
            rect = Rect2D::new(nx, ny, nx + self.width, ny + nh);
            items = &item.submenus;
            depth += 1;
        }
        out
    }

    /// The first submenu panel, if one is open.
    pub fn submenu_bounds(&self) -> Option<(Rect2D, &[ContextMenuItem])> {
        self.panels().into_iter().nth(1).map(|p| (p.rect, p.items))
    }

    fn item_at(&self, panel: &Panel<'_>, my: f32) -> Option<usize> {
        let mut cur_y = panel.rect.min_y + 4.0;
        for (i, item) in panel.items.iter().enumerate() {
            let h = self.row_height(item);
            if my >= cur_y && my < cur_y + h && !item.is_separator {
                return Some(i);
            }
            cur_y += h;
        }
        None
    }

    /// Deepest panel under the point, with the item under it (None over padding or a separator).
    fn hit(&self, mx: f32, my: f32) -> Option<(usize, Option<usize>)> {
        for p in self.panels().iter().rev() {
            let r = &p.rect;
            if mx >= r.min_x && mx <= r.max_x && my >= r.min_y && my <= r.max_y {
                return Some((p.depth, self.item_at(p, my)));
            }
        }
        None
    }

    /// Update the highlight path from the pointer position.
    pub fn handle_mouse_move(&mut self, mx: f32, my: f32) {
        if !self.is_visible {
            return;
        }
        if let Some((depth, idx)) = self.hit(mx, my) {
            self.path.truncate(depth);
            if let Some(i) = idx {
                self.path.push(i);
            }
        }
    }

    /// Process a mouse click. Returns an action if an actionable item was selected.
    /// Clicking a group opens it; clicking outside every panel closes the menu.
    pub fn handle_mouse_click(&mut self, mx: f32, my: f32) -> Option<ContextMenuAction> {
        if !self.is_visible {
            return None;
        }
        let Some((depth, idx)) = self.hit(mx, my) else {
            self.close();
            return None;
        };
        let i = idx?;

        let (has_submenus, action) = {
            let panels = self.panels();
            let item = &panels[depth].items[i];
            (!item.submenus.is_empty(), item.action.clone())
        };
        self.path.truncate(depth);
        self.path.push(i);

        if has_submenus || action == ContextMenuAction::None {
            return None;
        }
        self.close();
        Some(action)
    }

    /// Render every open panel onto the bitmap and vector overlay buffer.
    pub fn render(&self, bitmap: &mut Bitmap, vec_buf: &mut VectorBuffer, font: &HersheyFont) {
        for panel in self.panels() {
            self.draw_panel(&panel, bitmap, vec_buf, font);
        }
    }

    fn draw_panel(&self, panel: &Panel<'_>, bitmap: &mut Bitmap, vec_buf: &mut VectorBuffer, font: &HersheyFont) {
        let r = panel.rect;
        let px = r.min_x as usize;
        let py = r.min_y as usize;
        let pw = r.width() as usize;
        let ph = r.height() as usize;

        // Drop shadow, then the classic 3D face (0x00D4D0C8)
        bitmap.fill_rect(px + 3, py + 3, pw, ph, 0x00202020);
        bitmap.fill_rect(px, py, pw, ph, 0x00D4D0C8);

        // 3D bevel: white top/left, dark bottom/right
        vec_buf.set_width(1.0);
        vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::WHITE);
        vec_buf.move_to(r.min_x, r.max_y);
        vec_buf.line_to(r.min_x, r.min_y);
        vec_buf.line_to(r.max_x, r.min_y);
        vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(64, 64, 64));
        vec_buf.line_to(r.max_x, r.max_y);
        vec_buf.line_to(r.min_x, r.max_y);

        let mut cur_y = r.min_y + 4.0;
        for (i, item) in panel.items.iter().enumerate() {
            if item.is_separator {
                let sep_y = (cur_y + 2.0) as usize;
                bitmap.fill_rect(px + 4, sep_y, pw.saturating_sub(8), 1, 0x00808080);
                bitmap.fill_rect(px + 4, sep_y + 1, pw.saturating_sub(8), 1, 0x00FFFFFF);
                cur_y += 6.0;
                continue;
            }

            let is_hot = panel.highlighted == Some(i);
            if is_hot {
                // Highlight bar: classic navy blue
                bitmap.fill_rect(px + 3, cur_y as usize, pw.saturating_sub(6), self.item_height as usize, 0x00000080);
            }
            let text_color = if is_hot { ColorRgba::WHITE } else { ColorRgba::BLACK };
            let hint_color = if is_hot { ColorRgba::WHITE } else { ColorRgba::rgb(255, 0, 0) };

            vec_buf.set_color(text_color);
            vec_buf.set_width(1.0);
            font.draw_string(item.label, r.min_x + 12.0, cur_y + 15.0, 0.70, vec_buf);

            if !item.shortcut.is_empty() {
                let (sw, _) = font.measure_string(&item.shortcut, 0.65);
                let margin = if item.submenus.is_empty() { 12.0 } else { 20.0 };
                vec_buf.set_color(hint_color);
                font.draw_string(&item.shortcut, r.max_x - sw - margin, cur_y + 15.0, 0.65, vec_buf);
            }

            if !item.submenus.is_empty() {
                vec_buf.set_color(text_color);
                font.draw_string(">", r.max_x - 14.0, cur_y + 15.0, 0.70, vec_buf);
            }

            cur_y += self.item_height;
        }
    }
}

pub struct GeoWorks;

impl GeoWorks {
    /// Renders a classic beveled window.
    pub fn window(x: f32, y: f32, w: f32, h: f32, title: &str, font: &HersheyFont) -> VectorBuffer {
        let mut buf = VectorBuffer::new();

        // Outer Border (Shadow/Light effect)
        buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(0, 0, 0));
        buf.set_width(2.0);
        buf.rect(x, y, w, h);

        buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(255, 255, 255));
        buf.rect(x + 1.0, y + 1.0, w - 2.0, h - 2.0);

        // Title Bar
        buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(0, 0, 128));
        buf.set_width(1.0);
        buf.rect(x + 2.0, y + 2.0, w - 4.0, 20.0);

        // Title Text
        buf.set_color(ColorRgba::WHITE);
        buf.set_width(1.0);
        font.draw_string(title, x + 10.0, y + 14.0, 0.8, &mut buf);

        buf
    }

    /// Renders a beveled button.
    pub fn button(x: f32, y: f32, w: f32, h: f32, label: &str, font: &HersheyFont, cell: Coord) -> Button {
        let mut buf = VectorBuffer::new();

        buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(0, 0, 0));
        buf.set_width(1.0);
        buf.rect(x, y, w, h);

        buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(255, 255, 255));
        buf.rect(x + 1.0, y + 1.0, w - 2.0, h - 2.0);

        buf.set_color(ColorRgba::BLACK);
        buf.set_width(1.0);
        font.draw_string(label, x + (w / 2.0) - 20.0, y + (h / 2.0), 0.8, &mut buf);

        Button {
            ops: buf,
            bounds: Rect2D::new(x, y, x + w, y + h),
            cell_ref: cell,
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    /// Vertical centre of row `idx` in a panel with no separators.
    fn row_y(rect: Rect2D, idx: usize, item_height: f32) -> f32 {
        rect.min_y + 4.0 + idx as f32 * item_height + item_height / 2.0
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_context_menu_default_structure() {
        let menu = ContextMenu::default_context_menu();
        assert_eq!(menu.items.len(), 6);
        assert_eq!(menu.items[0].label, "Cut");
        assert_eq!(menu.items[0].shortcut, "Ctrl+X");
        assert_eq!(menu.items[1].label, "Copy");
        assert_eq!(menu.items[1].shortcut, "Ctrl+C");
        assert_eq!(menu.items[2].label, "Paste");
        assert_eq!(menu.items[2].shortcut, "Ctrl+V");
        assert_eq!(menu.items[3].label, "Clear");
        assert_eq!(menu.items[3].shortcut, "Del");
        assert!(menu.items[4].is_separator);
        assert_eq!(menu.items[5].label, "Lotus");
        assert_eq!(menu.items[5].submenus.len(), default_lotus_menu().len(), "one entry per top-level command");
        assert_eq!(menu.items[5].submenus[0].label, "Worksheet");

        // Check nested File -> Save (/FS)
        let file_menu = menu.items[5].submenus.iter().find(|m| m.label == "File").unwrap();
        let save_item = file_menu.submenus.iter().find(|m| m.label == "Save").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(save_item.shortcut, "/FS");
        assert_eq!(save_item.action, ContextMenuAction::Lotus(MenuAction::FileSave));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_context_menu_mouse_interaction() {
        let mut menu = ContextMenu::default_context_menu();
        menu.open_at(100.0, 100.0);
        assert!(menu.is_visible);

        // Hover over "Copy" item (item 1 at y = 100 + 4 + 22 = 126)
        menu.handle_mouse_move(150.0, 130.0);
        assert_eq!(menu.hovered_idx(), Some(1));

        // Click on "Copy"
        let action = menu.handle_mouse_click(150.0, 130.0);
        assert_eq!(action, Some(ContextMenuAction::Copy));
        assert!(!menu.is_visible); // Closes on action

        // Reopen and click outside
        menu.open_at(100.0, 100.0);
        let outside_action = menu.handle_mouse_click(10.0, 10.0);
        assert_eq!(outside_action, None);
        assert!(!menu.is_visible);
    }

    #[test]
    fn nested_commands_are_reachable_by_mouse_at_every_level() {
        let mut menu = ContextMenu::default_context_menu();
        menu.open_at(80.0, 120.0);

        // Level 0 -> "Lotus Commands" (row 5, after a 6px separator)
        let p0 = menu.panels()[0].rect;
        let lotus_y = p0.min_y + 4.0 + 4.0 * 22.0 + 6.0 + 11.0;
        menu.handle_mouse_move(100.0, lotus_y);
        assert_eq!(menu.path, vec![5]);
        assert_eq!(menu.panels().len(), 2);

        // Level 1 -> "File" (wherever the command tree puts it)
        let file_row = menu.items[5].submenus.iter().position(|m| m.label == "File").unwrap();
        let p1 = menu.panels()[1].rect;
        menu.handle_mouse_move(p1.min_x + 20.0, row_y(p1, file_row, 22.0));
        assert_eq!(menu.path, vec![5, file_row]);
        assert_eq!(menu.panels().len(), 3, "File's submenu must open as a third panel");

        // Level 2 -> "Save" (row 0) triggers /FS
        let p2 = menu.panels()[2].rect;
        assert!(p2.min_x > p1.min_x);
        let action = menu.handle_mouse_click(p2.min_x + 20.0, row_y(p2, 0, 22.0));
        assert_eq!(action, Some(ContextMenuAction::Lotus(MenuAction::FileSave)));
        assert!(!menu.is_visible);
    }

    #[test]
    fn clicking_a_group_opens_it_without_closing_the_menu() {
        let mut menu = ContextMenu::default_context_menu();
        menu.open_at(80.0, 120.0);
        let p0 = menu.panels()[0].rect;
        let lotus_y = p0.min_y + 4.0 + 4.0 * 22.0 + 6.0 + 11.0;

        assert_eq!(menu.handle_mouse_click(100.0, lotus_y), None);
        assert!(menu.is_visible);
        assert_eq!(menu.active_submenu_idx(), Some(5));

        let p1 = menu.panels()[1].rect;
        assert_eq!(menu.handle_mouse_click(p1.min_x + 20.0, row_y(p1, 0, 22.0)), None);
        assert!(menu.is_visible);
        assert_eq!(menu.path, vec![5, 0]);
        assert_eq!(menu.panels().len(), 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn hovering_a_plain_item_closes_open_submenus() {
        let mut menu = ContextMenu::default_context_menu();
        menu.open_at(80.0, 120.0);
        let p0 = menu.panels()[0].rect;
        menu.handle_mouse_move(100.0, p0.min_y + 4.0 + 4.0 * 22.0 + 6.0 + 11.0);
        assert_eq!(menu.panels().len(), 2);

        menu.handle_mouse_move(100.0, row_y(p0, 1, 22.0)); // Copy
        assert_eq!(menu.path, vec![1]);
        assert_eq!(menu.panels().len(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn panels_stay_inside_the_viewport() {
        let mut menu = ContextMenu::default_context_menu();
        menu.set_viewport(800.0, 600.0);
        menu.open_at(790.0, 590.0);

        let main = menu.main_bounds();
        assert!(main.max_x <= 800.0 && main.max_y <= 600.0, "root panel clamped: {main:?}");

        let p0 = menu.panels()[0].rect;
        menu.handle_mouse_move(p0.min_x + 20.0, p0.min_y + 4.0 + 4.0 * 22.0 + 6.0 + 11.0);
        let p1 = menu.panels()[1].rect;
        assert!(p1.max_x <= 800.0 && p1.min_x >= 0.0);
        assert!(p1.max_y <= 600.0);
        assert!(p1.max_x <= p0.max_x, "submenu flips to the left of its parent near the right edge");
    }

    #[test]
    fn context_menu_is_generated_from_the_command_tree() {
        let tree = vec![
            MenuItem::new('T', "Tools", "", MenuAction::None).with_submenus(vec![
                MenuItem::new('X', "Export", "", MenuAction::FileInspect),
            ]),
        ];
        let menu = ContextMenu::from_lotus_menu(&tree);
        let commands = &menu.items[5].submenus;
        assert_eq!(commands.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(commands[0].shortcut, "/T");
        assert_eq!(commands[0].submenus[0].shortcut, "/TX");
        assert_eq!(commands[0].submenus[0].action, ContextMenuAction::Lotus(MenuAction::FileInspect));
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_slash_command_appears_in_the_context_menu() {
        fn leaves(items: &[MenuItem], out: &mut Vec<MenuAction>) {
            for i in items {
                if i.submenus.is_empty() { out.push(i.action.clone()); } else { leaves(&i.submenus, out); }
            }
        }
        fn ctx_leaves(items: &[ContextMenuItem], out: &mut Vec<MenuAction>) {
            for i in items {
                if i.submenus.is_empty() {
                    if let ContextMenuAction::Lotus(a) = &i.action { out.push(a.clone()); }
                } else { ctx_leaves(&i.submenus, out); }
            }
        }
        let mut slash = Vec::new();
        leaves(&default_lotus_menu(), &mut slash);
        let mut ctx = Vec::new();
        ctx_leaves(&ContextMenu::default_context_menu().items, &mut ctx);
        assert_eq!(slash, ctx);
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-core/src/view/hershey.rs
================================================================================
//! Hershey vector stroke font engine.
//!
//! Decodes Dr. A. V. Hershey's 1967 public domain stroke fonts (Simplex Roman)
//! into continuous vector path operations without external dependencies or font libraries.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{vec, vec::Vec};

use crate::model::vector::VectorBuffer;

/// Raw embedded Hershey Simplex Roman font definition (JHF format).
pub const HERSHEY_ROMAN_SIMPLEX_JHF: &str = include_str!("rowmans.jhf");

/// A decoded Hershey glyph stroke segment (a continuous pen-down stroke).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct StrokeSegment {
    pub points: Vec<(i8, i8)>,
}

/// A decoded Hershey font glyph with horizontal metrics and stroke segments.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct HersheyGlyph {
    pub glyph_id: u32,
    pub left: i8,
    pub right: i8,
    pub strokes: Vec<StrokeSegment>,
}

impl HersheyGlyph {
    /// Width metric in font units.
    pub fn advance_width(&self) -> f32 {
        (self.right - self.left).max(1) as f32
    }
}

/// A parsed Hershey font set for printable ASCII (32..=126).
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct HersheyFont {
    glyphs: [Option<HersheyGlyph>; 128],
}

impl HersheyFont {
    /// Parse and load the built-in Roman Simplex font set.
    pub fn load_simplex() -> Self {
        let mut glyphs: [Option<HersheyGlyph>; 128] = core::array::from_fn(|_| None);
        let mut current_ascii = 32u8; // Starts at Space (' ')

        // JHF lines can wrap long glyphs across lines.
        // A new glyph line begins with 5 chars (digits/spaces) for glyph ID,
        // then 3 chars for pair count, then 1 char for left, 1 for right.
        let mut full_glyph_str = Vec::new();

        for line in HERSHEY_ROMAN_SIMPLEX_JHF.lines() {
            let line = line.trim_end();
            if line.is_empty() {
                continue;
            }

            // Check if this line is a continuation line or a new glyph
            // A new glyph line has at least 10 characters and columns 0..5 are numeric/whitespace ID
            let is_new_glyph = line.len() >= 10
                && line[..5].chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit() || c == ' ')
                && line[5..8].chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit() || c == ' ')
                && line[..5].trim().parse::<u32>().is_ok();

            if is_new_glyph && !full_glyph_str.is_empty() {
                // Parse previous completed glyph
                let combined = full_glyph_str.join("");
                if let Some(glyph) = parse_jhf_glyph(&combined) {
                    if current_ascii < 128 {
                        glyphs[current_ascii as usize] = Some(glyph);
                        current_ascii += 1;
                    }
                }
                full_glyph_str.clear();
            }

            full_glyph_str.push(line);
        }

        // Parse the final glyph
        if !full_glyph_str.is_empty() {
            let combined = full_glyph_str.join("");
            if let Some(glyph) = parse_jhf_glyph(&combined) {
                if current_ascii < 128 {
                    glyphs[current_ascii as usize] = Some(glyph);
                }
            }
        }

        Self { glyphs }
    }

    /// Retrieve the glyph for an ASCII character.
    pub fn get_glyph(&self, ch: char) -> Option<&HersheyGlyph> {
        let byte = ch as usize;
        if byte < 128 {
            self.glyphs[byte].as_ref()
        } else {
            None
        }
    }

    /// Append the strokes of a single character into a vector buffer.
    ///
    /// Returns the horizontal advance width in pixels.
    pub fn draw_char(
        &self,
        ch: char,
        origin_x: f32,
        origin_y: f32,
        scale: f32,
        buf: &mut VectorBuffer,
    ) -> f32 {
        let glyph = match self.get_glyph(ch) {
            Some(g) => g,
            None => {
                // Fallback for missing glyphs: advance equivalent to space
                return 16.0 * scale;
            }
        };

        let left_offset = glyph.left as f32;

        for stroke in &glyph.strokes {
            let mut is_first = true;
            for &(px, py) in &stroke.points {
                // Hershey coordinate origin: X centered at 0, Y goes positive DOWN
                let screen_x = origin_x + (px as f32 - left_offset) * scale;
                let screen_y = origin_y + (py as f32) * scale;

                if is_first {
                    buf.move_to(screen_x, screen_y);
                    is_first = false;
                } else {
                    buf.line_to(screen_x, screen_y);
                }
            }
        }

        glyph.advance_width() * scale
    }

    /// Append the strokes of a text string into a vector buffer.
    ///
    /// Returns the total horizontal advance width in pixels.
    pub fn draw_string(
        &self,
        text: &str,
        start_x: f32,
        start_y: f32,
        scale: f32,
        buf: &mut VectorBuffer,
    ) -> f32 {
        let mut current_x = start_x;
        for ch in text.chars() {
            if ch == '\n' {
                // Newlines handled by caller or line advancement
                continue;
            }
            let advance = self.draw_char(ch, current_x, start_y, scale, buf);
            current_x += advance;
        }
        current_x - start_x
    }

    /// Measure the width and height of a text string in pixels at the given scale.
    pub fn measure_string(&self, text: &str, scale: f32) -> (f32, f32) {
        let mut width = 0.0f32;
        for ch in text.chars() {
            if let Some(glyph) = self.get_glyph(ch) {
                width += glyph.advance_width() * scale;
            } else {
                width += 16.0 * scale;
            }
        }
        // Hershey standard vertical range is roughly -12 to +14 = 26 units
        let height = 26.0 * scale;
        (width, height)
    }
}

/// Parse a single JHF glyph record.
fn parse_jhf_glyph(raw: &str) -> Option<HersheyGlyph> {
    if raw.len() < 10 {
        return None;
    }

    let glyph_id = raw[..5].trim().parse::<u32>().ok()?;
    let pair_count = raw[5..8].trim().parse::<usize>().ok()?;

    let bytes = raw.as_bytes();
    let left = (bytes[8] as i8).wrapping_sub(b'R' as i8);
    let right = (bytes[9] as i8).wrapping_sub(b'R' as i8);

    let coord_bytes = &bytes[10..];
    let mut strokes = Vec::new();
    let mut current_stroke = Vec::new();

    let mut i = 0;
    let max_pairs = pair_count.saturating_sub(1); // First pair was left/right margin

    while i + 1 < coord_bytes.len() && strokes.len() + (if current_stroke.is_empty() { 0 } else { 1 }) <= max_pairs + 10 {
        let cx = coord_bytes[i];
        let cy = coord_bytes[i + 1];
        i += 2;

        if cx == b' ' && cy == b'R' {
            // Pen up sentinel
            if !current_stroke.is_empty() {
                strokes.push(StrokeSegment { points: current_stroke });
                current_stroke = Vec::new();
            }
        } else {
            let px = (cx as i8).wrapping_sub(b'R' as i8);
            let py = (cy as i8).wrapping_sub(b'R' as i8);
            current_stroke.push((px, py));
        }
    }

    if !current_stroke.is_empty() {
        strokes.push(StrokeSegment { points: current_stroke });
    }

    Some(HersheyGlyph {
        glyph_id,
        left,
        right,
        strokes,
    })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_hershey_simplex_loads_all_ascii() {
        let font = HersheyFont::load_simplex();

        // Printable ASCII 32..=126
        for ascii in 32u8..=126u8 {
            let ch = ascii as char;
            let glyph = font.get_glyph(ch);
            assert!(
                glyph.is_some(),
                "Glyph for ASCII '{}' ({}) should be loaded",
                ch,
                ascii
            );
            let g = glyph.unwrap();
            assert!(
                g.advance_width() > 0.0,
                "Glyph '{}' advance width should be > 0",
                ch
            );
        }

        // Test specific known characters
        let space = font.get_glyph(' ').unwrap();
        assert_eq!(space.glyph_id, 699);
        assert!(space.strokes.is_empty()); // Space has no visible strokes

        let letter_a = font.get_glyph('A').unwrap();
        assert_eq!(letter_a.glyph_id, 501);
        assert!(!letter_a.strokes.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_hershey_string_rendering() {
        let font = HersheyFont::load_simplex();
        let mut buf = VectorBuffer::new();

        let width = font.draw_string("Lotus 5-7-11", 0.0, 0.0, 1.0, &mut buf);
        assert!(width > 50.0, "Rendered text width should be positive and realistic");
        assert!(!buf.is_empty(), "Vector buffer should contain strokes");

        let (mw, mh) = font.measure_string("Lotus 5-7-11", 1.0);
        assert!((mw - width).abs() < 1.0, "Measured width should match drawn width");
        assert!(mh > 10.0, "Measured height should be positive");
    }
}

//! Software subpixel anti-aliased rasterizer and bitmap framebuffer surface.
//!
//! Implements ClearType-style subpixel horizontal anti-aliasing with FIR energy filtering
//! for razor-sharp rendering of Hershey stroke letterforms and vector paths directly onto
//! linear 32-bit pixel surfaces (`&mut [u32]`) without GPU or external C dependencies.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{vec, vec::Vec};

use crate::model::vector::{ColorRgba, Point2D, VectorBuffer, VectorOp};

/// 2D affine transform matrix for panning and zooming.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct Transform2D {
    pub pan_x: f32,
    pub pan_y: f32,
    pub zoom: f32,
}

impl Transform2D {
    pub const fn identity() -> Self {
        Self {
            pan_x: 0.0,
            pan_y: 0.0,
            zoom: 1.0,
        }
    }

    pub fn new(pan_x: f32, pan_y: f32, zoom: f32) -> Self {
        Self { pan_x, pan_y, zoom: zoom.max(0.01) }
    }

    /// Map a world point to screen coordinates.
    pub fn transform_point(&self, pt: Point2D) -> Point2D {
        Point2D::new(
            (pt.x - self.pan_x) * self.zoom,
            (pt.y - self.pan_y) * self.zoom,
        )
    }
}

/// A 32-bit pixel bitmap surface (`0x00RRGGBB`).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Bitmap {
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
    pub pitch: usize,
    pub pixels: Vec<u32>,
}

impl Bitmap {
    /// Create a new bitmap buffer initialized to black (0x00000000).
    pub fn new(width: usize, height: usize) -> Self {
        let size = width * height;
        Self {
            width,
            height,
            pitch: width,
            pixels: vec![0u32; size],
        }
    }

    /// Clear the entire bitmap with a solid 0x00RRGGBB color.
    pub fn clear(&mut self, color: u32) {
        self.pixels.fill(color);
    }

    /// Fill a rectangle with a solid 0x00RRGGBB color.
    pub fn fill_rect(&mut self, x: usize, y: usize, w: usize, h: usize, color: u32) {
        if x >= self.width || y >= self.height || w == 0 || h == 0 {
            return;
        }
        let max_x = (x + w).min(self.width);
        let max_y = (y + h).min(self.height);
        for row in y..max_y {
            let start = row * self.pitch + x;
            let end = row * self.pitch + max_x;
            if start < self.pixels.len() && end <= self.pixels.len() {
                self.pixels[start..end].fill(color);
            }
        }
    }

    /// Set a pixel with bounds checking.
    pub fn set_pixel(&mut self, x: usize, y: usize, color: u32) {
        if x < self.width && y < self.height {
            self.pixels[y * self.pitch + x] = color;
        }
    }

    /// Get a pixel with bounds checking.
    pub fn get_pixel(&self, x: usize, y: usize) -> Option<u32> {
        if x < self.width && y < self.height {
            Some(self.pixels[y * self.pitch + x])
        } else {
            None
        }
    }

    /// Export the bitmap to an uncompressed 24-bit BMP image in memory.
    pub fn to_bmp_bytes(&self) -> Vec<u8> {
        let width = self.width as u32;
        let height = self.height as u32;
        let row_padding = (4 - ((width * 3) % 4)) % 4;
        let image_size = (width * 3 + row_padding) * height;
        let file_size = 54 + image_size;

        let mut bmp = Vec::with_capacity(file_size as usize);

        // --- BMP Header (14 bytes) ---
        bmp.extend_from_slice(b"BM");
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&file_size.to_le_bytes());
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&0u32.to_le_bytes()); // Reserved
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&54u32.to_le_bytes()); // Offset to pixel array

        // --- DIB Header (BITMAPINFOHEADER - 40 bytes) ---
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&40u32.to_le_bytes()); // DIB Header size
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&(width as i32).to_le_bytes());
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&(height as i32).to_le_bytes()); // Bottom-up bitmap
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&1u16.to_le_bytes()); // 1 color plane
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&24u16.to_le_bytes()); // 24 bits per pixel (BGR)
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&0u32.to_le_bytes()); // BI_RGB (uncompressed)
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&image_size.to_le_bytes());
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&2835u32.to_le_bytes()); // Horizontal resolution (72 DPI)
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&2835u32.to_le_bytes()); // Vertical resolution (72 DPI)
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&0u32.to_le_bytes()); // Colors in palette
        bmp.extend_from_slice(&0u32.to_le_bytes()); // Important colors

        // --- Pixel Data (Bottom-Up BGR) ---
        for y in (0..self.height).rev() {
            let row_offset = y * self.pitch;
            for x in 0..self.width {
                let pixel = self.pixels[row_offset + x];
                let r = ((pixel >> 16) & 0xFF) as u8;
                let g = ((pixel >> 8) & 0xFF) as u8;
                let b = (pixel & 0xFF) as u8;
                bmp.push(b);
                bmp.push(g);
                bmp.push(r);
            }
            for _ in 0..row_padding {
                bmp.push(0);
            }
        }

        bmp
    }
}

/// Software rasterizer implementing subpixel anti-aliased stroke rendering.
pub struct SubpixelRasterizer;

impl SubpixelRasterizer {
    /// Render a vector buffer onto a target bitmap with full subpixel ClearType filtering.
    pub fn render(
        bitmap: &mut Bitmap,
        vector_buf: &VectorBuffer,
        transform: &Transform2D,
    ) {
        if bitmap.width == 0 || bitmap.height == 0 || vector_buf.is_empty() {
            return;
        }

        let mut cur_pt = Point2D::new(0.0, 0.0);
        let mut poly_start = Point2D::new(0.0, 0.0);
        let mut cur_color = ColorRgba::WHITE;
        let mut cur_width = 1.0f32;

        for op in &vector_buf.ops {
            match op {
                VectorOp::MoveTo(p) => {
                    cur_pt = transform.transform_point(*p);
                    poly_start = cur_pt;
                }
                VectorOp::LineTo(p) => {
                    let next_pt = transform.transform_point(*p);
                    Self::draw_subpixel_line(bitmap, cur_pt, next_pt, cur_width * transform.zoom, cur_color);
                    cur_pt = next_pt;
                }
                VectorOp::SetColor(c) => {
                    cur_color = *c;
                }
                VectorOp::SetWidth(w) => {
                    cur_width = *w;
                }
                VectorOp::ClosePoly => {
                    Self::draw_subpixel_line(bitmap, cur_pt, poly_start, cur_width * transform.zoom, cur_color);
                    cur_pt = poly_start;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Draw a line with 3x horizontal subpixel anti-aliasing and FIR energy filtering.
    pub fn draw_subpixel_line(
        bitmap: &mut Bitmap,
        p0: Point2D,
        p1: Point2D,
        width: f32,
        color: ColorRgba,
    ) {
        let width = width.max(0.75);
        let radius = width * 0.5;

        // Subpixel horizontal coordinate space: X is multiplied by 3
        let sub_width = (bitmap.width * 3) as i32;
        let bmp_height = bitmap.height as i32;

        let p0_sub = Point2D::new(p0.x * 3.0, p0.y);
        let p1_sub = Point2D::new(p1.x * 3.0, p1.y);
        let sub_radius_x = radius * 3.0;

        // Bounding box in subpixel coordinates
        let min_sub_x = ((p0_sub.x.min(p1_sub.x) - sub_radius_x - 2.0).floor() as i32).max(0);
        let max_sub_x = ((p0_sub.x.max(p1_sub.x) + sub_radius_x + 2.0).ceil() as i32).min(sub_width - 1);
        let min_y = ((p0_sub.y.min(p1_sub.y) - radius - 1.0).floor() as i32).max(0);
        let max_y = ((p0_sub.y.max(p1_sub.y) + radius + 1.0).ceil() as i32).min(bmp_height - 1);

        if min_sub_x > max_sub_x || min_y > max_y {
            return;
        }

        let dx = p1_sub.x - p0_sub.x;
        let dy = p1_sub.y - p0_sub.y;
        let len_sq = dx * dx + dy * dy;

        // Buffer for one scanline of subpixel coverages
        let sub_span_len = (max_sub_x - min_sub_x + 3) as usize;
        let mut sub_coverage = vec![0u8; sub_span_len];

        for y_idx in min_y..=max_y {
            let py = y_idx as f32 + 0.5;
            sub_coverage.fill(0);

            for sub_x_idx in min_sub_x..=max_sub_x {
                let px = sub_x_idx as f32 + 0.5;

                // Distance from point (px, py) to segment (p0_sub, p1_sub) in normalized metric
                let dist = if len_sq < 0.0001 {
                    let ddx = (px - p0_sub.x) / 3.0;
                    let ddy = py - p0_sub.y;
                    (ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy).sqrt()
                } else {
                    let t = (((px - p0_sub.x) * dx + (py - p0_sub.y) * dy) / len_sq).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
                    let proj_x = p0_sub.x + t * dx;
                    let proj_y = p0_sub.y + t * dy;
                    let ddx = (px - proj_x) / 3.0;
                    let ddy = py - proj_y;
                    (ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy).sqrt()
                };

                let d = dist - radius;
                let cov = if d <= -0.5 {
                    255u8
                } else if d >= 0.5 {
                    0u8
                } else {
                    ((0.5 - d) * 255.0).clamp(0.0, 255.0) as u8
                };

                sub_coverage[(sub_x_idx - min_sub_x) as usize] = cov;
            }

            // Apply ClearType FIR filter across subpixels [0.25, 0.50, 0.25]
            let start_pixel_x = min_sub_x / 3;
            let end_pixel_x = max_sub_x / 3;

            for px_idx in start_pixel_x..=end_pixel_x {
                if px_idx < 0 || px_idx >= bitmap.width as i32 {
                    continue;
                }

                let sub_base = (px_idx * 3) - min_sub_x;
                let get_cov = |offset: i32| -> u32 {
                    let idx = sub_base + offset;
                    if idx >= 0 && (idx as usize) < sub_coverage.len() {
                        sub_coverage[idx as usize] as u32
                    } else {
                        0
                    }
                };

                // FIR filter: R channel centers on 3X, G on 3X+1, B on 3X+2
                let r_cov = (get_cov(-1) + 2 * get_cov(0) + get_cov(1)) >> 2;
                let g_cov = (get_cov(0) + 2 * get_cov(1) + get_cov(2)) >> 2;
                let b_cov = (get_cov(1) + 2 * get_cov(2) + get_cov(3)) >> 2;

                if r_cov == 0 && g_cov == 0 && b_cov == 0 {
                    continue;
                }

                let dest_idx = (y_idx as usize) * bitmap.pitch + (px_idx as usize);
                let bg = bitmap.pixels[dest_idx];
                let bg_r = ((bg >> 16) & 0xFF) as u32;
                let bg_g = ((bg >> 8) & 0xFF) as u32;
                let bg_b = (bg & 0xFF) as u32;

                let fg_r = color.r as u32;
                let fg_g = color.g as u32;
                let fg_b = color.b as u32;

                // Alpha blend each channel with its subpixel coverage
                let out_r = (fg_r * r_cov + bg_r * (255 - r_cov)) / 255;
                let out_g = (fg_g * g_cov + bg_g * (255 - g_cov)) / 255;
                let out_b = (fg_b * b_cov + bg_b * (255 - b_cov)) / 255;

                bitmap.pixels[dest_idx] = (out_r << 16) | (out_g << 8) | out_b;
            }
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_bitmap_creation_and_bmp_export() {
        let mut bmp = Bitmap::new(64, 32);
        bmp.clear(0x00112233);
        assert_eq!(bmp.get_pixel(0, 0), Some(0x00112233));
        assert_eq!(bmp.get_pixel(63, 31), Some(0x00112233));
        assert_eq!(bmp.get_pixel(64, 32), None);

        let bmp_bytes = bmp.to_bmp_bytes();
        assert!(bmp_bytes.len() > 54);
        assert_eq!(&bmp_bytes[0..2], b"BM");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_subpixel_line_rasterization() {
        let mut bmp = Bitmap::new(50, 50);
        bmp.clear(0x00000000); // Black background

        let mut buf = VectorBuffer::new();
        buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(255, 255, 255)); // White line
        buf.set_width(1.0);
        buf.move_to(10.0, 10.0);
        buf.line_to(40.0, 10.0); // Horizontal line

        SubpixelRasterizer::render(&mut bmp, &buf, &Transform2D::identity());

        // Verify that pixels along the line at y=10 are illuminated
        let center_pixel = bmp.get_pixel(25, 10).unwrap();
        assert!(center_pixel > 0, "Pixel along line should be illuminated");

        // Verify that pixels far away are untouched
        let distant_pixel = bmp.get_pixel(25, 30).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(distant_pixel, 0, "Distant pixel should remain black");
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-core/src/view/workspace_view.rs
