//! Sovereign vector workspace screen renderer and spatial hit-tester.
//!
//! Renders the classic Lotus 1-2-3 two-line control panel, dynamic column and row headers,
//! reactive spreadsheet cells, multi-cell spans, and context menu directly to `Bitmap` and `VectorBuffer`.

#[cfg(not(feature = "std"))]
use alloc::{format, string::String, vec, vec::Vec};

use crate::{
    model::{
        coord::Coord,
        sheet::Sheet,
        vector::{ColorRgba, Rect2D, VectorBuffer},
        CellValue,
    },
    tui::editor::WorkspaceSession,
    view::{gui::ContextMenu, Bitmap, HersheyFont, SubpixelRasterizer, Transform2D},
};

pub struct VectorWorkspaceView {
    pub header_row_height: f32,
    pub header_col_width: f32,
    pub control_panel_height: f32,
    pub font_scale: f32,
    pub scroll_col: u32,
    pub scroll_row: u32,
}

impl Default for VectorWorkspaceView {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            header_row_height: 24.0,
            header_col_width: 50.0,
            control_panel_height: 48.0, // 2 lines: 24px status + 24px edit/menu
            font_scale: 0.75,
            scroll_col: 0,
            scroll_row: 0,
        }
    }
}

impl VectorWorkspaceView {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// Pan/move the viewport by delta columns and delta rows.
    pub fn pan(&mut self, delta_col: i32, delta_row: i32) {
        let new_col = (self.scroll_col as i64 + i64::from(delta_col)).clamp(0, 16_383) as u32;
        let new_row = (self.scroll_row as i64 + i64::from(delta_row)).clamp(0, 1_048_575) as u32;
        self.scroll_col = new_col;
        self.scroll_row = new_row;
    }

    /// Scroll the minimum distance needed so `coord` is fully inside a window
    /// of `view_w` x `view_h` pixels. A cell wider than the window is aligned
    /// to its left edge instead.
    pub fn ensure_visible(&mut self, sheet: &Sheet, coord: Coord, view_w: f32, view_h: f32) {
        if coord.col < self.scroll_col {
            self.scroll_col = coord.col;
        } else {
            while self.scroll_col < coord.col && self.cell_rect(sheet, coord).max_x > view_w {
                self.scroll_col += 1;
            }
        }
        if coord.row < self.scroll_row {
            self.scroll_row = coord.row;
        } else {
            while self.scroll_row < coord.row && self.cell_rect(sheet, coord).max_y > view_h {
                self.scroll_row += 1;
            }
        }
    }

    /// How many default-height rows fit in a window `view_h` pixels tall.
    pub fn rows_per_page(&self, sheet: &Sheet, view_h: f32) -> u32 {
        let grid_h = (view_h - self.control_panel_height - self.header_row_height).max(0.0);
        ((grid_h / sheet.default_row_height.max(1.0)) as u32).max(1)
    }

    /// Calculate the top-left (x, y) and (width, height) bounding rectangle of a grid cell
    /// relative to the current viewport scroll position.
    pub fn cell_rect(&self, sheet: &Sheet, coord: Coord) -> Rect2D {
        let mut x = self.header_col_width;
        if coord.col >= self.scroll_col {
            for c in self.scroll_col..coord.col {
                x += sheet.col_width(c) as f32;
            }
        } else {
            for c in coord.col..self.scroll_col {
                x -= sheet.col_width(c) as f32;
            }
        }

        let mut y = self.control_panel_height + self.header_row_height;
        if coord.row >= self.scroll_row {
            for r in self.scroll_row..coord.row {
                y += sheet.row_height(r) as f32;
            }
        } else {
            for r in coord.row..self.scroll_row {
                y -= sheet.row_height(r) as f32;
            }
        }

        let cell = sheet.get_cell(coord);
        let col_span = cell.map(|c| c.col_span).unwrap_or(1);
        let row_span = cell.map(|c| c.row_span).unwrap_or(1);

        let mut w = 0.0;
        for c in coord.col..(coord.col + col_span) {
            w += sheet.col_width(c) as f32;
        }

        let mut h = 0.0;
        for r in coord.row..(coord.row + row_span) {
            h += sheet.row_height(r) as f32;
        }

        Rect2D::new(x, y, x + w, y + h)
    }

    /// Convert a mouse screen position (x, y) to a spreadsheet `Coord`, accounting for viewport scroll.
    pub fn hit_test_cell(&self, sheet: &Sheet, screen_x: f32, screen_y: f32) -> Option<Coord> {
        let grid_min_x = self.header_col_width;
        let grid_min_y = self.control_panel_height + self.header_row_height;

        if screen_x < grid_min_x || screen_y < grid_min_y {
            return None;
        }

        // Determine column starting from scroll_col
        let mut cur_x = grid_min_x;
        let mut target_col = None;
        for c in self.scroll_col..(self.scroll_col + 1000) {
            let w = sheet.col_width(c) as f32;
            if screen_x >= cur_x && screen_x < cur_x + w {
                target_col = Some(c);
                break;
            }
            cur_x += w;
            if cur_x > screen_x + 500.0 {
                break;
            }
        }

        // Determine row starting from scroll_row
        let mut cur_y = grid_min_y;
        let mut target_row = None;
        for r in self.scroll_row..(self.scroll_row + 1000) {
            let h = sheet.row_height(r) as f32;
            if screen_y >= cur_y && screen_y < cur_y + h {
                target_row = Some(r);
                break;
            }
            cur_y += h;
            if cur_y > screen_y + 500.0 {
                break;
            }
        }

        match (target_col, target_row) {
            (Some(c), Some(r)) => Some(Coord::new(c, r)),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// Renders the complete sovereign workspace scene onto the provided bitmap and vector buffer.
    pub fn render(
        &self,
        sheet: &Sheet,
        session: &WorkspaceSession,
        context_menu: &ContextMenu,
        bitmap: &mut Bitmap,
        vec_buf: &mut VectorBuffer,
        font: &HersheyFont,
    ) {
        let bmp_w = bitmap.width as f32;
        let bmp_h = bitmap.height as f32;

        // 1. Workspace Background: Dark Slate (0x000E141E)
        bitmap.clear(0x000E141E);

        // 2. Control Panel (Top 48px)
        // Line 1: Status Line (0x0016202C)
        bitmap.fill_rect(0, 0, bitmap.width, 24, 0x0016202C);
        // Line 2: Edit / Menu Line (0x001E2B3B)
        bitmap.fill_rect(0, 24, bitmap.width, 24, 0x001E2B3B);

        // Divider between control panel and grid
        bitmap.fill_rect(0, 48, bitmap.width, 1, 0x003A4D66);

        // --- Render Control Panel Text ---
        // Line 1: Sheet title, Coord, Name, Value, Status
        let cell_info = match sheet.get_cell(session.cursor) {
            Some(cell) => {
                let name_tag = cell.name.as_deref().map(|n| format!(" ({})", n)).unwrap_or_default();
                let val_str = match &cell.value {
                    CellValue::Formula { expr, cached_num, cached_text, cached_bool } => {
                        let cached = cached_num.map(|n| format!("{:.2}", n))
                            .or_else(|| cached_bool.map(|b| if b { "TRUE".into() } else { "FALSE".into() }))
                            .or_else(|| cached_text.clone())
                            .unwrap_or_else(|| "<pending>".into());
                        format!("{} => {}", expr, cached)
                    }
                    CellValue::Number(n) => format!("{:.2}", n),
                    CellValue::Text(t) => format!("\"{}\"", t),
                    CellValue::Boolean(b) => (if *b { "TRUE" } else { "FALSE" }).into(),
                    CellValue::Basic { .. } => "BASIC Script".into(),
                    CellValue::Blob { bytes } => format!("Blob ({} bytes)", bytes.len()),
                    CellValue::Error(e) => format!("ERR: {}", e),
                    CellValue::Empty => "<empty>".into(),
                };
                format!("{} | {} | {}", session.cursor.to_a1(), name_tag, val_str)
            }
            None => format!("{} | <empty>", session.cursor.to_a1()),
        };

        let mode_str = if session.menu.is_active {
            "[MENU]"
        } else if session.edit_prompt.is_some() {
            "[PROMPT]"
        } else if session.edit_buffer.is_some() {
            "[EDIT]"
        } else if session.extend_mode {
            "[EXTEND]"
        } else if session.selection().is_some() {
            "[SELECT]"
        } else {
            "[READY]"
        };

        vec_buf.set_width(1.0);
        vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(180, 210, 240));
        let l1_text = format!("{} | Sheet: {}  {}", mode_str, sheet.name, cell_info);
        font.draw_string(&l1_text, 10.0, 16.0, 0.70, vec_buf);

        // Line 2: Live Edit or Lotus '/' Menu
        if session.menu.is_active {
            let (menu_l1, _menu_l2) = session.menu.render_lines();
            vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(0, 230, 160)); // Cyan / Lotus Accent
            font.draw_string(&menu_l1, 10.0, 40.0, 0.70, vec_buf);
        } else if let Some(ref edit_text) = session.edit_buffer {
            let prefix = session.edit_prompt.as_deref().unwrap_or("EDIT: ");
            let (pw, _) = font.measure_string(prefix, 0.75);

            // Highlight selected text range in Navy Blue behind text
            if let Some((sel_start, sel_end)) = session.selection_range() {
                let start_idx = sel_start.min(edit_text.len());
                let end_idx = sel_end.min(edit_text.len());
                let before_sel = &edit_text[..start_idx];
                let (before_w, _) = font.measure_string(before_sel, 0.75);
                let sel_str = &edit_text[start_idx..end_idx];
                let (sel_w, _) = font.measure_string(sel_str, 0.75);

                let highlight_x = (10.0 + pw + before_w) as usize;
                let highlight_y = 26;
                bitmap.fill_rect(highlight_x, highlight_y, (sel_w.ceil() as usize).max(2), 20, 0x00000080);
            }

            vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(255, 204, 0)); // Bright Amber for edit line
            font.draw_string(prefix, 10.0, 40.0, 0.75, vec_buf);

            let text_x = 10.0 + pw;
            font.draw_string(edit_text, text_x, 40.0, 0.75, vec_buf);

            // Draw vertical text cursor
            let cur_idx = session.edit_cursor.min(edit_text.len());
            let before_cur = &edit_text[..cur_idx];
            let (cur_offset, _) = font.measure_string(before_cur, 0.75);
            let cur_x = text_x + cur_offset;
            vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::WHITE);
            vec_buf.set_width(1.5);
            vec_buf.move_to(cur_x, 26.0);
            vec_buf.line_to(cur_x, 44.0);
        } else {
            vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(140, 160, 180));
            let message = session.status_message.as_str();
            if message.is_empty() || message == "READY" {
                font.draw_string("READY (Press '/' for Lotus Menu, Right-Click for Context Menu)", 10.0, 40.0, 0.65, vec_buf);
            } else {
                font.draw_string(message, 10.0, 40.0, 0.65, vec_buf);
            }
        }

        // 3. Headers & Grid Dimensions
        let grid_y0 = self.control_panel_height + self.header_row_height;

        // Header Backgrounds: Classic Header Tint (0x001B2430)
        bitmap.fill_rect(0, self.control_panel_height as usize, bitmap.width, self.header_row_height as usize, 0x001B2430);
        bitmap.fill_rect(0, self.control_panel_height as usize, self.header_col_width as usize, bitmap.height - self.control_panel_height as usize, 0x001B2430);

        // Top-left corner box
        bitmap.fill_rect(0, self.control_panel_height as usize, self.header_col_width as usize, self.header_row_height as usize, 0x00243040);

        // 4. Draw Column Headers (starting from self.scroll_col)
        let mut cur_col_x = self.header_col_width;
        let mut visible_cols = Vec::new();
        let mut c = self.scroll_col;
        while cur_col_x < bmp_w && c < 16_384 {
            let w = sheet.col_width(c) as f32;
            let col_name = Coord::new(c, 0).to_a1();
            let col_letter = col_name.trim_end_matches(|ch: char| ch.is_ascii_digit());

            // Header separator line
            bitmap.fill_rect((cur_col_x + w) as usize, self.control_panel_height as usize, 1, bitmap.height - self.control_panel_height as usize, 0x00283648);

            // Centered Column Header text
            let (tw, _) = font.measure_string(col_letter, 0.70);
            let tx = cur_col_x + (w - tw) * 0.5;
            let ty = self.control_panel_height + 17.0;
            vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(200, 220, 240));
            font.draw_string(col_letter, tx, ty, 0.70, vec_buf);

            visible_cols.push(c);
            cur_col_x += w;
            c += 1;
        }

        // 5. Draw Row Headers (starting from self.scroll_row)
        let mut cur_row_y = grid_y0;
        let mut visible_rows = Vec::new();
        let mut r = self.scroll_row;
        while cur_row_y < bmp_h && r < 1_048_576 {
            let h = sheet.row_height(r) as f32;
            let row_str = format!("{}", r + 1);

            // Row separator line
            bitmap.fill_rect(0, (cur_row_y + h) as usize, bitmap.width, 1, 0x00283648);

            // Centered Row Header text
            let (rw, _) = font.measure_string(&row_str, 0.70);
            let rx = (self.header_col_width - rw) * 0.5;
            let ry = cur_row_y + (h * 0.5) + 5.0;
            vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(200, 220, 240));
            font.draw_string(&row_str, rx, ry, 0.70, vec_buf);

            visible_rows.push(r);
            cur_row_y += h;
            r += 1;
        }

        // 5b. Highlight the selected range behind the cells.
        if let Some(range) = session.selection() {
            for &r in &visible_rows {
                for &c in &visible_cols {
                    if range.contains(Coord::new(c, r)) {
                        let rect = self.cell_rect(sheet, Coord::new(c, r));
                        let x0 = rect.min_x.max(self.header_col_width);
                        let y0 = rect.min_y.max(grid_y0);
                        let x1 = rect.max_x.min(bmp_w);
                        let y1 = rect.max_y.min(bmp_h);
                        if x1 > x0 && y1 > y0 {
                            bitmap.fill_rect(x0 as usize, y0 as usize, (x1 - x0) as usize, (y1 - y0) as usize, 0x00183050);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 6. Render Spreadsheet Cells
        for &r in &visible_rows {
            for &c in &visible_cols {
                let coord = Coord::new(c, r);
                if let Some(cell) = sheet.get_cell(coord) {
                    let rect = self.cell_rect(sheet, coord);

                    // Cell content string
                    let display_val = cell.value.display_text();

                    // Text needs room: collapsed rows and columns show no text.
                    if !display_val.is_empty() && rect.height() >= 20.0 && rect.width() >= 16.0 {
                        let text_color = match &cell.value {
                            CellValue::Formula { .. } => ColorRgba::rgb(100, 220, 255), // Cyan for formulas
                            CellValue::Number(_) => ColorRgba::rgb(230, 240, 255),
                            _ => ColorRgba::rgb(210, 220, 230),
                        };
                        let numeric = matches!(&cell.value, CellValue::Number(_))
                            || matches!(&cell.value, CellValue::Formula { cached_num: Some(_), .. });
                        let shown = fit_text(font, &display_val, self.font_scale, rect.width() - 10.0, numeric);

                        vec_buf.set_color(text_color);
                        let text_x = rect.min_x + 6.0;
                        let text_y = rect.min_y + 16.0;
                        font.draw_string(&shown, text_x, text_y, self.font_scale, vec_buf);
                    }

                    // Check for obfuscated data indicator (red notch)
                    if sheet.cell_obfuscates_data(coord) {
                        let notch_x = (rect.max_x - 3.0) as usize;
                        let notch_y = rect.min_y as usize;
                        bitmap.fill_rect(notch_x, notch_y, 3, rect.height() as usize, 0x00FF3333);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 7. Active Cell Selection Cursor Box
        let cursor_rect = self.cell_rect(sheet, session.cursor);
        if cursor_rect.max_x > self.header_col_width && cursor_rect.max_y > grid_y0
            && cursor_rect.min_x < bmp_w && cursor_rect.min_y < bmp_h
        {
            vec_buf.set_width(2.0);
            vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(0, 230, 160)); // Lotus Cyan Focus Box
            vec_buf.rect(cursor_rect.min_x, cursor_rect.min_y, cursor_rect.width(), cursor_rect.height());

            // Inner soft glow line
            vec_buf.set_width(1.0);
            vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::WHITE);
            vec_buf.rect(cursor_rect.min_x + 1.0, cursor_rect.min_y + 1.0, cursor_rect.width() - 2.0, cursor_rect.height() - 2.0);
        }

        // 8. Rasterize Vector Paths (strokes, headers, cell text) onto the bitmap
        SubpixelRasterizer::render(bitmap, vec_buf, &Transform2D::identity());
        vec_buf.clear();

        // 9. Context Menu Overlay (if visible, drawn on top of the rendered scene)
        if context_menu.is_visible {
            context_menu.render(bitmap, vec_buf, font);
            SubpixelRasterizer::render(bitmap, vec_buf, &Transform2D::identity());
            vec_buf.clear();
        }
    }
}

/// `text` cut down to fit `max_w` pixels. A number that cannot fit shows as
/// `####` rather than a misleading prefix of its digits.
fn fit_text(font: &HersheyFont, text: &str, scale: f32, max_w: f32, numeric: bool) -> String {
    if max_w <= 0.0 {
        return String::new();
    }
    let (full, _) = font.measure_string(text, scale);
    if full <= max_w {
        return text.into();
    }
    if numeric {
        let (hash_w, _) = font.measure_string("#", scale);
        let n = if hash_w > 0.0 { (max_w / hash_w) as usize } else { 0 };
        return "#".repeat(n);
    }
    let mut out = String::new();
    let mut used = 0.0;
    for ch in text.chars() {
        let mut buf = [0u8; 4];
        let (w, _) = font.measure_string(ch.encode_utf8(&mut buf), scale);
        if used + w > max_w {
            break;
        }
        used += w;
        out.push(ch);
    }
    out
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::model::Cell;

    #[test]
    fn test_workspace_view_cell_rect_and_hit_test() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("s1", "Main");
        sheet.set_col_width(0, 100.0);
        sheet.set_col_width(1, 120.0);
        sheet.set_row_height(0, 30.0);
        sheet.set_row_height(1, 40.0);

        let view = VectorWorkspaceView::new();

        // Header col = 50.0, Control panel = 48.0, Header row = 24.0 -> grid origin (50.0, 72.0)
        let rect_a1 = view.cell_rect(&sheet, Coord::new(0, 0));
        assert_eq!(rect_a1.min_x, 50.0);
        assert_eq!(rect_a1.min_y, 72.0);
        assert_eq!(rect_a1.width(), 100.0);
        assert_eq!(rect_a1.height(), 30.0);

        let rect_b2 = view.cell_rect(&sheet, Coord::new(1, 1));
        assert_eq!(rect_b2.min_x, 150.0); // 50 + 100
        assert_eq!(rect_b2.min_y, 102.0); // 72 + 30
        assert_eq!(rect_b2.width(), 120.0);
        assert_eq!(rect_b2.height(), 40.0);

        // Hit testing
        assert_eq!(view.hit_test_cell(&sheet, 60.0, 80.0), Some(Coord::new(0, 0)));
        assert_eq!(view.hit_test_cell(&sheet, 160.0, 110.0), Some(Coord::new(1, 1)));
        assert_eq!(view.hit_test_cell(&sheet, 10.0, 10.0), None); // In control panel / headers
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_workspace_view_render_produces_pixels() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("s1", "Main");
        sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 1234.5).with_name("rev"));
        let session = WorkspaceSession::new();
        let context_menu = ContextMenu::new();
        let font = HersheyFont::load_simplex();

        let view = VectorWorkspaceView::new();
        let mut bitmap = Bitmap::new(400, 300);
        let mut vec_buf = VectorBuffer::new();

        view.render(&sheet, &session, &context_menu, &mut bitmap, &mut vec_buf, &font);

        let lit_pixels = bitmap.pixels.iter().filter(|&&p| p != 0).count();
        assert!(lit_pixels > 1000, "Workspace render should illuminate pixels");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_shift_view_panning() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("s1", "Main");
        sheet.set_col_width(0, 100.0);
        sheet.set_col_width(1, 100.0);
        sheet.set_col_width(2, 100.0);
        sheet.set_row_height(0, 30.0);
        sheet.set_row_height(1, 30.0);

        let mut view = VectorWorkspaceView::new();
        assert_eq!(view.scroll_col, 0);
        assert_eq!(view.scroll_row, 0);

        // Cell C1 at (2, 0): when scroll_col = 0, x = 50 + 100 + 100 = 250
        assert_eq!(view.cell_rect(&sheet, Coord::new(2, 0)).min_x, 250.0);

        // Pan view by 1 column right (Shift+Right)
        view.pan(1, 0);
        assert_eq!(view.scroll_col, 1);
        // Cell C1 now appears at x = 50 + 100 = 150 (shifted left on screen)
        assert_eq!(view.cell_rect(&sheet, Coord::new(2, 0)).min_x, 150.0);

        // Pan view down by 1 row (Shift+Down)
        view.pan(0, 1);
        assert_eq!(view.scroll_row, 1);
        // Cell A2 at (0, 1) now appears at grid origin y = 72
        assert_eq!(view.cell_rect(&sheet, Coord::new(0, 1)).min_y, 72.0);

        // Pan back
        view.pan(-1, -1);
        assert_eq!(view.scroll_col, 0);
        assert_eq!(view.scroll_row, 0);
    }

    // ---- scrolling to the cursor, clipping, status, selection --------------

    #[test]
    fn ensure_visible_scrolls_only_as_far_as_needed() {
        let sheet = Sheet::new("s", "S");
        let mut view = VectorWorkspaceView::new();

        view.ensure_visible(&sheet, Coord::new(2, 3), 800.0, 600.0);
        assert_eq!((view.scroll_col, view.scroll_row), (0, 0), "already visible: no scroll");

        view.ensure_visible(&sheet, Coord::new(30, 0), 800.0, 600.0);
        assert!(view.scroll_col > 0);
        assert!(view.cell_rect(&sheet, Coord::new(30, 0)).max_x <= 800.0);
        assert_eq!(view.cell_rect(&sheet, Coord::new(30, 0)).max_x > 700.0, true, "scrolled just enough, not more");

        view.ensure_visible(&sheet, Coord::new(0, 50), 800.0, 600.0);
        assert!(view.cell_rect(&sheet, Coord::new(0, 50)).max_y <= 600.0);

        view.ensure_visible(&sheet, Coord::new(0, 0), 800.0, 600.0);
        assert_eq!((view.scroll_col, view.scroll_row), (0, 0), "back to the corner");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_cell_wider_than_the_window_is_aligned_to_its_left_edge() {
        let mut sheet = Sheet::new("s", "S");
        sheet.set_col_width(5, 1_000.0);
        let mut view = VectorWorkspaceView::new();
        view.ensure_visible(&sheet, Coord::new(5, 0), 400.0, 600.0);
        assert_eq!(view.scroll_col, 5);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_page_is_the_number_of_default_rows_that_fit() {
        let sheet = Sheet::new("s", "S");
        let view = VectorWorkspaceView::new();
        assert_eq!(view.rows_per_page(&sheet, 600.0), 22); // (600 - 48 - 24) / 24
        assert_eq!(view.rows_per_page(&sheet, 10.0), 1, "never zero");
    }

    #[test]
    fn fit_text_clips_text_and_hashes_numbers_that_do_not_fit() {
        let font = HersheyFont::load_simplex();
        let scale = 0.75;
        let (full, _) = font.measure_string("Projection Summary", scale);

        assert_eq!(fit_text(&font, "Projection Summary", scale, full + 1.0, false), "Projection Summary");
        let clipped = fit_text(&font, "Projection Summary", scale, full / 2.0, false);
        assert!(!clipped.is_empty() && clipped.len() < "Projection Summary".len());
        assert!("Projection Summary".starts_with(&clipped));
        assert!(font.measure_string(&clipped, scale).0 <= full / 2.0);

        let hashes = fit_text(&font, "12345678.00", scale, 30.0, true);
        assert!(!hashes.is_empty() && hashes.chars().all(|c| c == '#'), "{hashes:?}");
        assert_eq!(fit_text(&font, "anything", scale, 0.0, false), "");
    }

    /// Only the grid area of a frame (below the 72px control panel), so a test about
    /// what the grid draws is not disturbed by the info line above it.
    fn grid_of(frame: &[u32]) -> Vec<u32> {
        frame[72 * 800..].to_vec()
    }

    fn frame(session: &WorkspaceSession, sheet: &Sheet, view: &VectorWorkspaceView) -> Vec<u32> {
        let font = HersheyFont::load_simplex();
        let mut bitmap = Bitmap::new(800, 600);
        let mut vec_buf = VectorBuffer::with_capacity(1024);
        let menu = ContextMenu::default_context_menu();
        view.render(sheet, session, &menu, &mut bitmap, &mut vec_buf, &font);
        bitmap.pixels.clone()
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_status_message_is_drawn_and_the_hint_returns_when_it_is_cleared() {
        let sheet = Sheet::new("s", "S");
        let view = VectorWorkspaceView::new();
        let mut session = WorkspaceSession::new();
        session.status_message = "READY".into();
        let idle = frame(&session, &sheet, &view);

        session.status_message = "Saved workbook to demo.lotus".into();
        assert!(frame(&session, &sheet, &view) != idle, "the message shows");

        session.status_message = "READY".into();
        assert!(frame(&session, &sheet, &view) == idle, "back to the hint line");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_selected_range_is_highlighted_behind_the_cells() {
        let sheet = Sheet::new("s", "S");
        let view = VectorWorkspaceView::new();
        let mut session = WorkspaceSession::new();
        session.cursor = Coord::new(2, 4); // C5
        let plain = frame(&session, &sheet, &view);

        session.selection_anchor = Some(Coord::new(3, 5)); // D6
        let selected = frame(&session, &sheet, &view);
        assert!(selected != plain, "selecting changes the picture");

        let r = view.cell_rect(&sheet, Coord::new(3, 5));
        let (x, y) = (((r.min_x + r.max_x) / 2.0) as usize, ((r.min_y + r.max_y) / 2.0) as usize);
        assert_eq!(selected[y * 800 + x], 0x00183050, "the far corner cell is tinted");
        assert!(plain[y * 800 + x] != 0x00183050);
    }

    #[test]
    fn collapsed_rows_and_columns_draw_no_text() {
        let view = VectorWorkspaceView::new();
        let session = WorkspaceSession::new();
        let mut with_text = Sheet::new("s", "S");
        with_text.set_cell(Cell::text(Coord::new(0, 0), "visible"));
        let mut without = Sheet::new("s", "S");
        let grid = |sheet: &Sheet| grid_of(&frame(&session, sheet, &view));

        // sanity: at normal size the text shows up in the grid
        assert!(grid(&with_text) != grid(&without), "text is drawn at normal size");

        // collapse the row: the grid is the same with or without the text
        with_text.set_row_height(0, 8.0);
        without.set_row_height(0, 8.0);
        assert!(grid(&with_text) == grid(&without), "a collapsed row draws no text");

        // and for a collapsed column
        with_text.set_row_height(0, 24.0);
        without.set_row_height(0, 24.0);
        with_text.set_col_width(0, 10.0);
        without.set_col_width(0, 10.0);
        assert!(grid(&with_text) == grid(&without), "a collapsed column draws no text");
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-core/src/zlayer/mod.rs
================================================================================
//! Z-layer management system for AI integration.
//!
//! Handles the three AI edit paths:
//! 1. Direct file modification (z-layers appended to file)
//! 2. Chat-based updates (paste z-layers into UI)
//! 3. API integration (submit via OpenAI-like interface)

pub mod manifest;
pub mod chaining;

use alloc::vec::Vec;
use crate::model::coord::Coord;

pub use manifest::{Manifest, ManifestEntry, LayerStatus};
pub use chaining::{MerkleChain, ChainNode, TombstoneType, Hash, generate_salt};

/// Z-layer identifier with turn and index for addressing
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct LayerAddress {
    pub turn: u32,
    pub index: u32,
}

impl LayerAddress {
    pub fn new(turn: u32, index: u32) -> Self {
        Self { turn, index }
    }
}

/// Z-layer with metadata about its status and origin
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ZLayer {
    pub address: LayerAddress,
    pub content: Vec<(Coord, Option<crate::model::cell::Cell>)>,
    pub status: LayerStatus,
    /// Optional salt for Merkle chaining (128-bit minimum)
    pub salt: [u8; 16],
}

impl ZLayer {
    pub fn new(address: LayerAddress, content: Vec<(Coord, Option<crate::model::cell::Cell>)>) -> Self {
        let mut salt = [0u8; 16];
        // In a real implementation this would be properly randomized
        for i in 0..16 {
            salt[i] = (address.turn as u8 + address.index as u8 + i as u8) % 256;
        }
        
        Self { 
            address, 
            content,
            status: LayerStatus::Pending,
            salt
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-core/tests/edge.rs
================================================================================
use lotus_core::*;

fn rt(wb: &Workbook) -> Result<Workbook, String> {
    let bytes = serialize_workbook(wb);
    deserialize_workbook(&bytes).map_err(|e| format!("{:?}", e))
}

fn sheet_with(cells: Vec<Cell>) -> Workbook {
    let mut s = Sheet::new("main", "Main");
    for c in cells { s.set_cell(c); }
    Workbook::new("t", "t").with_sheet(s)
}

fn report(label: &str, original: &Workbook) {
    let back = rt(original).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{label}: round trip parse failed: {e}"));
    assert_eq!(&back, original, "round trip mismatch: {label}");
}

#[test]
fn edge_cases() {
    let c = |col, row| Coord::new(col, row);

    report("BASIC source containing closing tags",
        &sheet_with(vec![Cell::basic(c(0,0), "s.bas", "SUB A()\n  LET x = \"</cell>\"\nEND SUB")]));

    let doc_wb = Workbook::new("t","t").with_document(Document::new("d","d","before </document> after"));
    report("Document body containing closing tag", &doc_wb);

    report("Text with leading/trailing space",   &sheet_with(vec![Cell::text(c(0,0), "  padded  ")]));
    report("Text with newlines",                  &sheet_with(vec![Cell::text(c(0,0), "line1\nline2\n")]));
    report("Text with CRLF",                      &sheet_with(vec![Cell::text(c(0,0), "a\r\nb")]));
    report("Text with & < > and unicode",         &sheet_with(vec![Cell::text(c(0,0), "R&D <5 > 3 — café 日本")]));
    report("Text that looks like entity",         &sheet_with(vec![Cell::text(c(0,0), "&amp; literally")]));
    report("Empty text",                          &sheet_with(vec![Cell::text(c(0,0), "")]));

    for (l, n) in [("NaN", f64::NAN), ("+inf", f64::INFINITY), ("-inf", f64::NEG_INFINITY),
                   ("0.1+0.2", 0.1+0.2), ("1e300", 1e300), ("-0.0", -0.0), ("1/3", 1.0/3.0), ("5e-324", 5e-324)] {
        // NaN != NaN so compare via bits
        let wb = sheet_with(vec![Cell::number(c(0,0), n)]);
        match rt(&wb) {
            Err(e) => println!("[FAIL-PARSE] number {l}: {e}"),
            Ok(b) => {
                let got = b.primary_sheet().unwrap().get_cell(c(0,0)).map(|x| x.value.clone());
                let same = matches!(&got, Some(CellValue::Number(m)) if m.to_bits()==n.to_bits());
                println!("[{}] number {l}: got {:?}", if same {"ok"} else {"MISMATCH"}, got);
            }
        }
    }

    report("Blob with </cell> + NUL + invalid UTF-8",
        &sheet_with(vec![Cell::blob(c(0,0), "b.bin", b"x</cell>\0\xff\xfe<cell ref=\"Z9\" type=\"number\">1</cell>".to_vec())]));
    report("Empty blob", &sheet_with(vec![Cell::blob(c(0,0), "e.bin", vec![])]));

    report("Formula with quotes", &sheet_with(vec![Cell::formula(c(0,0), "=IF(1>0,\"a\",\"b\")")]));
    report("Name with quote/newline", &sheet_with(vec![Cell::number(c(0,0), 1.0).with_name("we\"ird\nname")]));
    report("Workbook title with newline", &{ let mut w = sheet_with(vec![]); w.title = "a\nb\tc".into(); w });
    report("Boolean", &sheet_with(vec![Cell::boolean(c(0,0), true)]));
    report("Error cell", &sheet_with(vec![Cell::new(c(0,0), CellValue::Error("#DIV/0!".into()))]));
    report("Empty cell", &sheet_with(vec![Cell::new(c(0,0), CellValue::Empty)]));

    // Large coordinates (sparse) — TOC position / A1 conversion
    let big = sheet_with(vec![Cell::number(c(16383, 1048575), 1.0)]);
    report("Cell at XFD1048576", &big);
    println!("    TOC at: {}", big.compute_toc().pos.to_a1());

    // Duplicate names
    let dup = sheet_with(vec![Cell::text(c(0,0), "first").with_name("x"), Cell::text(c(1,0), "second").with_name("x")]);
    println!("[info] duplicate name 'x' eject -> {:?}", dup.eject_cell("x").map(|b| String::from_utf8_lossy(b).to_string()));
}

#[test]
fn literal_entities_in_text_and_names() {
    let c = |col, row| Coord::new(col, row);
    report("Text that is literally '&lt;'", &sheet_with(vec![Cell::text(c(0,0), "&lt;b&gt; &quot;x&quot; &amp;amp;")]));
    report("Cached formula text with & <", &sheet_with(vec![Cell::new(c(0,0), CellValue::Formula{expr:"=A2".into(), cached_num:None, cached_text:Some("R&D <5".into()), cached_bool:None})]));
    report("Numeric-looking cached formula text", &sheet_with(vec![Cell::new(c(0,0), CellValue::Formula{expr:"=\"123\"".into(), cached_num:None, cached_text:Some("123".into()), cached_bool:None})]));
    report("Whitespace cached formula text", &sheet_with(vec![Cell::new(c(0,0), CellValue::Formula{expr:"=\" x \"".into(), cached_num:None, cached_text:Some("  x  ".into()), cached_bool:None})]));
    report("Workbook title with quote & amp", &{ let mut w = sheet_with(vec![]); w.title = "Q&A \"2026\"".into(); w });
}

use lotus_core::{
    deserialize_workbook, serialize_workbook, Bitmap, Cell, CellValue, ColorRgba, Coord,
    HersheyFont, Sheet, SubpixelRasterizer, Transform2D, VectorBuffer, Workbook,
};

#[test]
fn test_hershey_text_bitmap_rasterization_and_bmp_export() {
    let font = HersheyFont::load_simplex();
    let mut vec_buf = VectorBuffer::new();

    vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(255, 255, 255));
    vec_buf.set_width(1.0);

    let text = "Lotus 5-7-11";
    let advance = font.draw_string(text, 20.0, 30.0, 1.0, &mut vec_buf);
    assert!(advance > 50.0);

    let mut bitmap = Bitmap::new(200, 60);
    bitmap.clear(0x00000000); // Black background

    SubpixelRasterizer::render(&mut bitmap, &vec_buf, &Transform2D::identity());

    // Count non-black pixels
    let lit_pixels = bitmap.pixels.iter().filter(|&&p| p != 0).count();
    assert!(
        lit_pixels > 100,
        "Rendered text should illuminate more than 100 pixels, got {}",
        lit_pixels
    );

    // Export to BMP
    let bmp_bytes = bitmap.to_bmp_bytes();
    assert_eq!(&bmp_bytes[0..2], b"BM");
    assert!(bmp_bytes.len() >= 54 + (200 * 3 * 60));
}

#[test]
fn test_far_out_vector_asset_cells_do_not_distort_toc() {
    let mut wb = Workbook::new("test_wb", "Test Workbook");
    let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main Sheet");

    // Normal active spreadsheet data in A1:C26
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 100.0).with_name("revenue")); // A1
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(1, 1), 0.20).with_name("tax_rate")); // B2
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(2, 25), "revenue * tax_rate").with_name("tax_amount")); // C26

    // Far-out vector asset stored in row 10,000 (col 0, row 10,000)
    let mut vec_buf = VectorBuffer::new();
    vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(0, 255, 128));
    vec_buf.rect(0.0, 0.0, 50.0, 50.0);
    let blob_bytes = vec_buf.to_bytes();

    let asset_cell = Cell::blob(Coord::new(0, 10_000), "hud_logo", blob_bytes.clone());
    assert!(asset_cell.is_asset(), "Cell at row 10,000 should automatically be flagged as an asset");
    sheet.set_cell(asset_cell);

    wb.sheets.push(sheet);

    // Compute Dynamic TOC
    let toc = wb.compute_toc();

    // Verify TOC position is at D27 (col 3, row 26), NOT row 10,001!
    assert_eq!(
        toc.extent_col, 2,
        "Active grid extent col should be 2 (col C)"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        toc.extent_row, 25,
        "Active grid extent row should be 25 (row 26)"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        toc.pos,
        Coord::new(3, 26),
        "TOC should be dynamically anchored at D27 (3, 26)"
    );

    // Verify all named items including the far-out asset appear in TOC entries
    let entry_names: Vec<&str> = toc.entries.iter().map(|e| e.name.as_str()).collect();
    assert!(entry_names.contains(&"revenue"));
    assert!(entry_names.contains(&"tax_rate"));
    assert!(entry_names.contains(&"tax_amount"));
    assert!(
        entry_names.contains(&"hud_logo"),
        "TOC should catalog the asset blob"
    );

    let hud_entry = toc.entries.iter().find(|e| e.name == "hud_logo").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(hud_entry.type_name, "blob");
    assert_eq!(hud_entry.byte_size, blob_bytes.len());
    assert_eq!(hud_entry.coord, Coord::new(0, 10_000));

    // Full roundtrip serialization check
    let serialized = serialize_workbook(&wb);
    let deserialized = deserialize_workbook(&serialized).expect("Workbook should roundtrip cleanly");
    let des_sheet = &deserialized.sheets[0];
    let loaded_asset = des_sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 10_000)).expect("Asset cell should be preserved");
    assert_eq!(loaded_asset.name.as_deref(), Some("hud_logo"));
    assert_eq!(loaded_asset.value, CellValue::Blob { bytes: blob_bytes });
}

#[test]
fn test_rasterizer_zoom_and_pan_transform() {
    let mut vec_buf = VectorBuffer::new();
    vec_buf.set_color(ColorRgba::rgb(255, 0, 0));
    vec_buf.set_width(1.0);
    vec_buf.move_to(10.0, 10.0);
    vec_buf.line_to(20.0, 10.0);

    let mut bmp_1x = Bitmap::new(100, 100);
    SubpixelRasterizer::render(&mut bmp_1x, &vec_buf, &Transform2D::identity());
    let lit_1x = bmp_1x.pixels.iter().filter(|&&p| p != 0).count();

    let mut bmp_2x = Bitmap::new(100, 100);
    SubpixelRasterizer::render(&mut bmp_2x, &vec_buf, &Transform2D::new(0.0, 0.0, 2.0));
    let lit_2x = bmp_2x.pixels.iter().filter(|&&p| p != 0).count();

    // At 2x zoom, the stroke length doubles on screen so more pixels should be illuminated
    assert!(
        lit_2x > lit_1x,
        "2x zoom should illuminate more pixels than 1x: {} vs {}",
        lit_2x,
        lit_1x
    );
}

#[test]
fn test_end_to_end_basic_turtle_to_bitmap_raster() {
    let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Main Sheet");
    let owner = Coord::new(0, 0);
    let asset_coord = Coord::parse_a1("A10000").unwrap();

    sheet.set_cell(Cell::blob(asset_coord, "chart_vec", vec![]));

    // BASIC script that uses Logo turtle to generate a data chart/shape and label it
    let script = r#"
        COLOR "green"
        WIDTH 2
        ' Draw a triangular marker
        FOR I = 1 TO 3
            FD 40
            RT 120
        NEXT I
        PU
        FD 20
        PD
        LABEL "123"
        COMMIT_TURTLE("A10000")
    "#;

    let writes = lotus_core::BasicRunner::execute(&mut sheet, owner, script, &[], &[asset_coord], None)
        .expect("BASIC script should execute");
    assert_eq!(writes, 1);

    // Retrieve committed blob
    let cell = sheet.get_cell(asset_coord).unwrap();
    let blob_bytes = match &cell.value {
        CellValue::Blob { bytes } => bytes,
        _ => panic!("Expected blob"),
    };

    let vector_buf = VectorBuffer::from_bytes(blob_bytes).expect("Should decode vector buffer");
    assert!(vector_buf.len() > 5);

    // Rasterize with SubpixelRasterizer into a bitmap buffer
    let mut bmp = Bitmap::new(160, 160);
    bmp.clear(0x00000000);
    // Pan to center the turtle drawing which starts around (0,0) and moves north
    SubpixelRasterizer::render(&mut bmp, &vector_buf, &Transform2D::new(-50.0, -80.0, 1.0));

    let illuminated = bmp.pixels.iter().filter(|&&p| p != 0).count();
    assert!(
        illuminated > 50,
        "Rendered turtle graphic should illuminate pixels, got {}",
        illuminated
    );

    // Verify BMP file export
    let bmp_bytes = bmp.to_bmp_bytes();
    assert_eq!(&bmp_bytes[0..2], b"BM");
}

#[test]
fn test_gui_scene_projection() {
    let font = HersheyFont::load_simplex();
    let mut scene = VectorBuffer::new();
    
    // Create a window
    let win = lotus_core::view::gui::GeoWorks::window(10.0, 10.0, 100.0, 50.0, "TEST", &font);
    scene.ops.extend(win.ops);

    let mut bitmap = Bitmap::new(200, 200);
    bitmap.clear(0);
    
    SubpixelRasterizer::render(&mut bitmap, &scene, &Transform2D::identity());
    
    let lit_pixels = bitmap.pixels.iter().filter(|&&p| p != 0).count();
    assert!(lit_pixels > 100, "GUI window should produce visible pixels");
}

#[test]
fn test_layout_persistence_roundtrip() {
    let mut wb = Workbook::new("layout_wb", "Layout Test");
    let mut sheet = Sheet::new("s1", "Sheet1");
    
    // 1. Test Sheet Defaults
    sheet.default_col_width = 123.4;
    sheet.default_row_height = 45.6;
    
    // 2. Test Custom Dimensions
    sheet.set_col_width(0, 200.0);
    sheet.set_row_height(10, 80.0);
    
    // 3. Test Cell Spans and Overlays
    let cell_a1 = Cell::text(Coord::new(0, 0), "Spanned").with_span(3, 2);
    let cell_b1 = Cell::text(Coord::new(1, 0), "Overlay").with_overlay("asset_ref");
    
    sheet.set_cell(cell_a1);
    sheet.set_cell(cell_b1);
    
    wb.sheets.push(sheet);
    
    // Roundtrip
    let serialized = serialize_workbook(&wb);
    let deserialized = deserialize_workbook(&serialized).expect("Should deserialize layout wb");
    let des_sheet = &deserialized.sheets[0];
    
    // Verify Sheet Defaults
    assert_eq!(des_sheet.default_col_width, 123.4);
    assert_eq!(des_sheet.default_row_height, 45.6);
    
    // Verify Custom Dimensions
    assert_eq!(des_sheet.col_width(0), 200.0);
    assert_eq!(des_sheet.row_height(10), 80.0);
    
    // Verify Cell Spans
    let loaded_a1 = des_sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(0, 0)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(loaded_a1.col_span, 3);
    assert_eq!(loaded_a1.row_span, 2);
    
    // Verify Overlay
    let loaded_b1 = des_sheet.get_cell(Coord::new(1, 0)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(loaded_b1.overlay.as_deref(), Some("asset_ref"));
}

#[test]
fn test_context_menu_with_nested_lotus_commands_and_workspace_view() {
    use lotus_core::{
        ContextMenu, ContextMenuAction, VectorWorkspaceView, WorkspaceSession,
    };

    let mut sheet = Sheet::new("main", "Model");
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 0), 5000.0).with_name("rev"));
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::number(Coord::new(0, 1), 0.25).with_name("rate"));
    sheet.set_cell(Cell::formula(Coord::new(0, 2), "rev * rate").with_name("tax"));

    let mut session = WorkspaceSession::new();
    session.cursor = Coord::new(0, 2);

    let mut context_menu = ContextMenu::default_context_menu();
    context_menu.open_at(80.0, 120.0);
    assert!(context_menu.is_visible);

    // Hover over "Lotus" (index 5)
    // y = 120 (start) + 4 (padding) + 4 * 22 (items 0..3) + 6 (sep) + 10 = 228.0
    context_menu.handle_mouse_move(100.0, 228.0);
    assert_eq!(context_menu.hovered_idx(), Some(5));
    assert_eq!(context_menu.active_submenu_idx(), Some(5));

    // Submenu is popped out: hover over "File" -> "Save" (/FS)
    let (sub_rect, sub_items) = context_menu.submenu_bounds().unwrap();
    assert!(sub_rect.min_x > 80.0);
    assert_eq!(sub_items.len(), 9);

    // Click on "Cut" in the main menu (item 0 at y = 120 + 4 + 10 = 134.0)
    context_menu.open_at(80.0, 120.0);
    let cut_action = context_menu.handle_mouse_click(100.0, 134.0);
    assert_eq!(cut_action, Some(ContextMenuAction::Cut));
    assert!(!context_menu.is_visible);

    // Render complete workspace view with context menu
    context_menu.open_at(150.0, 100.0);
    let view = VectorWorkspaceView::new();
    let font = HersheyFont::load_simplex();
    let mut bitmap = Bitmap::new(600, 400);
    let mut vec_buf = VectorBuffer::new();

    view.render(&sheet, &session, &context_menu, &mut bitmap, &mut vec_buf, &font);

    let lit_pixels = bitmap.pixels.iter().filter(|&&p| p != 0).count();
    assert!(lit_pixels > 2000, "Workspace with context menu should produce rich pixel output");

    let bmp_bytes = bitmap.to_bmp_bytes();
    assert_eq!(&bmp_bytes[0..2], b"BM");
}

[package]

FILE: lotus-gui/src/main.rs

//! Lotus 5-7-11 Sovereign Workspace — Win32 shell
//!
//! All application logic lives in `lotus_core::App`. This file is the whole of
//! what Windows contributes:
//!
//!   1. a window and a message loop,
//!   2. translation of Win32 messages into `lotus_core::Event`,
//!   3. blitting `App::render()` to the window (`SetDIBitsToDevice`),
//!   4. an implementation of `lotus_core::Host` (Win32 clipboard, `std::fs` files).
//!
//! A shell for another platform (X11, a framebuffer, BareMetal OS) supplies the
//! same four things and nothing else.

#![windows_subsystem = "windows"]

mod win32;

use core::ffi::c_void;
use std::ptr;
use win32::*;

use lotus_core::{App, Event, Host, Key, Mods, PointerButton};

const GWLP_USERDATA: i32 = -21;
const VK_SHIFT: i32 = 0x10;
const VK_CONTROL: i32 = 0x11;
const VK_MENU: i32 = 0x12; // Alt

// Keys and messages beyond what win32.rs declares.
const VK_TAB: usize = 0x09;
const VK_PRIOR: usize = 0x21; // Page Up
const VK_NEXT: usize = 0x22; // Page Down
const VK_END: usize = 0x23;
const VK_HOME: usize = 0x24;
const VK_F1: usize = 0x70;
const VK_F12: usize = 0x7B;
const WM_LBUTTONUP: u32 = 0x0202;
const WM_RBUTTONUP: u32 = 0x0205;
const WM_MOUSEWHEEL: u32 = 0x020A;
const WM_MOUSEHWHEEL: u32 = 0x020E;
const MK_LBUTTON: usize = 0x0001;
const MK_SHIFT: usize = 0x0004;
/// One wheel notch is 120 units; a notch scrolls three rows.
const WHEEL_DELTA: i32 = 120;
const ROWS_PER_NOTCH: i32 = 3;

// While the left button is held the window keeps receiving mouse moves, even
// outside it, so a drag selection can run past the window edge.
#[link(name = "user32")]
extern "system" {
    fn SetCapture(hwnd: HWND) -> HWND;
    fn ReleaseCapture() -> i32;
}

/// The OS services `App` asks for, answered by Windows.
struct Win32Host {
    hwnd: HWND,
}

impl Host for Win32Host {
    fn clipboard_get(&mut self) -> Option<String> {
        Win32Clipboard::get_text(self.hwnd)
    }

    fn clipboard_set(&mut self, text: &str) {
        Win32Clipboard::set_text(self.hwnd, text);
    }

    fn read_file(&mut self, path: &str) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
        std::fs::read(path).ok()
    }

    fn write_file(&mut self, path: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(), String> {
        std::fs::write(path, bytes).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
    }

    fn request_quit(&mut self) {
        unsafe { PostQuitMessage(0) };
    }
}

unsafe fn current_mods() -> Mods {
    Mods {
        ctrl: (GetKeyState(VK_CONTROL) as i16) < 0,
        shift: (GetKeyState(VK_SHIFT) as i16) < 0,
        alt: (GetKeyState(VK_MENU) as i16) < 0,
    }
}

/// Virtual-key code to `Key`.
fn translate_key(vk: usize, mods: Mods) -> Option<Key> {
    match vk {
        VK_UP => Some(Key::Up),
        VK_DOWN => Some(Key::Down),
        VK_LEFT => Some(Key::Left),
        VK_RIGHT => Some(Key::Right),
        VK_RETURN => Some(Key::Enter),
        VK_ESCAPE => Some(Key::Escape),
        VK_BACK => Some(Key::Backspace),
        VK_DELETE => Some(Key::Delete),
        VK_HOME => Some(Key::Home),
        VK_END => Some(Key::End),
        VK_PRIOR => Some(Key::PageUp),
        VK_NEXT => Some(Key::PageDown),
        VK_TAB => Some(Key::Tab),
        VK_F1..=VK_F12 => Some(Key::F((vk - VK_F1 + 1) as u8)),
        // Letters and digits only count as chords (Ctrl held); plain typing arrives via WM_CHAR.
        0x41..=0x5A if mods.ctrl => Some(Key::Char((vk as u8).to_ascii_lowercase() as char)),
        0x30..=0x39 if mods.ctrl => Some(Key::Char(vk as u8 as char)),
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn pointer_pos(lparam: isize) -> (f32, f32) {
    let x = (lparam & 0xFFFF) as i16 as f32;
    let y = ((lparam >> 16) & 0xFFFF) as i16 as f32;
    (x, y)
}

/// Wheel rotation carried in the high word of `wparam`, as signed units.
fn wheel_delta(wparam: usize) -> i32 {
    i32::from(((wparam >> 16) & 0xFFFF) as u16 as i16)
}

unsafe fn paint(hwnd: HWND, app: &mut App) {
    let mut ps: PAINTSTRUCT = core::mem::zeroed();
    let hdc = BeginPaint(hwnd, &mut ps);

    let frame = app.render();
    let width = frame.width as i32;
    let height = frame.height as i32;

    let mut bmi: BITMAPINFO = core::mem::zeroed();
    bmi.bmiHeader.biSize = core::mem::size_of::<BITMAPINFOHEADER>() as u32;
    bmi.bmiHeader.biWidth = width;
    bmi.bmiHeader.biHeight = -height; // top-down DIB
    bmi.bmiHeader.biPlanes = 1;
    bmi.bmiHeader.biBitCount = 32;
    bmi.bmiHeader.biCompression = BI_RGB;

    SetDIBitsToDevice(
        hdc,
        0,
        0,
        width as u32,
        height as u32,
        0,
        0,
        0,
        height as u32,
        frame.pixels.as_ptr() as *const c_void,
        &bmi,
        DIB_RGB_COLORS,
    );

    EndPaint(hwnd, &ps);
}

unsafe extern "system" fn window_proc(hwnd: HWND, msg: u32, wparam: usize, lparam: isize) -> isize {
    let app_ptr = GetWindowLongPtrW(hwnd, GWLP_USERDATA) as *mut App;
    if app_ptr.is_null() {
        return DefWindowProcW(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam);
    }
    let app = &mut *app_ptr;
    let mut host = Win32Host { hwnd };

    let event = match msg {
        WM_PAINT => {
            paint(hwnd, app);
            return 0;
        }
        WM_DESTROY => {
            PostQuitMessage(0);
            return 0;
        }
        WM_SIZE => Some(Event::Resize {
            width: (lparam & 0xFFFF) as usize,
            height: ((lparam >> 16) & 0xFFFF) as usize,
        }),
        WM_LBUTTONDOWN => {
            SetCapture(hwnd);
            let (x, y) = pointer_pos(lparam);
            Some(Event::PointerDown { x, y, button: PointerButton::Left })
        }
        WM_LBUTTONUP => {
            ReleaseCapture();
            let (x, y) = pointer_pos(lparam);
            Some(Event::PointerUp { x, y, button: PointerButton::Left })
        }
        WM_RBUTTONUP => {
            let (x, y) = pointer_pos(lparam);
            Some(Event::PointerUp { x, y, button: PointerButton::Right })
        }
        WM_RBUTTONDOWN => {
            let (x, y) = pointer_pos(lparam);
            Some(Event::PointerDown { x, y, button: PointerButton::Right })
        }
        WM_MOUSEMOVE => {
            let (x, y) = pointer_pos(lparam);
            Some(Event::PointerMove { x, y, left_down: wparam & MK_LBUTTON != 0 })
        }
        WM_MOUSEWHEEL => {
            // Wheel up is positive; scrolling up is fewer rows. Shift+wheel scrolls sideways.
            let amount = -(wheel_delta(wparam) * ROWS_PER_NOTCH) / WHEEL_DELTA;
            if wparam & MK_SHIFT != 0 {
                Some(Event::Scroll { cols: amount, rows: 0 })
            } else {
                Some(Event::Scroll { cols: 0, rows: amount })
            }
        }
        WM_MOUSEHWHEEL => {
            let amount = (wheel_delta(wparam) * ROWS_PER_NOTCH) / WHEEL_DELTA;
            Some(Event::Scroll { cols: amount, rows: 0 })
        }
        WM_KEYDOWN => {
            let mods = current_mods();
            translate_key(wparam, mods).map(|key| Event::Key { key, mods })
        }
        WM_CHAR => {
            // Ctrl+letter also produces a WM_CHAR control code; chords are handled via WM_KEYDOWN.
            let ch = char::from_u32(wparam as u32).unwrap_or('\0');
            if current_mods().ctrl { None } else { Some(Event::Text(ch)) }
        }
        _ => return DefWindowProcW(hwnd, msg, wparam, lparam),
    };

    if let Some(event) = event {
        app.handle_event(&mut host, event);
    }
    if app.take_dirty() {
        InvalidateRect(hwnd, ptr::null(), 0);
    }
    0
}

fn main() {
    let file_path = std::env::args().nth(1);

    let class_name = to_wide_chars("Lotus5711WindowClass");
    let window_title = to_wide_chars("Lotus 5-7-11 Sovereign Workspace");

    unsafe {
        let h_instance = GetModuleHandleW(ptr::null());

        let mut wc: WNDCLASSEXW = core::mem::zeroed();
        wc.cbSize = core::mem::size_of::<WNDCLASSEXW>() as u32;
        wc.style = CS_HREDRAW | CS_VREDRAW | CS_OWNDC;
        wc.lpfnWndProc = Some(window_proc);
        wc.hInstance = h_instance;
        wc.hCursor = LoadCursorW(ptr::null_mut(), 32512 as *const u16); // IDC_ARROW
        wc.lpszClassName = class_name.as_ptr();

        RegisterClassExW(&wc);

        let hwnd = CreateWindowExW(
            0,
            class_name.as_ptr(),
            window_title.as_ptr(),
            WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW | WS_VISIBLE,
            100,
            100,
            850,
            620,
            ptr::null_mut(),
            ptr::null_mut(),
            h_instance,
            ptr::null_mut(),
        );

        if hwnd.is_null() {
            eprintln!("Failed to create Win32 window.");
            return;
        }

        let mut client_rect: RECT = core::mem::zeroed();
        GetClientRect(hwnd, &mut client_rect);
        let client_w = (client_rect.right - client_rect.left) as usize;
        let client_h = (client_rect.bottom - client_rect.top) as usize;

        let mut host = Win32Host { hwnd };
        let app = Box::new(App::new(&mut host, client_w, client_h, file_path));
        SetWindowLongPtrW(hwnd, GWLP_USERDATA, Box::into_raw(app) as isize);

        ShowWindow(hwnd, 1);
        UpdateWindow(hwnd);

        let mut msg: MSG = core::mem::zeroed();
        while GetMessageW(&mut msg, ptr::null_mut(), 0, 0) > 0 {
            TranslateMessage(&msg);
            DispatchMessageW(&msg);
        }

        // Free the app state on exit.
        let app_raw = SetWindowLongPtrW(hwnd, GWLP_USERDATA, 0) as *mut App;
        if !app_raw.is_null() {
            drop(Box::from_raw(app_raw));
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: lotus-gui/src/win32.rs
================================================================================
//! Raw Zero-Dependency Win32 Window & GDI DIB Surface Harness for Lotus 5-7-11.
//!
//! Direct C-ABI FFI linking against standard Windows user32.dll and gdi32.dll.
//! Eliminates bloated third-party windowing crates (winit/softbuffer) while providing
//! native 60fps subpixel vector blitting, mouse events, and clipboard access.

#![allow(non_snake_case, dead_code)]

use core::ffi::c_void;
use std::ptr;

pub type HWND = *mut c_void;
pub type HDC = *mut c_void;
pub type HINSTANCE = *mut c_void;
pub type HICON = *mut c_void;
pub type HCURSOR = *mut c_void;
pub type HBRUSH = *mut c_void;
pub type HGLOBAL = *mut c_void;

pub const WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW: u32 = 0x00CF0000;
pub const WS_VISIBLE: u32 = 0x10000000;
pub const CS_HREDRAW: u32 = 0x0002;
pub const CS_VREDRAW: u32 = 0x0001;
pub const CS_OWNDC: u32 = 0x0020;

pub const WM_DESTROY: u32 = 0x0002;
pub const WM_SIZE: u32 = 0x0005;
pub const WM_PAINT: u32 = 0x000F;
pub const WM_CHAR: u32 = 0x0102;
pub const WM_KEYDOWN: u32 = 0x0100;
pub const WM_MOUSEMOVE: u32 = 0x0200;
pub const WM_LBUTTONDOWN: u32 = 0x0201;
pub const WM_RBUTTONDOWN: u32 = 0x0204;

pub const VK_BACK: usize = 0x08;
pub const VK_SHIFT: usize = 0x10;
pub const VK_CONTROL: usize = 0x11;
pub const VK_RETURN: usize = 0x0D;
pub const VK_ESCAPE: usize = 0x1B;
pub const VK_LEFT: usize = 0x25;
pub const VK_UP: usize = 0x26;
pub const VK_RIGHT: usize = 0x27;
pub const VK_DOWN: usize = 0x28;
pub const VK_DELETE: usize = 0x2E;

pub const DIB_RGB_COLORS: u32 = 0;
pub const SRCCOPY: u32 = 0x00CC0020;
pub const BI_RGB: u32 = 0;
pub const CF_UNICODETEXT: u32 = 13;
pub const GMEM_MOVEABLE: u32 = 0x0002;

#[repr(C)]
pub struct RECT {
    pub left: i32,
    pub top: i32,
    pub right: i32,
    pub bottom: i32,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct POINT {
    pub x: i32,
    pub y: i32,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct MSG {
    pub hwnd: HWND,
    pub message: u32,
    pub wParam: usize,
    pub lParam: isize,
    pub time: u32,
    pub pt: POINT,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct PAINTSTRUCT {
    pub hdc: HDC,
    pub fErase: i32,
    pub rcPaint: RECT,
    pub fRestore: i32,
    pub fIncUpdate: i32,
    pub rgbReserved: [u8; 32],
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct BITMAPINFOHEADER {
    pub biSize: u32,
    pub biWidth: i32,
    pub biHeight: i32,
    pub biPlanes: u16,
    pub biBitCount: u16,
    pub biCompression: u32,
    pub biSizeImage: u32,
    pub biXPelsPerMeter: i32,
    pub biYPelsPerMeter: i32,
    pub biClrUsed: u32,
    pub biClrImportant: u32,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct BITMAPINFO {
    pub bmiHeader: BITMAPINFOHEADER,
    pub bmiColors: [u32; 1],
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct WNDCLASSEXW {
    pub cbSize: u32,
    pub style: u32,
    pub lpfnWndProc: Option<unsafe extern "system" fn(HWND, u32, usize, isize) -> isize>,
    pub cbClsExtra: i32,
    pub cbWndExtra: i32,
    pub hInstance: HINSTANCE,
    pub hIcon: HICON,
    pub hCursor: HCURSOR,
    pub hbrBackground: HBRUSH,
    pub lpszMenuName: *const u16,
    pub lpszClassName: *const u16,
    pub hIconSm: HICON,
}

#[link(name = "user32")]
extern "system" {
    pub fn GetModuleHandleW(lpModuleName: *const u16) -> HINSTANCE;
    pub fn RegisterClassExW(lpWndClass: *const WNDCLASSEXW) -> u16;
    pub fn CreateWindowExW(
        dwExStyle: u32,
        lpClassName: *const u16,
        lpWindowName: *const u16,
        dwStyle: u32,
        X: i32,
        Y: i32,
        nWidth: i32,
        nHeight: i32,
        hWndParent: HWND,
        hMenu: *mut c_void,
        hInstance: HINSTANCE,
        lpParam: *mut c_void,
    ) -> HWND;
    pub fn ShowWindow(hWnd: HWND, nCmdShow: i32) -> i32;
    pub fn UpdateWindow(hWnd: HWND) -> i32;
    pub fn DefWindowProcW(hWnd: HWND, Msg: u32, wParam: usize, lParam: isize) -> isize;
    pub fn GetMessageW(lpMsg: *mut MSG, hWnd: HWND, wMsgFilterMin: u32, wMsgFilterMax: u32) -> i32;
    pub fn TranslateMessage(lpMsg: *const MSG) -> i32;
    pub fn DispatchMessageW(lpMsg: *const MSG) -> isize;
    pub fn PostQuitMessage(nExitCode: i32);
    pub fn GetClientRect(hWnd: HWND, lpRect: *mut RECT) -> i32;
    pub fn InvalidateRect(hWnd: HWND, lpRect: *const RECT, bErase: i32) -> i32;
    pub fn BeginPaint(hWnd: HWND, lpPaint: *mut PAINTSTRUCT) -> HDC;
    pub fn EndPaint(hWnd: HWND, lpPaint: *const PAINTSTRUCT) -> i32;
    pub fn SetWindowTextW(hWnd: HWND, lpString: *const u16) -> i32;
    pub fn LoadCursorW(hInstance: HINSTANCE, lpCursorName: *const u16) -> HCURSOR;
    pub fn GetKeyState(nVirtKey: i32) -> i16;
    pub fn SetWindowLongPtrW(hWnd: HWND, nIndex: i32, dwNewLong: isize) -> isize;
    pub fn GetWindowLongPtrW(hWnd: HWND, nIndex: i32) -> isize;

    // Clipboard
    pub fn OpenClipboard(hWndNewOwner: HWND) -> i32;
    pub fn CloseClipboard() -> i32;
    pub fn EmptyClipboard() -> i32;
    pub fn SetClipboardData(uFormat: u32, hMem: *mut c_void) -> *mut c_void;
    pub fn GetClipboardData(uFormat: u32) -> *mut c_void;
}

#[link(name = "gdi32")]
extern "system" {
    pub fn SetDIBitsToDevice(
        hdc: HDC,
        xDest: i32,
        yDest: i32,
        w: u32,
        h: u32,
        xSrc: i32,
        ySrc: i32,
        StartScan: u32,
        cLines: u32,
        lpvBits: *const c_void,
        lpbmi: *const BITMAPINFO,
        ColorUse: u32,
    ) -> i32;
}

#[link(name = "kernel32")]
extern "system" {
    pub fn GlobalAlloc(uFlags: u32, dwBytes: usize) -> HGLOBAL;
    pub fn GlobalLock(hMem: HGLOBAL) -> *mut c_void;
    pub fn GlobalUnlock(hMem: HGLOBAL) -> i32;
}

pub fn to_wide_chars(s: &str) -> Vec<u16> {
    s.encode_utf16().chain(core::iter::once(0)).collect()
}

/// Native Windows clipboard helper (zero crates).
pub struct Win32Clipboard;

impl Win32Clipboard {
    pub fn get_text(hwnd: HWND) -> Option<String> {
        unsafe {
            if OpenClipboard(hwnd) == 0 {
                return None;
            }
            let h_data = GetClipboardData(CF_UNICODETEXT);
            if h_data.is_null() {
                CloseClipboard();
                return None;
            }
            let ptr_u16 = GlobalLock(h_data) as *const u16;
            if ptr_u16.is_null() {
                CloseClipboard();
                return None;
            }

            let mut len = 0;
            while *ptr_u16.add(len) != 0 {
                len += 1;
            }
            let slice = core::slice::from_raw_parts(ptr_u16, len);
            let text = String::from_utf16_lossy(slice);

            GlobalUnlock(h_data);
            CloseClipboard();
            Some(text)
        }
    }

    pub fn set_text(hwnd: HWND, text: &str) -> bool {
        unsafe {
            if OpenClipboard(hwnd) == 0 {
                return false;
            }
            EmptyClipboard();

            let wide = to_wide_chars(text);
            let byte_count = wide.len() * 2;
            let h_mem = GlobalAlloc(GMEM_MOVEABLE, byte_count);
            if h_mem.is_null() {
                CloseClipboard();
                return false;
            }

            let dest = GlobalLock(h_mem) as *mut u16;
            if !dest.is_null() {
                ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(wide.as_ptr(), dest, wide.len());
                GlobalUnlock(h_mem);
                SetClipboardData(CF_UNICODETEXT, h_mem);
            }

            CloseClipboard();
            true
        }
    }
}
